  166 | /**
  167 |  * IA-Lite: intercepta continuaciones y reservas obvias antes de router/RAG/LLM.
  168 |  *
  169 |  * @return array{type:string,response?:string,message?:string,x_continue?:bool}|null
  170 |  */
  171 | function xabia_intercept_keywords(string $project_id, string $message, array $context = []): ?array {
  172 |     $msg = trim($message);
  173 |     if ($msg === '') {
  174 |         return null;
  175 |     }
  176 |     $norm = strtolower(remove_accents($msg));
  177 |     $norm = preg_replace('/[^\p{L}\p{N}\?]+/u', ' ', $norm);
  178 |     $norm = trim(preg_replace('/\s+/', ' ', (string) $norm));
  179 | 
  180 |     if (!session_id() && !headers_sent()) {
  181 |         session_start();
  182 |     }
  183 |     $meta = $_SESSION['xabia_last_response_meta'][$project_id] ?? [];
  184 |     $availability = $_SESSION['xabia_last_availability'][$project_id] ?? [];
  185 | 
  186 |     $is_continue = in_array($norm, ['sigue', 'continua', 'continue', 'y', 'y?', 'mas', 'dale'], true);
  187 |     if ($is_continue && !empty($meta['truncated'])) {
  188 |         return [
  189 |             'type'       => 'continue',
  190 |             'message'    => 'Continúa exactamente desde donde lo dejaste, sin repetir lo anterior.',
  191 |             'x_continue' => true,
  192 |         ];
  193 |     }
  194 | 
  195 |     $is_booking = preg_match('/\b(reservar|reserva|reservalo|resérvalo|quiero reservar|hacer reserva|book)\b/u', $msg) === 1;
  196 |     $booking_url = '';
  197 |     if (is_array($availability)) {
  198 |         $booking_url = trim((string) ($availability['booking_url'] ?? ''));
  199 |     }
  200 |     if ($booking_url === '' && is_array($meta)) {
  201 |         $booking_url = trim((string) ($meta['booking_url'] ?? ''));
  202 |     }
  203 |     if ($is_booking && $booking_url !== '') {
  204 |         $room = is_array($availability) ? trim((string) ($availability['requested_room'] ?? '')) : '';
  205 |         $prefix = $room !== ''
  206 |             ? 'Perfecto. Para reservar ' . $room . ', puedes hacerlo aquí:'
  207 |             : 'Perfecto. Puedes hacer la reserva aquí:';
  208 |         return [
  209 |             'type'     => 'response',
  210 |             'response' => $prefix . "\n\n[ACTION:URL:" . esc_url_raw($booking_url) . "]",
  211 |         ];
  212 |     }
  213 | 
  214 |     return null;
  215 | }

  244 | function xabia_agent_ensure_secure_uploads_dir() {
  245 |     if (!function_exists('wp_upload_dir')) {
  246 |         return;
  247 |     }
  248 |     $uploads = wp_upload_dir();
  249 |     $base = rtrim((string) ($uploads['basedir'] ?? ''), '/');
  250 |     if ($base === '') {
  251 |         return;
  252 |     }
  253 |     $xabia_uploads = $base . '/xabia';
  254 |     if (!is_dir($xabia_uploads) && !wp_mkdir_p($xabia_uploads)) {
  255 |         return;
  256 |     }
  257 |     $htaccess = $xabia_uploads . '/.htaccess';
  258 |     if (!file_exists($htaccess)) {
  259 |         $rules = "Deny from all\n<IfModule mod_authz_core.c>\nRequire all denied\n</IfModule>\n";
  260 |         @file_put_contents($htaccess, $rules, LOCK_EX);
  261 |     }
  262 |     $index = $xabia_uploads . '/index.php';
  263 |     if (!file_exists($index)) {
  264 |         @file_put_contents($index, "<?php\n// Silence is golden.\n", LOCK_EX);
  265 |     }
  266 | }

  322 | /**
  323 |  * Permalink de la página de aterrizaje Smart QR configurada para el agente (post publicado).
  324 |  */
  325 | function xabia_agent_smart_qr_landing_permalink(string $project_id): string {
  326 |     $project_id = sanitize_key($project_id);
  327 |     if ($project_id === '') {
  328 |         return '';
  329 |     }
  330 |     $projects = get_option('xabia_projects_config', []);
  331 |     if (!isset($projects[$project_id]) || !is_array($projects[$project_id])) {
  332 |         return '';
  333 |     }
  334 |     $page_id = isset($projects[$project_id]['smart_qr_landing_page_id']) ? absint($projects[$project_id]['smart_qr_landing_page_id']) : 0;
  335 |     if ($page_id < 1) {
  336 |         return '';
  337 |     }
  338 |     if (get_post_status($page_id) !== 'publish') {
  339 |         return '';
  340 |     }
  341 |     $url = get_permalink($page_id);
  342 |     return is_string($url) ? $url : '';
  343 | }

    1 | <?php
    2 | /**
    3 |  * Bootstrap del core: instalación automática del mu-plugin de aislamiento de tablas (sitio vs Hub).
    4 |  */
    5 | 
    6 | if (!defined('ABSPATH')) {
    7 |     exit;
    8 | }
    9 | 
   10 | final class Xabia_Agent_Core {
   11 | 
   12 |     public const MU_PLUGIN_FILE = 'xabia-hub-table-isolation.php';
   13 | 
   14 |     private const BUNDLED_RELATIVE = 'includes/mu-plugins/xabia-hub-table-isolation.php';
   15 | 
   16 |     public static function bundled_mu_source_path(): string {
   17 |         return trailingslashit(XABIA_PATH) . self::BUNDLED_RELATIVE;
   18 |     }
   19 | 
   20 |     public static function mu_plugin_destination_path(): string {
   21 |         if (defined('WPMU_PLUGIN_DIR') && is_string(WPMU_PLUGIN_DIR) && WPMU_PLUGIN_DIR !== '') {
   22 |             return trailingslashit(WPMU_PLUGIN_DIR) . self::MU_PLUGIN_FILE;
   23 |         }
   24 | 
   25 |         return trailingslashit(WP_CONTENT_DIR) . 'mu-plugins/' . self::MU_PLUGIN_FILE;
   26 |     }
   27 | 
   28 |     /**
   29 |      * Crea wp-content/mu-plugins si hace falta y copia (sobrescribe) el peacekeeper desde el bundle del core.
   30 |      */
   31 |     public static function install_mu_plugin_table_isolation(): bool {
   32 |         $src = self::bundled_mu_source_path();
   33 |         if (!is_readable($src)) {
   34 |             return false;
   35 |         }
   36 |         $dest = self::mu_plugin_destination_path();
   37 |         $dir = dirname($dest);
   38 |         if (!is_dir($dir) && !wp_mkdir_p($dir)) {
   39 |             return false;
   40 |         }
   41 | 
   42 |         // phpcs:ignore WordPress.WP.AlternativeFunctions.file_system_operations_copy -- única vía portable para despliegue MU.
   43 |         return @copy($src, $dest);
   44 |     }
   45 | 
   46 |     /**
   47 |      * Incluye el mu-plugin en esta petición si hace falta (p. ej. recién copiado antes de que WP lo cargara al inicio).
   48 |      */
   49 |     public static function load_mu_plugin_for_current_request(): void {
   50 |         $dest = self::mu_plugin_destination_path();
   51 |         if (!is_readable($dest)) {
   52 |             return;
   53 |         }
   54 |         require_once $dest;
   55 |     }
   56 | 
   57 |     /**
   58 |      * Sincroniza el archivo MU con el bundle del core cuando cambia la versión del plugin o el contenido del fuente.
   59 |      *
   60 |      * @return bool True si hubo copia nueva (conviene cargar el MU en la misma petición antes de dbDelta).
   61 |      */
   62 |     public static function maybe_sync_mu_plugin(): bool {
   63 |         $src = self::bundled_mu_source_path();
   64 |         if (!is_readable($src)) {
   65 |             return false;
   66 |         }
   67 |         $dest = self::mu_plugin_destination_path();
   68 |         $verOpt = (string) get_option('xabia_mu_table_isolation_pkg', '');
   69 |         $need = $verOpt !== XABIA_VERSION || !is_readable($dest);
   70 |         if (!$need && is_readable($dest)) {
   71 |             $sumSrc = @md5_file($src);
   72 |             $sumDst = @md5_file($dest);
   73 |             $need = is_string($sumSrc) && is_string($sumDst) && $sumSrc !== $sumDst;
   74 |         }
   75 |         if (!$need) {
   76 |             return false;
   77 |         }
   78 |         if (!self::install_mu_plugin_table_isolation()) {
   79 |             return false;
   80 |         }
   81 |         update_option('xabia_mu_table_isolation_pkg', XABIA_VERSION, false);
   82 | 
   83 |         return true;
   84 |     }
   85 | }

    1 | <?php
    2 | /**
    3 |  * XABIA BRAIN — búsqueda RAG (LIKE y vectorial) y formateo de contexto.
    4 |  * - LIKE: prioridad frase exacta y palabras clave (cuando no hay vectores o use_vector = false).
    5 |  * - Vector: embeddings + similitud coseno, umbral y top_k (cuando use_vector y hay vector_data).
    6 |  */
    7 | 
    8 | if (!defined('ABSPATH')) exit;
    9 | 
   10 | if (!class_exists('Xabia_Brain')) :
   11 | 
   12 | class Xabia_Brain {
   13 | 
   14 |     
   15 |     const DEFAULT_MAX_CHUNKS = 4;
   16 |     /** Coincide con el límite del Hub y el campo «Resultados máximos de contexto» en el admin. */
   17 |     const MAX_RAG_CHUNKS = 15;
   18 |     const EMBEDDING_MODEL = 'text-embedding-3-small';
   19 |     const VECTOR_CANDIDATES_LIMIT = 200;
   20 | 
   21 |     /**
   22 |      * Top-K efectivo para RAG según reglas del proyecto (legacy context_chunk_limit o max_chunks_context).
   23 |      */
   24 |     public static function effective_rag_max_chunks_from_project_config(array $config): int {
   25 |         $rules = isset($config['rules']) && is_array($config['rules']) ? $config['rules'] : [];
   26 |         if (isset($rules['max_chunks_context']) && $rules['max_chunks_context'] !== '' && $rules['max_chunks_context'] !== null) {
   27 |             return max(1, min(self::MAX_RAG_CHUNKS, (int) $rules['max_chunks_context']));
   28 |         }
   29 |         if (isset($rules['context_chunk_limit']) && $rules['context_chunk_limit'] !== '' && $rules['context_chunk_limit'] !== null) {
   30 |             return max(1, min(self::MAX_RAG_CHUNKS, (int) $rules['context_chunk_limit']));
   31 |         }
   32 | 
   33 |         return self::DEFAULT_MAX_CHUNKS;
   34 |     }
   35 | 
   36 |     /**
   37 |      * Términos para LIKE: frase completa, sin signos finales típicos y palabras sin puntuación adherida
   38 |      * (p. ej. "caballo?" → también "caballo"), UTF-8. Ampliable con `xabia_brain_search_like_terms`.
   39 |      *
   40 |      * @return list<string>
   41 |      */
   42 |     private static function normalize_search_like_terms(string $query): array {
   43 |         $query = trim((string) $query);
   44 |         if ($query === '') {
   45 |             return [];
   46 |         }
   47 |         $terms = [];
   48 |         $terms[] = $query;
   49 |         $stripped_end = preg_replace('/[\p{P}\p{Z}]+$/u', '', $query);
   50 |         if ($stripped_end !== '' && $stripped_end !== $query) {
   51 |             $terms[] = $stripped_end;
   52 |         }
   53 |         foreach (preg_split('/\s+/u', $query, -1, PREG_SPLIT_NO_EMPTY) as $raw) {
   54 |             $t = preg_replace('/^[\p{P}\p{Z}]+|[\p{P}\p{Z}]+$/u', '', $raw);
   55 |             if ($t !== '' && mb_strlen($t, 'UTF-8') > 2) {
   56 |                 $terms[] = $t;
   57 |             }
   58 |         }
   59 |         $terms = apply_filters('xabia_brain_search_like_terms', array_values(array_unique($terms)), $query);
   60 | 
   61 |         return is_array($terms) ? array_values(array_filter(array_map('strval', $terms))) : [];
   62 |     }
   63 | 
   64 |     /**
   65 |      * Columna de metadatos en knowledge_vectors si existe (Hub: meta_json; local: meta_data).
   66 |      */
   67 |     private static function knowledge_meta_column_for_search(): ?string {
   68 |         if (!class_exists('Xabia_DB', false)) {
   69 |             return null;
   70 |         }
   71 |         $cm = Xabia_DB::knowledge_vectors_column_map();
   72 |         if (isset($cm['meta_json'])) {
   73 |             return 'meta_json';
   74 |         }
   75 |         if (isset($cm['meta_data'])) {
   76 |             return 'meta_data';
   77 |         }
   78 | 
   79 |         return null;
   80 |     }
   81 | 
   82 |     /**
   83 |      * Búsqueda por LIKE (keywords). Usado cuando no hay vector search o como fallback.
   84 |      */
   85 |     public static function search_knowledge($project_id, $query, $scope = 'global', $strict_ente = false, $max_chunks = null) {
   86 |         global $wpdb;
   87 |         $table = Xabia_DB::table('knowledge_vectors');
   88 |         
   89 |         $sql = "SELECT content_chunk FROM $table WHERE project_id = %s";
   90 |         $args = [$project_id];
   91 | 
   92 |         if ($scope !== 'global' && !empty($scope)) {
   93 |             $sql .= " AND ente_id = %s";
   94 |             $args[] = $scope;
   95 |         }
   96 | 
   97 |         $query = trim((string)$query);
   98 |         if (!($strict_ente && $scope !== 'global' && !empty($scope))) {
   99 |             $terms = self::normalize_search_like_terms($query);
  100 |             $meta_col = self::knowledge_meta_column_for_search();
  101 |             $like_parts = [];
  102 |             foreach ($terms as $term) {
  103 |                 if ($term === '') {
  104 |                     continue;
  105 |                 }
  106 |                 $pat = '%' . $wpdb->esc_like($term) . '%';
  107 |                 if ($meta_col !== null) {
  108 |                     $col_sql = ($meta_col === 'meta_json') ? 'meta_json' : 'meta_data';
  109 |                 $like_parts[] = "(content_chunk LIKE %s OR `{$col_sql}` LIKE %s)";
  110 |                     $args[] = $pat;
  111 |                     $args[] = $pat;
  112 |                 } else {
  113 |                     $like_parts[] = 'content_chunk LIKE %s';
  114 |                     $args[] = $pat;
  115 |                 }
  116 |             }
  117 |             if (!empty($like_parts)) {
  118 |                 $sql .= ' AND (' . implode(' OR ', $like_parts) . ')';
  119 |             }
  120 |             $order_hint = $terms[0] ?? $query;
  121 |             $sql .= ' ORDER BY (CASE WHEN content_chunk LIKE %s THEN 1 ELSE 2 END) ASC, id DESC';
  122 |             $args[] = '%' . $wpdb->esc_like($order_hint) . '%';
  123 |         } else {
  124 |             $sql .= " ORDER BY id DESC";
  125 |         }
  126 | 
  127 |         $limit = $max_chunks !== null ? max(1, min(self::MAX_RAG_CHUNKS, (int) $max_chunks)) : self::DEFAULT_MAX_CHUNKS;
  128 |         $sql .= " LIMIT " . (int) $limit;
  129 | 
  130 |         $results = $wpdb->get_results($wpdb->prepare($sql, $args));
  131 |         return self::format_context_from_rows($results);
  132 |     }
  133 | 
  134 |     /**
  135 |      * Búsqueda vectorial: usa el vector de la query (o lo genera con OpenAI si no se pasa).
  136 |      * Recupera chunks con vector_data, filtra por umbral y top_k. Doble salto: lista vs desarrollo.
  137 |      *
  138 |      * @param float $threshold Umbral de similitud coseno (0.0–1.0). Por debajo se descarta.
  139 |      * @param array|null $query_vector Vector precalculado (p. ej. Vertex/OpenAI según ai_driver). Si null, se usa get_embedding (OpenAI).
  140 |      * @return array{context: string, chunk_count: int, similarity_avg: float|null, total_found?: int|null}
  141 |      */
  142 |     public static function search_knowledge_vector($project_id, $query, $scope = 'global', $strict_ente = false, $max_chunks = null, $threshold = 0.2, $query_vector = null) {
  143 |         global $wpdb;
  144 |         $table = Xabia_DB::table('knowledge_vectors');
  145 | 
  146 |         if ($query_vector === null || !is_array($query_vector)) {
  147 |             $query_vector = self::get_embedding($query, $project_id);
  148 |         }
  149 |         if ($query_vector === null || !is_array($query_vector)) {
  150 |             return [
  151 |                 'context'        => '',
  152 |                 'chunk_count'    => 0,
  153 |                 'similarity_avg' => null,
  154 |                 'total_found'    => null,
  155 |             ];
  156 |         }
  157 | 
  158 |         if (class_exists('Xabia_Hub_Knowledge', false) && Xabia_Hub_Knowledge::is_hub_rag_enabled($project_id)) {
  159 |             $threshold = max(0, min(1, (float) $threshold));
  160 |             $max_k = $max_chunks !== null ? max(1, min(self::MAX_RAG_CHUNKS, (int) $max_chunks)) : self::DEFAULT_MAX_CHUNKS;
  161 |             $hubRes = Xabia_Hub_Knowledge::search_vector($project_id, $query_vector, $scope, $strict_ente, $max_k, $threshold, $query);
  162 |             if (($hubRes['chunk_count'] ?? 0) > 0) {
  163 |                 return $hubRes;
  164 |             }
  165 |             if (!apply_filters('xabia_hub_knowledge_fallback_local', true, $project_id)) {
  166 |                 return [
  167 |                     'context'        => '',
  168 |                     'chunk_count'    => 0,
  169 |                     'similarity_avg' => null,
  170 |                     'total_found'    => null,
  171 |                 ];
  172 |             }
  173 |         }
  174 | 
  175 |         $vec_col = class_exists('Xabia_DB', false) ? Xabia_DB::knowledge_vectors_vector_column() : 'vector_data';
  176 |         $has_emb = class_exists('Xabia_DB', false) ? Xabia_DB::knowledge_vectors_sql_has_embedding() : 'vector_data IS NOT NULL';
  177 | 
  178 |         $sql = "SELECT id, content_chunk, {$vec_col} AS vector_data FROM $table WHERE project_id = %s AND ({$has_emb})";
  179 |         $args = [$project_id];
  180 |         if ($scope !== 'global' && !empty($scope)) {
  181 |             $sql .= " AND ente_id = %s";
  182 |             $args[] = $scope;
  183 |         }
  184 |         $sql .= " ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT " . (int) self::VECTOR_CANDIDATES_LIMIT;
  185 | 
  186 |         $rows = $wpdb->get_results($wpdb->prepare($sql, $args));
  187 |         if (empty($rows)) {
  188 |             return ['context' => '', 'chunk_count' => 0, 'similarity_avg' => null, 'total_found' => null];
  189 |         }
  190 | 
  191 |         $threshold = max(0, min(1, (float) $threshold));
  192 |         $max_chunks = $max_chunks !== null ? max(1, min(self::MAX_RAG_CHUNKS, (int) $max_chunks)) : self::DEFAULT_MAX_CHUNKS;
  193 | 
  194 |         $scored = [];
  195 |         foreach ($rows as $r) {
  196 |             $vec = json_decode($r->vector_data, true);
  197 |             if (!is_array($vec)) continue;
  198 |             $sim = self::cosine_similarity($query_vector, $vec);
  199 |             if ($sim >= $threshold) {
  200 |                 $scored[] = ['chunk' => $r->content_chunk, 'score' => $sim];
  201 |             }
  202 |         }
  203 |         usort($scored, function ($a, $b) { return $b['score'] <=> $a['score']; });
  204 |         $total_qualifying = count($scored);
  205 |         $sliced = array_slice($scored, 0, $max_chunks);
  206 | 
  207 |         $context = self::format_context_from_rows(array_map(function ($s) { return (object) ['content_chunk' => $s['chunk']]; }, $sliced));
  208 |         $avg = empty($sliced) ? null : array_sum(array_column($sliced, 'score')) / count($sliced);
  209 | 
  210 |         $out = [
  211 |             'context'        => $context,
  212 |             'chunk_count'    => count($sliced),
  213 |             'similarity_avg' => $avg,
  214 |             'total_found'    => null,
  215 |         ];
  216 |         if ($total_qualifying > count($sliced)) {
  217 |             $out['total_found'] = $total_qualifying;
  218 |         }
  219 | 
  220 |         return $out;
  221 |     }
  222 | 
  223 |     /**
  224 |      * Genera embedding del texto con OpenAI (mismo modelo que el entrenamiento) o vía proxy Xabia si aplica.
  225 |      *
  226 |      * @param string $project_id ID del agente (para herencia de claves y licencia).
  227 |      */
  228 |     public static function get_embedding($text, $project_id = '') {
  229 |         $text = trim(strip_tags((string) $text));
  230 |         if ($text === '') {
  231 |             return null;
  232 |         }
  233 |         $projects = get_option('xabia_projects_config', []);
  234 |         $config = ($project_id !== '' && isset($projects[$project_id]) && is_array($projects[$project_id]))
  235 |             ? $projects[$project_id]
  236 |             : [];
  237 | 
  238 |         if (class_exists('Xabia_Digixop_Client') && Xabia_Digixop_Client::should_use_openai_proxy($project_id, $config)) {
  239 |             return Xabia_Digixop_Client::embedding_via_proxy($text, self::EMBEDDING_MODEL, $project_id, $config);
  240 |         }
  241 | 
  242 |         $key = class_exists('Xabia_Digixop_Client')
  243 |             ? Xabia_Digixop_Client::get_effective_openai_key($project_id, $config)
  244 |             : trim((string) get_option('xabia_openai_key', ''));
  245 |         if ($key === '') {
  246 |             return null;
  247 |         }
  248 | 
  249 |         $resp = wp_remote_post('https://api.openai.com/v1/embeddings', [
  250 |             'headers' => ['Authorization' => 'Bearer ' . $key, 'Content-Type' => 'application/json'],
  251 |             'body'    => json_encode(['input' => $text, 'model' => self::EMBEDDING_MODEL]),
  252 |             'timeout' => 15,
  253 |         ]);
  254 |         if (is_wp_error($resp)) {
  255 |             return null;
  256 |         }
  257 |         $body = json_decode(wp_remote_retrieve_body($resp), true);
  258 |         return isset($body['data'][0]['embedding']) ? $body['data'][0]['embedding'] : null;
  259 |     }
  260 | 
  261 |     private static function cosine_similarity(array $a, array $b) {
  262 |         if (count($a) !== count($b) || count($a) === 0) return 0;
  263 |         $dot = 0;
  264 |         $norm_a = 0;
  265 |         $norm_b = 0;
  266 |         foreach ($a as $i => $v) {
  267 |             $w = isset($b[$i]) ? (float) $b[$i] : 0;
  268 |             $v = (float) $v;
  269 |             $dot += $v * $w;
  270 |             $norm_a += $v * $v;
  271 |             $norm_b += $w * $w;
  272 |         }
  273 |         $den = sqrt($norm_a) * sqrt($norm_b);
  274 |         return $den > 0 ? $dot / $den : 0;
  275 |     }
  276 | 
  277 |     /**
  278 |      * Ensambla chunks recuperados para el LLM: sin viñetas ni pipes; bloques separados por línea en blanco
  279 |      * (más denso frente al límite ~20k del prompt).
  280 |      */
  281 |     private static function format_context_from_rows($results) {
  282 |         $chunks = [];
  283 |         $seen = [];
  284 |         if ($results) {
  285 |             foreach ($results as $r) {
  286 |                 $chunk = trim(strip_tags($r->content_chunk ?? ''));
  287 |                 if ($chunk === '' || isset($seen[$chunk])) {
  288 |                     continue;
  289 |                 }
  290 |                 $seen[$chunk] = true;
  291 |                 
  292 |                 if (strlen($chunk) > 900) {
  293 |                     $chunk = substr($chunk, 0, 900) . '…';
  294 |                 }
  295 |                 $chunks[] = $chunk;
  296 |             }
  297 |         }
  298 |         return implode("\n\n", $chunks);
  299 |     }
  300 | }
  301 | 
  302 | endif;

   13 |     public static function classify(string $project_id, string $message, array $config = [], string $lang = 'es'): string {
   14 |         $msg = trim($message);
   15 |         if ($msg === '') {
   16 |             return self::ROUTE_GENERAL;

   19 |         $route = apply_filters('xabia_router_classify_route', $heuristic, $project_id, $msg, $config, $lang);
   20 |         if (!in_array($route, [self::ROUTE_ACTION, self::ROUTE_KNOWLEDGE, self::ROUTE_GENERAL], true)) {
   21 |             $route = $heuristic;
   22 |         }
   23 |         return $route;
   24 |     }

   26 |     private static function heuristic_classify(string $msg): string {

   90 |         if (is_array($body) && isset($body['error']) && is_array($body['error'])) {

   93 |             $errCode = $body['error']['code'] ?? null;
   94 |         }
   95 |         if ($msg === '' && isset($out['raw']) && is_string($out['raw'])) {
   96 |             $msg = $out['raw'];
   97 |         }
   98 |         self::$last_proxy_failure = [
   99 |             'http_code' => (int) ($out['code'] ?? 0),
  100 |             'message'   => substr($msg, 0, 600),
  101 |             'type'      => $type,
  102 |             'code'      => $errCode,

  188 |     public static function should_use_openai_proxy(string $project_id, array $config): bool {
  189 |         if (!self::is_license_configured()) {

  199 |             return false;

  249 |     /**
  250 |      * POST JSON firmado (X-Xabia-*) hacia rutas del hub que no son el proxy OpenAI.
  251 |      *
  252 |      * @param array<string, mixed> $body
  253 |      * @return array{ok: bool, code: int, body: ?array, raw: string, insufficient_balance: bool}
  254 |      */
  255 |     public static function hub_signed_json_post(string $url, array $body, string $project_id = ''): array {
  256 |         $license = self::get_license_key();
  257 |         $json = wp_json_encode($body);
  258 |         if (!is_string($json)) {
  259 |             $json = '{}';
  260 |         }
  261 |         $cred = [
  262 |             'site_url'   => self::get_client_source_url(),
  263 |             'project_id' => $project_id,
  264 |         ];
  265 |         $headers = apply_filters('xabia_hub_signed_post_headers', self::default_proxy_headers($license, $cred, $json), $url, $body, $cred);
  266 |         if (!is_array($headers)) {
  267 |             $headers = self::default_proxy_headers($license, $cred, $json);
  268 |         }
  269 |         $args = apply_filters(
  270 |             'xabia_hub_signed_post_args',
  271 |             [
  272 |                 'headers' => $headers,
  273 |                 'body'    => $json,
  274 |                 'timeout' => 120,
  275 |             ],
  276 |             $url,
  277 |             $body
  278 |         );
  279 |         $resp = wp_remote_post($url, is_array($args) ? $args : []);
  280 |         $code = is_wp_error($resp) ? 0 : (int) wp_remote_retrieve_response_code($resp);
  281 |         $raw = is_wp_error($resp) ? $resp->get_error_message() : (string) wp_remote_retrieve_body($resp);
  282 |         $parsed = null;
  283 |         if (!is_wp_error($resp)) {
  284 |             $parsed = json_decode($raw, true);
  285 |             if (is_array($parsed)) {
  286 |                 self::merge_license_meta_from_api_payload($parsed);
  287 |             } else {
  288 |                 $parsed = null;
  289 |             }
  290 |         }
  291 |         $out = [
  292 |             'ok'                   => !is_wp_error($resp) && $code >= 200 && $code < 300,
  293 |             'code'                 => $code,
  294 |             'body'                 => $parsed,
  295 |             'raw'                  => $raw,
  296 |             'insufficient_balance' => false,
  297 |         ];
  298 |         $out['insufficient_balance'] = self::is_insufficient_proxy_response([
  299 |             'code' => $code,
  300 |             'body' => is_array($parsed) && isset($parsed['error']) ? $parsed : (is_array($parsed) ? $parsed : null),
  301 |         ]);
  302 |         if ($out['insufficient_balance']) {
  303 |             self::mark_insufficient_balance();
  304 |         }
  305 | 
  306 |         return $out;
  307 |     }

  309 |     /**
  310 |      * Cabeceras obligatorias hacia el proxy: licencia + origen del sitio.
  311 |      *
  312 |      * @param array<string, mixed> $cred Contexto (site_url, project_id) para filtros.
  313 |      * @return array<string, string>
  314 |      */
  315 |     private static function default_proxy_headers(string $license, array $cred, string $body = ''): array {
  316 |         $headers = [
  317 |             'Content-Type'     => 'application/json',
  318 |             'Accept'           => 'application/json',
  319 |             'X-Xabia-License'  => $license,
  320 |             'X-Xabia-Source'   => self::get_client_source_url(),
  321 |         ];
  322 |         if ($license !== '') {
  323 |             $timestamp = (string) time();
  324 |             $payload = $license . "\n" . self::get_client_source_url() . "\n" . $timestamp . "\n" . $body;
  325 |             $headers['X-Xabia-Timestamp'] = $timestamp;
  326 |             $headers['X-Xabia-Signature'] = 'sha256=' . hash_hmac('sha256', $payload, $license);
  327 |         }
  328 | 
  329 |         return $headers;
  330 |     }

  342 |             'X-Xabia-License'  => $license,

  352 |     public static function default_hub_headers_for_license(string $license_key): array {
  353 |         $license_key = trim($license_key);
  354 |

  357 |             'Accept'           => 'application/json',

  363 |     /**
  364 |      * POST /license/validate: envía en cuerpo y cabecera exactamente $license_key (tras trim), sin normalizar.
  365 |      * No actualiza el transiente global del Core.
  366 |      *
  367 |      * @return array<string, mixed>|null JSON decodificado o null si falla la red o el parseo.
  368 |      */
  369 |     public static function license_validate_json_for_key(string $license_key): ?array {
  370 |         $license_key = trim($license_key);
  371 |         if ($license_key === '') {
  372 |             return null;
  373 |         }
  374 | 
  375 |         $siteUrl = self::get_client_source_url();
  376 |         $host = wp_parse_url($siteUrl, PHP_URL_HOST);
  377 |         $domain = is_string($host) && $host !== '' ? strtolower($host) : '';
  378 |         $baseUrl = (string) apply_filters('xabia_digixop_license_validate_url', self::default_license_validate_url());
  379 |         $urls = self::build_license_validate_url_candidates($baseUrl);
  380 |         $body = apply_filters(
  381 |             'xabia_digixop_license_validate_body',
  382 |             [
  383 |                 'license_key' => $license_key,
  384 |                 'site_url'    => $siteUrl,
  385 |                 'domain'      => $domain,
  386 |             ],
  387 |             $baseUrl
  388 |         );
  389 |         $headers = apply_filters(
  390 |             'xabia_digixop_license_validate_headers',
  391 |             self::default_hub_headers_for_license($license_key),
  392 |             $baseUrl,
  393 |             $body
  394 |         );
  395 |         if (!is_array($headers)) {
  396 |             $headers = self::default_hub_headers_for_license($license_key);
  397 |         }
  398 |         $headers['Content-Type'] = 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded; charset=utf-8';
  399 | 
  400 |         $json = null;
  401 |         foreach ($urls as $url) {
  402 |             $args = apply_filters(
  403 |                 'xabia_digixop_license_validate_http_args',
  404 |                 [
  405 |                     'headers' => $headers,
  406 |                     'body'    => is_array($body) ? $body : [],
  407 |                     'timeout' => 12,
  408 |                 ],
  409 |                 $url,
  410 |                 $body
  411 |             );
  412 |             $resp = wp_remote_post($url, is_array($args) ? $args : []);
  413 |             if (is_wp_error($resp)) {
  414 |                 continue;
  415 |             }
  416 |             $raw = (string) wp_remote_retrieve_body($resp);
  417 |             $decoded = json_decode($raw, true);
  418 |             if (is_array($decoded)) {
  419 |                 $json = $decoded;
  420 |                 break;
  421 |             }
  422 |         }
  423 | 
  424 |         return is_array($json) ? $json : null;
  425 |     }
  426 | 
  427 |     /**
  428 |      * HTTP 402 o cuerpo JSON de error de saldo (proxy espejo OpenAI + extensiones Xabia).
  429 |      *
  430 |      * @param array{code?: int, body?: array|null} $out Resultado de proxy_openai_post.
  431 |      */
  432 |     public static function is_insufficient_proxy_response(array $out): bool {
  433 |         if ((int) ($out['code'] ?? 0) === 402) {
  434 |             return true;
  435 |         }
  436 |         $b = $out['body'] ?? null;
  437 |         if (!is_array($b)) {
  438 |             return false;
  439 |         }
  440 |         if (!empty($b['digixop_insufficient_balance']) || !empty($b['insufficient_balance'])) {
  441 |             return true;
  442 |         }
  443 |         $err = $b['error'] ?? null;
  444 |         if (!is_array($err)) {
  445 |             return false;
  446 |         }
  447 |         $code = $err['code'] ?? '';
  448 |         if ($code === 'insufficient_balance' || $code === 'insufficient_quota' || $code === 'billing_hard_limit_reached') {
  449 |             return true;
  450 |         }
  451 |         $msg = strtolower((string) ($err['message'] ?? ''));
  452 |         if ($msg !== '' && (strpos($msg, 'saldo insuficiente') !== false || strpos($msg, 'insufficient balance') !== false)) {
  453 |             return true;
  454 |         }
  455 |         return false;
  456 |     }
  457 | 
  458 |     /**
  459 |      * Fusiona tokens_remaining y expiry_date del hub en el transiente de saldo (Ajustes generales).
  460 |      *
  461 |      * @param array<string, mixed>|null $json Respuesta JSON del hub o del proxy.
  462 |      */
  463 |     public static function merge_license_meta_from_api_payload(?array $json): void {
  464 |         if ($json === null) {
  465 |             return;
  466 |         }
  467 |         $tokens = $json['tokens_remaining'] ?? $json['remaining_tokens'] ?? $json['balance_tokens'] ?? $json['tokens'] ?? null;
  468 |         if ($tokens !== null && !is_numeric($tokens)) {
  469 |             $tokens = null;
  470 |         }
  471 |         $expiry = $json['expiry_date'] ?? $json['expires_at'] ?? $json['license_expires'] ?? null;
  472 |         if ($expiry !== null && !is_string($expiry)) {
  473 |             $expiry = (string) $expiry;
  474 |         }
  475 |         if ($tokens === null && ($expiry === null || $expiry === '')) {
  476 |             return;
  477 |         }
  478 | 
  479 |         $prev = get_transient(self::TRANSIENT_META);
  480 |         if (!is_array($prev)) {
  481 |             $prev = [];
  482 |         }
  483 |         if ($tokens !== null) {
  484 |             $prev['tokens_remaining'] = (int) $tokens;
  485 |         }
  486 |         if ($expiry !== null && $expiry !== '') {
  487 |             $prev['expiry_date'] = $expiry;
  488 |         }
  489 |         $prev['checked_at'] = time();
  490 |         set_transient(self::TRANSIENT_META, $prev, HOUR_IN_SECONDS * 12);
  491 |         if ($tokens !== null && class_exists('Xabia_DB', false)) {
  492 |             Xabia_DB::sync_wallet_balance((int) $tokens);
  493 |         }
  494 |     }

  496 |     /**
  497 |      * Traduce códigos `reason` del hub a texto legible en el escritorio (evita mensajes técnicos XABIA_DB_DSN a clientes).
  498 |      *
  499 |      * @param array<string, mixed> $json
  500 |      */
  501 |     private static function userFacingLicenseValidateMessage(array $json, string $defaultMsg): string {
  502 |         $reason = isset($json['reason']) && is_string($json['reason']) ? trim($json['reason']) : '';
  503 |         switch ($reason) {
  504 |             case 'license_key_unknown':
  505 |                 return __('El hub no reconoce esta clave. Copia y pega exactamente la licencia que te muestra Polar (misma cadena, sin espacios de más). Si acabas de comprar, espera unos segundos a que se registre el pago o escribe a soporte indicando el email de la compra.', 'xabia-intelligence');
  506 |             case 'unauthorized_domain':
  507 |             case 'domain_mismatch': {
  508 |                 $reg = '';
  509 |                 if (isset($json['registered_domain']) && is_string($json['registered_domain'])) {
  510 |                     $reg = trim($json['registered_domain']);
  511 |                 }
  512 |                 if ($reg === '') {
  513 |                     $domains = $json['registered_domains'] ?? null;
  514 |                     if (is_array($domains) && $domains !== []) {
  515 |                         $clean = [];
  516 |                         foreach ($domains as $d) {
  517 |                             if (is_string($d) || is_numeric($d)) {
  518 |                                 $t = trim((string) $d);
  519 |                                 if ($t !== '') {
  520 |                                     $clean[] = $t;
  521 |                                 }
  522 |                             }
  523 |                         }
  524 |                         $clean = array_values(array_unique($clean));
  525 |                         if ($clean !== []) {
  526 |                             $reg = $clean[0];
  527 |                         }
  528 |                     }
  529 |                 }
  530 | 
  531 |                 return sprintf(
  532 |                     /* translators: %s: registered domain for the license */
  533 |                     __('Esta licencia está vinculada a otro dominio (%s). Contacta con soporte para mover tu licencia.', 'xabia-intelligence'),
  534 |                     $reg !== '' ? $reg : __('desconocido', 'xabia-intelligence')
  535 |                 );
  536 |             }
  537 |             case 'license_not_active':
  538 |                 return __('La licencia consta en el hub pero está inactiva, suspendida o caducada.', 'xabia-intelligence');
  539 |             case 'wallet_missing':
  540 |                 return __('La licencia y el dominio encajan, pero falta la cartera de tokens en el hub. Contacta con soporte técnico.', 'xabia-intelligence');
  541 |             default:
  542 |                 return $defaultMsg;
  543 |         }
  544 |     }
  545 | 
  546 |     /**
  547 |      * POST JSON al proxy con el cuerpo exacto de OpenAI (chat o embeddings). Respuesta: formato estándar OpenAI (choices, usage, data…).
  548 |      *
  549 |      * @param array<string, mixed> $openai_body
  550 |      * @return array{ok: bool, body: array|null, raw: string, code: int, insufficient_balance: bool}
  551 |      */
  552 |     public static function proxy_openai_post(array $openai_body, string $project_id, array $config = []): array {
  553 |         $license = self::get_license_key();
  554 |         $url = (string) apply_filters('xabia_digixop_proxy_url', self::default_proxy_url(), $openai_body, $project_id, $config);
  555 |         $cred = [
  556 |             'site_url'   => self::get_client_source_url(),
  557 |             'project_id' => $project_id,
  558 |         ];
  559 |         $body = wp_json_encode($openai_body);
  560 |         if (!is_string($body)) {
  561 |             $body = '{}';
  562 |         }
  563 |         $headers = apply_filters('xabia_digixop_proxy_headers', self::default_proxy_headers($license, $cred, $body), $openai_body, $project_id, $config, $cred);
  564 |         if (!is_array($headers)) {
  565 |             $headers = self::default_proxy_headers($license, $cred, $body);
  566 |         }
  567 |         $args = [
  568 |             'headers' => $headers,
  569 |             'body'    => $body,
  570 |             'timeout' => 60,
  571 |         ];
  572 |         $args = apply_filters('xabia_digixop_proxy_http_args', $args, $openai_body, $project_id, $config);
  573 |         $resp = wp_remote_post($url, is_array($args) ? $args : []);
  574 |         $code = is_wp_error($resp) ? 0 : (int) wp_remote_retrieve_response_code($resp);
  575 |         $raw = is_wp_error($resp) ? $resp->get_error_message() : (string) wp_remote_retrieve_body($resp);
  576 |         $json = null;
  577 |         if (!is_wp_error($resp)) {
  578 |             $json = json_decode($raw, true);
  579 |             if (!is_array($json)) {
  580 |                 $json = null;
  581 |             } else {
  582 |                 self::merge_license_meta_from_api_payload($json);
  583 |             }
  584 |         }
  585 |         $out = [
  586 |             'ok'                   => !is_wp_error($resp) && $code >= 200 && $code < 300,
  587 |             'body'                 => $json,
  588 |             'raw'                  => $raw,
  589 |             'code'                 => $code,
  590 |             'insufficient_balance' => false,
  591 |         ];
  592 |         $out['insufficient_balance'] = self::is_insufficient_proxy_response($out);
  593 |         if ($out['insufficient_balance']) {
  594 |             self::mark_insufficient_balance();
  595 |         }
  596 |         if (!$out['ok'] || $out['insufficient_balance']) {
  597 |             self::record_proxy_failure($out);
  598 |         }
  599 |         return $out;
  600 |     }
  601 | 
  602 |     /**
  603 |      * @return array{content: string, usage: array<string, int>|null}|null
  604 |      */
  605 |     public static function parse_chat_completion_response(?array $json): ?array {
  606 |         if ($json === null) {
  607 |             return null;
  608 |         }
  609 |         $content = $json['choices'][0]['message']['content'] ?? null;
  610 |         if (!is_string($content)) {
  611 |             return null;
  612 |         }
  613 |         $usage = isset($json['usage']) && is_array($json['usage']) ? $json['usage'] : null;
  614 |         return ['content' => $content, 'usage' => $usage];
  615 |     }
  616 | 
  617 |     /**
  618 |      * @return array{embedding: array<int, float>|null, usage: array<string, int>|null}
  619 |      */
  620 |     public static function parse_embedding_response(?array $json): array {
  621 |         $emb = null;
  622 |         if (isset($json['data'][0]['embedding']) && is_array($json['data'][0]['embedding'])) {
  623 |             $emb = $json['data'][0]['embedding'];
  624 |         }
  625 |         $usage = isset($json['usage']) && is_array($json['usage']) ? $json['usage'] : null;
  626 |         return ['embedding' => $emb, 'usage' => $usage];
  627 |     }
  628 | 
  629 |     public static function get_last_embedding_total_tokens(): ?int {
  630 |         return self::$last_embedding_total_tokens;
  631 |     }
  632 | 
  633 |     public static function reset_last_embedding_total_tokens(): void {
  634 |         self::$last_embedding_total_tokens = null;
  635 |     }

  637 |     /**
  638 |      * Embeddings vía proxy o null.
  639 |      *
  640 |      * @return array<int, float>|null
  641 |      */
  642 |     public static function embedding_via_proxy(string $text, string $model, string $project_id, array $config = []): ?array {
  643 |         self::$last_embedding_total_tokens = null;
  644 |         $body = [
  645 |             'model' => $model,
  646 |             'input' => $text,
  647 |         ];
  648 |         $out = self::proxy_openai_post($body, $project_id, $config);
  649 |         if (!empty($out['insufficient_balance'])) {
  650 |             return null;
  651 |         }
  652 |         if (!$out['ok']) {
  653 |             return null;
  654 |         }
  655 |         $parsed = self::parse_embedding_response($out['body']);
  656 |         if ($parsed['embedding'] === null) {
  657 |             self::record_proxy_failure([
  658 |                 'ok'   => false,
  659 |                 'code' => $out['code'],
  660 |                 'body' => $out['body'],
  661 |                 'raw'  => $out['raw'],
  662 |             ]);
  663 |             if (is_array(self::$last_proxy_failure) && self::$last_proxy_failure['message'] === '') {
  664 |                 self::$last_proxy_failure['message'] = __('El hub respondió sin vectores válidos (revisa embeddings Vertex en el servidor central).', 'xabia-intelligence');
  665 |             }
  666 |             return null;
  667 |         }
  668 |         if (!empty($parsed['usage']['total_tokens'])) {
  669 |             self::$last_embedding_total_tokens = (int) $parsed['usage']['total_tokens'];
  670 |         }
  671 |         return $parsed['embedding'];
  672 |     }
  673 | 
  674 |     /**
  675 |      * Valida la licencia guardada en opciones contra el hub (misma cadena en POST/cabecera que en el campo).
  676 |      * Guarda saldo en transiente.
  677 |      *
  678 |      * @return array{valid: bool, tokens_remaining: int|null, expiry_date: string|null, message: string, raw?: string}
  679 |      */
  680 |     public static function validate_license_remote(): array {
  681 |         if (!self::is_license_configured()) {
  682 |             return ['valid' => false, 'tokens_remaining' => null, 'expiry_date' => null, 'message' => __('No hay licencia configurada.', 'xabia-intelligence')];
  683 |         }
  684 |         $siteUrl = self::get_client_source_url();
  685 |         $host = wp_parse_url($siteUrl, PHP_URL_HOST);
  686 |         $domain = is_string($host) && $host !== '' ? strtolower($host) : '';
  687 |         $baseUrl = (string) apply_filters('xabia_digixop_license_validate_url', self::default_license_validate_url());
  688 |         $urls = self::build_license_validate_url_candidates($baseUrl);
  689 |         $body = apply_filters(
  690 |             'xabia_digixop_license_validate_body',
  691 |             [
  692 |                 'license_key' => self::get_license_key(),
  693 |                 'site_url'    => $siteUrl,
  694 |                 'domain'      => $domain,
  695 |             ],
  696 |             $baseUrl
  697 |         );
  698 |         $headers = apply_filters('xabia_digixop_license_validate_headers', self::default_hub_headers(), $baseUrl, $body);
  699 |         if (!is_array($headers)) {
  700 |             $headers = self::default_hub_headers();
  701 |         }
  702 |         
  703 |         $headers['Content-Type'] = 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded; charset=utf-8';
  704 | 
  705 |         $lastRaw = '';
  706 |         $lastErr = '';
  707 |         $json = null;
  708 |         foreach ($urls as $url) {
  709 |             $args = apply_filters(
  710 |                 'xabia_digixop_license_validate_http_args',
  711 |                 [
  712 |                     'headers' => $headers,
  713 |                     'body'    => is_array($body) ? $body : [],
  714 |                     'timeout' => 15,
  715 |                 ],
  716 |                 $url,
  717 |                 $body
  718 |             );
  719 |             $resp = wp_remote_post($url, is_array($args) ? $args : []);
  720 |             if (is_wp_error($resp)) {
  721 |                 $lastErr = $resp->get_error_message();
  722 |                 continue;
  723 |             }
  724 |             $lastRaw = (string) wp_remote_retrieve_body($resp);
  725 |             $decoded = json_decode($lastRaw, true);
  726 |             if (is_array($decoded)) {
  727 |                 $json = $decoded;
  728 |                 break;
  729 |             }
  730 |         }
  731 | 
  732 |         if (!is_array($json)) {
  733 |             $fallbackMsg = $lastErr !== '' ? $lastErr : trim(wp_strip_all_tags((string) $lastRaw));
  734 |             if ($fallbackMsg === '') {
  735 |                 $fallbackMsg = __('Respuesta no válida del servidor de licencias.', 'xabia-intelligence');
  736 |             }
  737 |             return ['valid' => false, 'tokens_remaining' => null, 'expiry_date' => null, 'message' => $fallbackMsg, 'raw' => $lastRaw];
  738 |         }
  739 |         self::merge_license_meta_from_api_payload($json);
  740 | 
  741 |         $valid = !empty($json['valid']) || !empty($json['success']) || !empty($json['active']);
  742 |         $tokens = $json['tokens_remaining'] ?? $json['remaining_tokens'] ?? $json['balance_tokens'] ?? $json['tokens'] ?? null;
  743 |         if ($tokens !== null && !is_numeric($tokens)) {
  744 |             $tokens = null;
  745 |         }
  746 |         if ($tokens !== null) {
  747 |             $tokens = (int) $tokens;
  748 |         }
  749 |         $expiry = $json['expiry_date'] ?? $json['expires_at'] ?? null;
  750 |         if ($expiry !== null && !is_string($expiry)) {
  751 |             $expiry = (string) $expiry;
  752 |         }
  753 |         $hubMsg = isset($json['message']) && is_string($json['message']) ? trim($json['message']) : '';
  754 |         $defaultMsg = $hubMsg !== '' ? $hubMsg : ($valid ? __('Licencia activa.', 'xabia-intelligence') : __('Licencia no reconocida.', 'xabia-intelligence'));
  755 |         $msg = (string) apply_filters(
  756 |             'xabia_digixop_license_validate_user_message',
  757 |             self::userFacingLicenseValidateMessage($json, $defaultMsg),
  758 |             $json,
  759 |             $valid
  760 |         );
  761 | 
  762 |         $cached = get_transient(self::TRANSIENT_META);
  763 |         if (!is_array($cached)) {
  764 |             $cached = [];
  765 |         }
  766 |         $cached['valid'] = $valid;
  767 |         $cached['tokens_remaining'] = $tokens;
  768 |         $cached['message'] = $msg;
  769 |         $cached['checked_at'] = time();
  770 |         if ($expiry !== null && $expiry !== '') {
  771 |             $cached['expiry_date'] = $expiry;
  772 |         }
  773 |         $cached['validate_reason'] = isset($json['reason']) && is_string($json['reason']) ? $json['reason'] : '';
  774 |         $cached['license_validate_response'] = $json;
  775 |         set_transient(self::TRANSIENT_META, $cached, HOUR_IN_SECONDS * 12);
  776 | 
  777 |         return ['valid' => $valid, 'tokens_remaining' => $tokens, 'expiry_date' => $expiry, 'message' => $msg, 'raw' => (string) wp_json_encode($json)];
  778 |     }
  779 | 
  780 |     /**
  781 |      * @return list<string>
  782 |      */
  783 |     private static function build_license_validate_url_candidates(string $baseUrl): array {
  784 |         $u = trim($baseUrl);
  785 |         $candidates = [];
  786 |         if ($u !== '') {
  787 |             $candidates[] = $u;
  788 |         }
  789 |         $pairs = [
  790 |             ['/api/xabia/v1/license/validate', '/api/index.php/xabia/v1/license/validate'],
  791 |             ['/api/xabia/v1/license/validate', '/api/public/index.php/xabia/v1/license/validate'],
  792 |             ['/api/index.php/xabia/v1/license/validate', '/api/xabia/v1/license/validate'],
  793 |             ['/api-xabia-saas/xabia/v1/license/validate', '/api-xabia-saas/public/index.php/xabia/v1/license/validate'],
  794 |             ['/api-xabia-saas/public/index.php/xabia/v1/license/validate', '/api-xabia-saas/xabia/v1/license/validate'],
  795 |         ];
  796 |         foreach ($pairs as [$a, $b]) {
  797 |             if ($u !== '' && strpos($u, $a) !== false) {
  798 |                 $candidates[] = str_replace($a, $b, $u);
  799 |             }
  800 |         }
  801 | 
  802 |         $candidates[] = 'https://xabia.ai/api/xabia/v1/license/validate';
  803 |         $candidates[] = 'https://xabia.ai/api/index.php/xabia/v1/license/validate';
  804 |         $candidates[] = 'https://xabia.ai/api/public/index.php/xabia/v1/license/validate';
  805 |         $candidates[] = 'https://xabia.ai/api-xabia-saas/public/index.php/xabia/v1/license/validate';
  806 |         $candidates[] = 'https://xabia.ai/api-xabia-saas/xabia/v1/license/validate';
  807 | 
  808 |         return array_values(array_unique(array_filter($candidates, static fn ($v) => is_string($v) && $v !== '')));
  809 |     }
  810 | 
  811 |     /**
  812 |      * Si hay licencia pero el caché no tiene saldo del hub, valida una vez para rellenar tokens_remaining (xabia_wallets en el hub).
  813 |      */
  814 |     public static function refresh_license_meta_from_hub_if_stale(): void {
  815 |         if (!self::is_license_configured()) {
  816 |             return;
  817 |         }
  818 |         $m = self::get_cached_license_meta();
  819 |         if (is_array($m) && array_key_exists('tokens_remaining', $m) && $m['tokens_remaining'] !== null && $m['tokens_remaining'] !== '') {
  820 |             return;
  821 |         }
  822 |         self::validate_license_remote();
  823 |     }
  824 | 
  825 |     /**
  826 |      * @return array<string, mixed>|null
  827 |      */
  828 |     public static function get_cached_license_meta(): ?array {
  829 |         $m = get_transient(self::TRANSIENT_META);
  830 |         return is_array($m) ? $m : null;
  831 |     }
  832 | 
  833 |     /**
  834 |      * Informa consumo de tokens al hub; si la respuesta incluye saldo, actualiza el transiente.
  835 |      *
  836 |      * @param array<string, mixed> $payload
  837 |      */
  838 |     public static function report_usage(array $payload): void {
  839 |         if (!self::is_license_configured()) {
  840 |             return;
  841 |         }
  842 |         $pt = (int) ($payload['prompt_tokens'] ?? 0);
  843 |         $ct = (int) ($payload['completion_tokens'] ?? 0);
  844 |         $total = (int) ($payload['total_tokens'] ?? 0);
  845 |         if ($total < 1) {
  846 |             $total = $pt + $ct;
  847 |         }
  848 |         if ($total < 1 && empty($payload['force'])) {
  849 |             return;
  850 |         }
  851 |         $url = (string) apply_filters('xabia_digixop_usage_report_url', self::default_usage_report_url(), $payload);
  852 |         $base_report = [
  853 |             'license_key'        => self::get_license_key(),
  854 |             'site_url'           => self::get_client_source_url(),
  855 |             'plugin_version'     => defined('XABIA_VERSION') ? (string) XABIA_VERSION : '',
  856 |             'prompt_tokens'      => $pt,
  857 |             'completion_tokens'  => $ct,
  858 |             'total_tokens'       => $total,
  859 |             'context'            => sanitize_key((string) ($payload['context'] ?? 'chat')),
  860 |             'project_id'         => sanitize_text_field((string) ($payload['project_id'] ?? '')),
  861 |         ];
  862 |         $body = apply_filters('xabia_digixop_usage_report_body', array_merge($payload, $base_report), $url);
  863 |         $headers = apply_filters('xabia_digixop_usage_report_headers', self::default_hub_headers(), $url, $body);
  864 |         if (!is_array($headers)) {
  865 |             $headers = self::default_hub_headers();
  866 |         }
  867 |         $args = apply_filters(
  868 |             'xabia_digixop_usage_http_args',
  869 |             [
  870 |                 'headers'  => $headers,
  871 |                 'body'     => wp_json_encode($body),
  872 |                 'timeout'  => 8,
  873 |                 'blocking' => true,
  874 |             ],
  875 |             $url,
  876 |             $body
  877 |         );
  878 |         $resp = wp_remote_post($url, is_array($args) ? $args : []);
  879 |         if (!is_wp_error($resp)) {
  880 |             $raw = (string) wp_remote_retrieve_body($resp);
  881 |             $json = json_decode($raw, true);
  882 |             if (is_array($json)) {
  883 |                 self::merge_license_meta_from_api_payload($json);
  884 |             }
  885 |         }
  886 |         do_action('xabia_digixop_usage_reported', $body, $url);
  887 |     }
  888 | }

   29 |         if (!is_array($raw)) {
   30 |             return [];
   31 |         }
   32 |         $out = [];
   33 |         foreach ($raw as $row) {

   35 |                 continue;

   38 |             $url = isset($row['url']) ? esc_url_raw((string) $row['url']) : '';

   40 |                 continue;

   52 |                 'slug'       => $slug,
   53 |             ];

   55 |

   57 |     }

   92 |     public static function register_rest_routes(): void {

   99 |                 'permission_callback' => '__return_true',
  100 |                 'args'                => [

  102 |                         'required'          => true,
  103 |                         'type'              => 'string',
  104 |                         'sanitize_callback' => static function ($v) {
  105 |                             return sanitize_text_field((string) $v);
  106 |                         },
  107 |                     ],

  109 |                         'required'          => true,
  110 |                         'type'              => 'string',
  111 |                         'sanitize_callback' => static function ($v) {
  112 |                             return sanitize_key((string) $v);
  113 |                         },
  114 |                     ],
  115 |                 ],
  116 |             ]
  117 |         );
  118 |     }
  119 | 
  120 |     /**
  121 |      * @param WP_REST_Request $request
  122 |      * @return WP_REST_Response|WP_Error
  123 |      */
  124 |     public static function rest_federate($request) {
  125 |         $provided = '';
  126 |         if (!empty($_SERVER['HTTP_X_XABIA_FED_KEY'])) {
  127 |             $provided = sanitize_text_field(wp_unslash((string) $_SERVER['HTTP_X_XABIA_FED_KEY']));
  128 |         }
  129 |         $secret = self::get_bridge_secret();
  130 |         if ($secret === '' || !hash_equals($secret, $provided)) {
  131 |             return new WP_Error('xabia_fed_forbidden', __('Clave de federación no válida.', 'xabia-intelligence'), ['status' => 403]);
  132 |         }
  133 | 
  134 |         $q = (string) $request->get_param('q');
  135 |         $project_id = (string) $request->get_param('project');
  136 |         if ($q === '' || $project_id === '') {
  137 |             return new WP_Error('xabia_fed_bad_request', __('Parámetros q y project son obligatorios.', 'xabia-intelligence'), ['status' => 400]);
  138 |         }
  139 | 
  140 |         if (class_exists('Xabia_API')) {
  141 |             Xabia_API::digixop_reset_session_for_federation();
  142 |         }
  143 | 
  144 |         $projects = get_option('xabia_projects_config', []);
  145 |         $config = is_array($projects) && isset($projects[$project_id]) ? $projects[$project_id] : null;
  146 |         if (!is_array($config)) {
  147 |             return new WP_Error('xabia_fed_unknown_project', __('Proyecto no encontrado.', 'xabia-intelligence'), ['status' => 404]);
  148 |         }
  149 | 
  150 |         $bundle = self::run_semantic_search_bundle($project_id, $q, $config);
  151 |         if (class_exists('Xabia_Digixop_Client') && Xabia_Digixop_Client::was_insufficient_balance()) {
  152 |             return new WP_Error(
  153 |                 'digixop_insufficient',
  154 |                 Xabia_Digixop_Client::get_insufficient_balance_user_message(),
  155 |                 ['status' => 402]
  156 |             );
  157 |         }
  158 |         $raw_context = $bundle['context'];
  159 | 
  160 |         if (!class_exists('Xabia_API')) {
  161 |             return new WP_REST_Response(
  162 |                 [
  163 |                     'summary' => __('Motor de IA no disponible.', 'xabia-intelligence'),
  164 |                     'links'   => [],
  165 |                     'query'   => $q,
  166 |                     'project' => $project_id,
  167 |                 ],
  168 |                 200
  169 |             );
  170 |         }
  171 | 
  172 |         $sum = Xabia_API::federation_summarize_context($q, $raw_context, $project_id, $config);
  173 |         if (class_exists('Xabia_API')) {
  174 |             Xabia_API::digixop_report_federation_session($project_id);
  175 |         }
  176 | 
  177 |         return new WP_REST_Response(
  178 |             [
  179 |                 'summary' => $sum['summary'],
  180 |                 'links'   => $sum['links'],
  181 |                 'query'   => $q,
  182 |                 'project' => $project_id,
  183 |             ],
  184 |             200
  185 |         );
  186 |     }
  187 | 
  188 |     /**
  189 |      * @return array{context:string,chunk_count:int,had_rows:bool}
  190 |      */
  191 |     public static function run_semantic_search_bundle(string $project_id, string $search_term, array $config): array {
  192 |         if (!class_exists('Xabia_Brain')) {
  193 |             $p = defined('XABIA_PATH') ? XABIA_PATH : dirname(__DIR__) . '/';
  194 |             $bp = $p . 'core/class-xabia-brain.php';
  195 |             if (file_exists($bp)) {
  196 |                 require_once $bp;
  197 |             }
  198 |         }
  199 |         $use_vector = !empty($config['rules']['use_vector_search']);
  200 |         $max_chunks = class_exists('Xabia_Brain', false) ? Xabia_Brain::effective_rag_max_chunks_from_project_config($config) : 4;
  201 |         $similarity_threshold = isset($config['rules']['similarity_threshold']) ? max(0, min(1, (float) $config['rules']['similarity_threshold'])) : 0.2;
  202 |         $ente_scope = 'global';
  203 |         $strict_ente = false;
  204 | 
  205 |         $context = '';
  206 |         $chunk_count = 0;
  207 |         $had_rows = false;
  208 | 
  209 |         if (class_exists('Xabia_Brain')) {
  210 |             if ($use_vector) {
  211 |                 $query_vector = null;
  212 |                 if (class_exists('Xabia_API')) {
  213 |                     $query_vector = Xabia_API::get_query_embedding($search_term, $config, $project_id);
  214 |                     Xabia_API::digixop_absorb_embedding_for_federation($project_id, $config);
  215 |                     if (class_exists('Xabia_Digixop_Client') && Xabia_Digixop_Client::was_insufficient_balance()) {
  216 |                         return [
  217 |                             'context'     => '',
  218 |                             'chunk_count' => 0,
  219 |                             'had_rows'    => false,
  220 |                         ];
  221 |                     }
  222 |                 }
  223 |                 $out = Xabia_Brain::search_knowledge_vector($project_id, $search_term, $ente_scope, false, $max_chunks, $similarity_threshold, $query_vector);
  224 |                 $context = $out['context'] ?? '';
  225 |                 $chunk_count = (int) ($out['chunk_count'] ?? 0);
  226 |                 $had_rows = ($chunk_count > 0);
  227 |                 if (strlen(trim((string) $context)) < 10) {
  228 |                     $context = Xabia_Brain::search_knowledge($project_id, $search_term, $ente_scope, $strict_ente, $max_chunks);
  229 |                     $chunk_count = 0;
  230 |                     $had_rows = strlen(trim($context)) >= 10;
  231 |                 } elseif (class_exists('Xabia_API', false) && Xabia_API::rag_context_misses_query_signal_terms($search_term, $context)) {
  232 |                     $lex = Xabia_Brain::search_knowledge($project_id, $search_term, $ente_scope, $strict_ente, $max_chunks);
  233 |                     if (strlen(trim($lex)) >= 10) {
  234 |                         $context .= "\n\n### Búsqueda por palabras clave (refuerzo) ###\n" . $lex;
  235 |                         $had_rows = true;
  236 |                     }
  237 |                 }
  238 |             } else {
  239 |                 $context = Xabia_Brain::search_knowledge($project_id, $search_term, $ente_scope, $strict_ente, $max_chunks);
  240 |                 $had_rows = strlen(trim($context)) >= 10;
  241 |             }
  242 |         }
  243 | 
  244 |         $discovery = apply_filters('xabia_chat_addon_discovery_blocks', [], $project_id, $config);
  245 |         if (is_array($discovery) && $discovery !== []) {
  246 |             $disc_text = trim(implode("\n\n", array_filter(array_map('strval', $discovery))));
  247 |             if ($disc_text !== '') {
  248 |                 $context .= "\n\n### ADDON DISCOVERY ###\n" . $disc_text;
  249 |             }
  250 |         }
  251 | 
  252 |         if (strlen($context) > 20000) {
  253 |             $context = substr($context, 0, 20000) . '...';
  254 |         }
  255 | 
  256 |         return [
  257 |             'context'     => $context,
  258 |             'chunk_count' => $chunk_count,
  259 |             'had_rows'    => $had_rows,
  260 |         ];
  261 |     }
  262 | 
  263 |     public static function extract_urls_from_text(string $text): array {
  264 |         $links = [];
  265 |         if ($text === '') {
  266 |             return $links;
  267 |         }
  268 |         if (!preg_match_all('#https?://[^\s\]\)>"\'<>]+#iu', $text, $m)) {
  269 |             return $links;
  270 |         }
  271 |         foreach ($m[0] as $u) {
  272 |             $u = esc_url_raw(rtrim($u, '.,;:)'));
  273 |             if ($u !== '' && strlen($u) < 2000) {
  274 |                 $links[$u] = ['title' => $u, 'url' => $u];
  275 |             }
  276 |         }
  277 | 
  278 |         return array_values($links);
  279 |     }

  281 |     /**
  282 |      * Consulta un nodo amigo (GET federate en el remoto).
  283 |      */
  284 |     public static function ask_federated_node(string $node_slug, string $query, string $caller_project_id = ''): string {
  285 |         $node = self::get_node_by_slug($node_slug);
  286 |         if ($node === null) {
  287 |             return __('Nodo federado no encontrado o slug inválido.', 'xabia-intelligence');
  288 |         }
  289 |         $remote_project = (string) ($node['project_id'] ?? '');
  290 |         if ($remote_project === '') {
  291 |             return __('El nodo no tiene project_id remoto configurado.', 'xabia-intelligence');
  292 |         }
  293 |         $base = rtrim((string) $node['url'], '/');
  294 |         $url = $base . '/wp-json/xabia/v1/federate?' . http_build_query(
  295 |             [
  296 |                 'q'       => $query,
  297 |                 'project' => $remote_project,
  298 |             ],
  299 |             '',
  300 |             '&',
  301 |             PHP_QUERY_RFC3986
  302 |         );
  303 | 
  304 |         $headers = [
  305 |             'Accept' => 'application/json',
  306 |         ];
  307 |         $api_key = (string) ($node['api_key'] ?? '');
  308 |         if ($api_key !== '') {
  309 |             $headers['X-Xabia-Fed-Key'] = $api_key;
  310 |         }
  311 | 
  312 |         $resp = wp_remote_get(
  313 |             $url,
  314 |             [
  315 |                 'headers' => $headers,
  316 |                 'timeout' => 25,
  317 |             ]
  318 |         );
  319 |         if (is_wp_error($resp)) {
  320 |             return sprintf(
  321 |                 
  322 |                 __('Error al contactar el nodo: %s', 'xabia-intelligence'),
  323 |                 $resp->get_error_message()
  324 |             );
  325 |         }
  326 |         $code = (int) wp_remote_retrieve_response_code($resp);
  327 |         $body = (string) wp_remote_retrieve_body($resp);
  328 |         if ($code === 403) {
  329 |             return __('El nodo rechazó la clave de federación (403).', 'xabia-intelligence');
  330 |         }
  331 |         if ($code < 200 || $code >= 300) {
  332 |             return sprintf(
  333 |                 
  334 |                 __('Respuesta HTTP no válida del nodo (%d).', 'xabia-intelligence'),
  335 |                 $code
  336 |             );
  337 |         }
  338 |         $json = json_decode($body, true);
  339 |         if (!is_array($json)) {
  340 |             return __('El nodo devolvió un cuerpo no JSON.', 'xabia-intelligence');
  341 |         }
  342 |         $summary = isset($json['summary']) ? (string) $json['summary'] : '';
  343 |         $links = isset($json['links']) && is_array($json['links']) ? $json['links'] : [];
  344 |         $lines = [];
  345 |         $lines[] = '--- ' . __('Contexto del nodo federado', 'xabia-intelligence') . ' "' . ($node['name'] ?? $node_slug) . '" ---';
  346 |         if ($summary !== '') {
  347 |             $lines[] = $summary;
  348 |         }
  349 |         if ($links !== []) {
  350 |             $lines[] = __('Enlaces de interés (remoto):', 'xabia-intelligence');
  351 |             foreach ($links as $L) {
  352 |                 if (!is_array($L)) {
  353 |                     continue;
  354 |                 }
  355 |                 $t = isset($L['title']) ? (string) $L['title'] : '';
  356 |                 $u = isset($L['url']) ? (string) $L['url'] : '';
  357 |                 if ($u !== '') {
  358 |                     $lines[] = '- ' . ($t !== '' ? $t . ': ' : '') . $u;
  359 |                 }
  360 |             }
  361 |         }
  362 |         if (count($lines) < 2) {
  363 |             return __('El nodo no devolvió resumen útil.', 'xabia-intelligence');
  364 |         }
  365 | 
  366 |         return implode("\n", $lines);
  367 |     }

  378 |             'xabia-intelligence'
