  132 |     /**
  133 |      * @param list<string> $needles Normalizados a minúsculas
  134 |      */
  135 |     public static function hub_payload_includes_addon(array $json, array $needles): ?array {
  136 |         if ($needles === []) {
  137 |             return null;
  138 |         }
  139 |         $rows = [];
  140 |         if (!empty($json['addon_activations']) && is_array($json['addon_activations'])) {
  141 |             foreach ($json['addon_activations'] as $r) {
  142 |                 if (is_array($r) && isset($r['addon_slug'])) {
  143 |                     $rows[] = $r;
  144 |                 }
  145 |             }
  146 |         }
  147 |         if ($rows === [] && !empty($json['active_addons']) && is_array($json['active_addons'])) {
  148 |             foreach ($json['active_addons'] as $s) {
  149 |                 if (is_string($s) && $s !== '') {
  150 |                     $rows[] = ['addon_slug' => $s, 'expiry_date' => null];
  151 |                 }
  152 |             }
  153 |         }
  154 |         foreach ($rows as $r) {
  155 |             $s = (string) ($r['addon_slug'] ?? '');
  156 |             foreach ($needles as $needle) {
  157 |                 if (self::addon_slug_matches_hub($s, $needle)) {
  158 |                     return $r;
  159 |                 }
  160 |             }
  161 |             $pid = isset($r['product_id']) ? (string) $r['product_id'] : '';
  162 |             if ($pid !== '') {
  163 |                 foreach ($needles as $needle) {
  164 |                     if (self::addon_slug_matches_hub($pid, $needle)) {
  165 |                         return $r;
  166 |                     }
  167 |                 }
  168 |             }
  169 |         }
  170 | 
  171 |         return null;
  172 |     }
  173 | 
  174 |     private static function normalize_hub_addon_token(string $s): string {
  175 |         $s = strtolower(trim($s));
  176 |         $s = str_replace('_', '-', preg_replace('/\s+/u', '-', $s));
  177 | 
  178 |         return $s;
  179 |     }
  180 | 
  181 |     /**
  182 |      * Coincide slug del hub (addon_slug, product_id) con agujas del registry aunque difieran guiones / prefijo xabia-.
  183 |      */
  184 |     private static function addon_slug_matches_hub(string $rowSlug, string $needle): bool {
  185 |         $a = self::normalize_hub_addon_token($rowSlug);
  186 |         $b = self::normalize_hub_addon_token($needle);
  187 |         if ($a === '' || $b === '') {
  188 |             return false;
  189 |         }
  190 |         if ($a === $b) {
  191 |             return true;
  192 |         }
  193 |         $strip = static function (string $x): string {
  194 |             return (string) preg_replace('#^xabia[-]#', '', $x);
  195 |         };
  196 | 
  197 |         return $strip($a) === $strip($b);
  198 |     }
  199 | 
  200 |     /**
  201 |      * @return array{
  202 |      *   subscription_active:bool,
  203 |      *   license_valid:bool,
  204 |      *   renewal_iso:?string,
  205 |      *   renewal_ts:int,
  206 |      *   addon_activated_iso:?string,
  207 |      *   addon_activated_ts:int,
  208 |      *   message:string,
  209 |      *   hub_addon_slugs:list<string>,
  210 |      *   checked_at:int,
  211 |      *   from_cache:bool,
  212 |      *   validated_with_core_fallback:bool
  213 |      * }
  214 |      */
  215 |     public static function get_hub_status(string $slug, bool $force_refresh = false): array {
  216 |         $slug = sanitize_key($slug);
  217 |         $def = self::get_definition($slug);
  218 |         $hubSlugsRaw = is_array($def) ? ($def['hub_addon_slugs'] ?? ['']) : [''];
  219 |         $hubSlugs = [];
  220 |         foreach ((array) $hubSlugsRaw as $h) {
  221 |             $h = strtolower(trim((string) $h));
  222 |             if ($h !== '') {
  223 |                 $hubSlugs[] = $h;
  224 |             }
  225 |         }
  226 |         if ($hubSlugs === []) {
  227 |             $hubSlugs = [sanitize_key($slug)];
  228 |         }
  229 | 
  230 |         $empty = [
  231 |             'subscription_active'            => false,
  232 |             'license_valid'                  => false,
  233 |             'renewal_iso'                  => null,
  234 |             'renewal_ts'                   => 0,
  235 |             'addon_activated_iso'          => null,
  236 |             'addon_activated_ts'           => 0,
  237 |             'message'                      => __('Sin datos de suscripción.', 'xabia-intelligence'),
  238 |             'hub_addon_slugs'                => $hubSlugs,
  239 |             'checked_at'                     => 0,
  240 |             'from_cache'                     => false,
  241 |             'validated_with_core_fallback'   => false,
  242 |             'inactive_reason'                => '',
  243 |         ];
  244 | 
  245 |         $tname = self::transient_name($slug);
  246 |         if (!$force_refresh) {
  247 |             $cached = get_transient($tname);
  248 |             if (is_array($cached) && isset($cached['subscription_active'], $cached['checked_at'])) {
  249 |                 $cached['from_cache'] = true;
  250 | 
  251 |                 return wp_parse_args($cached, $empty);
  252 |             }
  253 |         }
  254 | 
  255 |         $storedAddonKey = self::get_stored_license_key($slug);
  256 |         $primaryKey = self::effective_license_key($slug);
  257 |         $coreKey = '';
  258 |         if (class_exists('Xabia_Digixop_Client', false)) {
  259 |             $coreKey = trim(Xabia_Digixop_Client::get_license_key());
  260 |         }
  261 |         $keysToTry = [];
  262 |         if ($primaryKey !== '') {
  263 |             $keysToTry[] = $primaryKey;
  264 |         }
  265 |         if ($coreKey !== '' && !in_array($coreKey, $keysToTry, true)) {
  266 |             $keysToTry[] = $coreKey;
  267 |         }
  268 |         $keysToTry = apply_filters('xabia_addons_hub_license_keys_to_try', $keysToTry, $slug, $hubSlugs);
  269 |         if (!is_array($keysToTry)) {
  270 |             $keysToTry = [];
  271 |         }
  272 |         $keysToTry = array_values(array_filter(array_map(static function ($k): string {
  273 |             return is_string($k) ? trim($k) : '';
  274 |         }, $keysToTry), static fn (string $k): bool => $k !== ''));
  275 | 
  276 |         if ($keysToTry === []) {
  277 |             $out = $empty;
  278 |             $out['message'] = __('Introduce la clave de licencia o configura la licencia Core.', 'xabia-intelligence');
  279 |             set_transient($tname, $out, self::HUB_CACHE_TTL_INACTIVE);
  280 | 
  281 |             return $out;
  282 |         }
  283 | 
  284 |         if (!class_exists('Xabia_Digixop_Client', false)) {
  285 |             set_transient($tname, $empty, self::HUB_CACHE_TTL_INACTIVE);
  286 | 
  287 |             return $empty;
  288 |         }
  289 | 
  290 |         $json = null;
  291 |         $subscriptionActive = false;
  292 |         $licenseValid = false;
  293 |         $row = null;
  294 |         $usedCoreFallback = false;
  295 |         foreach ($keysToTry as $idx => $tryKey) {
  296 |             $j = Xabia_Digixop_Client::license_validate_json_for_key($tryKey);
  297 |             if (!is_array($j)) {
  298 |                 continue;
  299 |             }
  300 |             $json = $j;
  301 |             $lv = !empty($j['valid']) || !empty($j['success']);
  302 |             $licenseValid = $lv;
  303 |             $r = self::hub_payload_includes_addon($j, $hubSlugs);
  304 |             $row = $r;
  305 |             if ($lv && $r !== null) {
  306 |                 $subscriptionActive = true;
  307 |                 if ($idx > 0) {
  308 |                     $usedCoreFallback = true;
  309 |                 }
  310 | 
  311 |                 break;
  312 |             }
  313 |         }
  314 | 
  315 |         if (!is_array($json)) {
  316 |             $out = $empty;
  317 |             $out['message'] = __('No se pudo contactar con el hub de licencias en este momento.', 'xabia-intelligence');
  318 |             $out['inactive_reason'] = 'hub_unreachable';
  319 |             set_transient($tname, $out, min(self::HUB_CACHE_TTL_INACTIVE, 120));
  320 | 
  321 |             return $out;
  322 |         }
  323 | 
  324 |         $renewalIso = null;
  325 |         $renewalTs = 0;
  326 |         $activatedIso = null;
  327 |         $activatedTs = 0;
  328 |         if (is_array($row)) {
  329 |             $exp = $row['expiry_date'] ?? null;
  330 |             if (is_string($exp) && $exp !== '') {
  331 |                 $renewalIso = $exp;
  332 |                 $renewalTs = (int) strtotime($exp);
  333 |             }
  334 |             $act = $row['activated_at'] ?? null;
  335 |             if (is_string($act) && $act !== '') {
  336 |                 $activatedIso = $act;
  337 |                 $activatedTs = (int) strtotime($act);
  338 |             }
  339 |         }
  340 | 
  341 |         $hubReason = isset($json['reason']) ? (string) $json['reason'] : '';
  342 |         $msg = isset($json['message']) && is_string($json['message']) ? trim((string) $json['message']) : '';
  343 |         if ($msg === '') {
  344 |             $msg = $subscriptionActive
  345 |                 ? __('Suscripción activa.', 'xabia-intelligence')
  346 |                 : __('Suscripción no encontrada o caducada para este addon.', 'xabia-intelligence');
  347 |         }
  348 |         if (!$subscriptionActive) {
  349 |             if ($hubReason === 'unauthorized_domain') {
  350 |                 $msg = __('Dominio no autorizado para esta licencia en el Hub.', 'xabia-intelligence');
  351 |             } elseif ($hubReason === 'license_key_unknown') {
  352 |                 $msg = __('Clave de licencia desconocida en el Hub.', 'xabia-intelligence');
  353 |             } elseif ($licenseValid) {
  354 |                 $msg = __('Licencia válida: el Hub no lista este add-on en tu cuenta (actívalo en Polar o contacta soporte).', 'xabia-intelligence');
  355 |             } elseif ($hubReason === '') {
  356 |                 $msg = __('Licencia no válida o inactiva; revisa Conexión a la IA.', 'xabia-intelligence');
  357 |             }
  358 |         }
  359 |         if ($subscriptionActive && $usedCoreFallback && $storedAddonKey !== '') {
  360 |             $msg = __('Suscripción activa (el hub reconoce Avirato en tu licencia principal del sitio). Puedes borrar la clave de este campo y guardar para evitar confusiones.', 'xabia-intelligence');
  361 |         }
  362 | 
  363 |         $inactiveReason = '';
  364 |         if (!$subscriptionActive && $licenseValid) {
  365 |             $inactiveReason = 'addon_not_on_license';
  366 |         } elseif (!$subscriptionActive && !$licenseValid) {
  367 |             $inactiveReason = 'license_invalid';
  368 |         }
  369 |         if ($hubReason === 'unauthorized_domain') {
  370 |             $inactiveReason = 'license_invalid';
  371 |         }
  372 |         $out = [
  373 |             'subscription_active'          => $subscriptionActive,
  374 |             'license_valid'                => $licenseValid,
  375 |             'renewal_iso'                  => $renewalIso,
  376 |             'renewal_ts'                   => $renewalTs,
  377 |             'addon_activated_iso'          => $activatedIso,
  378 |             'addon_activated_ts'           => $activatedTs,
  379 |             'message'                      => $msg,
  380 |             'hub_addon_slugs'              => $hubSlugs,
  381 |             'checked_at'                   => time(),
  382 |             'from_cache'                   => false,
  383 |             'validated_with_core_fallback' => $usedCoreFallback,
  384 |             'inactive_reason'              => $inactiveReason,
  385 |         ];
  386 |         $ttl = $subscriptionActive ? self::HUB_CACHE_TTL : self::HUB_CACHE_TTL_INACTIVE;
  387 |         set_transient($tname, $out, $ttl);
  388 | 
  389 |         return $out;
  390 |     }
  391 | 
  392 |     public static function is_active(string $slug): bool {
  393 |         $slug = sanitize_key($slug);
  394 |         if ($slug === '') {
  395 |             return false;
  396 |         }
  397 | 
  398 |         return self::get_hub_status($slug, false)['subscription_active'];
  399 |     }
  400 | 
  401 |     public static function flush_status_cache(string $slug): void {
  402 |         delete_transient(self::transient_name(sanitize_key($slug)));
  403 |     }
  404 | 
  405 |     /**
  406 |      * @return array{expiring_soon:bool,days_left:?int,urgent:bool}
  407 |      */
  408 |     public static function renewal_hint(string $slug): array {
  409 |         $st = self::get_hub_status($slug, false);
  410 |         $ts = (int) ($st['renewal_ts'] ?? 0);
  411 |         if ($ts < 1) {
  412 |             return ['expiring_soon' => false, 'days_left' => null, 'urgent' => false];
  413 |         }
  414 |         $days = (int) floor(($ts - time()) / DAY_IN_SECONDS);
  415 | 
  416 |         return [
  417 |             'expiring_soon' => $days >= 0 && $days <= 30,
  418 |             'days_left'     => $days,
  419 |             'urgent'        => $days >= 0 && $days <= 7,
  420 |         ];
  421 |     }
  422 | }

   10 | class Xabia_DB_Bridge {
   11 | 
   12 |     /**
   13 |      * MODO A: Procesar CSV (Legacy file-based)
   14 |      * Lee archivos físicos de la carpeta del proyecto y los vuelca a la tabla de vectores.
   15 |      * @param string $project_id
   16 |      * @param string $file_path Ruta al CSV.
   17 |      * @param array|null $attributes_override Mapeo de atributos para esta fuente (si multi-fuente). Si null, usa el del proyecto.
   18 |      */
   19 |     public static function process_csv_knowledge($project_id, $file_path, $attributes_override = null) {
   20 |         global $wpdb;
   21 |         if (!file_exists($file_path)) return 0;
   22 | 
   23 |         $projects = get_option('xabia_projects_config', []);
   24 |         $mapping  = $attributes_override !== null ? $attributes_override : ($projects[$project_id]['attributes'] ?? []);
   25 |         $table_target = Xabia_DB::table('knowledge_vectors');
   26 |         $count = 0;
   27 | 
   28 |         $handle = fopen($file_path, 'r');
   29 |         if (!$handle) return 0;
   30 | 
   31 |         
   32 |         $first_line = fgets($handle);
   33 |         $first_line = preg_replace('/^\xEF\xBB\xBF/', '', $first_line); 
   34 |         $separator = (substr_count($first_line, ';') > substr_count($first_line, ',')) ? ';' : ',';
   35 |         $headers = str_getcsv($first_line, $separator);
   36 |         $headers = array_map('trim', $headers);
   37 | 
   38 |         if (empty($headers)) { fclose($handle); return 0; }
   39 | 
   40 |         while (($data = fgetcsv($handle, 0, $separator)) !== FALSE) {
   41 |             if (empty($data) || (count($data) === 1 && is_null($data[0]))) continue;
   42 |             
   43 |             
   44 |             if (count($data) < count($headers)) $data = array_pad($data, count($headers), '');
   45 |             if (count($data) > count($headers)) $data = array_slice($data, 0, count($headers));
   46 |             
   47 |             $row = array_combine($headers, $data);
   48 |             if (!$row) continue;
   49 | 
   50 |             
   51 |             if (self::insert_record($project_id, $row, $mapping, $table_target)) $count++;
   52 |         }
   53 |         fclose($handle);
   54 |         return $count;
   55 |     }
   56 | 
   57 |     /**
   58 |      * MODO B: Procesar SQL (NUEVO / HÍBRIDO)
   59 |      * Capaz de inyectar el prefijo correcto (Manual o Automático) si la base de datos es remota.
   60 |      * @param string $project_id
   61 |      * @param array $sql_config host, user, name, pass, query, prefix (opcional).
   62 |      * @param array|null $attributes_override Mapeo de atributos para esta fuente (si multi-fuente). Si null, usa el del proyecto.
   63 |      */
   64 |     public static function process_sql_knowledge($project_id, $sql_config, $attributes_override = null) {
   65 |         $projects = get_option('xabia_projects_config', []);
   66 |         $config = $projects[$project_id] ?? [];
   67 |         $type = $config['source_type'] ?? 'sql';
   68 |         
   69 |         $sql = $sql_config['query'] ?? '';
   70 |         if (empty($sql)) return 0;
   71 | 
   72 |         global $wpdb;
   73 | 
   74 |         
   75 |         
   76 |         
   77 |         
   78 |         $manual_prefix = $sql_config['prefix'] ?? '';
   79 | 
   80 |         if ($manual_prefix !== '') {
   81 |             $real_prefix = $manual_prefix;
   82 |         } elseif (!empty($sql_config['host']) && $sql_config['host'] !== 'localhost' && $sql_config['host'] !== '127.0.0.1') {
   83 |             $real_prefix = 'wp_';
   84 |         } else {
   85 |             $real_prefix = $wpdb->prefix;
   86 |         }
   87 | 
   88 |         if (stripos($sql, '{prefix}') !== false) {
   89 |             $sql = str_replace('{prefix}', $real_prefix, $sql);
   90 |         }
   91 | 
   92 |         if ($real_prefix !== 'wp_' && stripos($sql, 'wp_') !== false) {
   93 |             $sql = preg_replace('/wp_([a-z])/', $real_prefix . '$1', $sql);
   94 |         }
   95 | 
   96 |         $sql_config['query'] = $sql;
   97 | 
   98 |         
   99 |         if (!class_exists('Xabia_SQL_Connector')) {
  100 |             $path = plugin_dir_path(dirname(__FILE__)) . 'integrations/class-xabia-sql-connector.php';
  101 |             if (file_exists($path)) require_once $path;
  102 |             else return 0;
  103 |         }
  104 | 
  105 |         
  106 |         $raw_data = Xabia_SQL_Connector::fetch_data($sql_config);
  107 |         
  108 |         if (is_wp_error($raw_data) || empty($raw_data)) return 0;
  109 | 
  110 |         $table_target = Xabia_DB::table('knowledge_vectors');
  111 |         $mapping = $attributes_override !== null ? $attributes_override : ($config['attributes'] ?? []);
  112 |         $count = 0;
  113 | 
  114 |         
  115 |         foreach ($raw_data as $row) {
  116 |             if (empty($row)) continue;
  117 |             if (self::insert_record($project_id, $row, $mapping, $table_target)) $count++;
  118 |         }
  119 | 
  120 |         return $count;
  121 |     }

  123 |     /**
  124 |      * Ingesta filas ya materializadas en PHP (addons con lógica no portable a un único SELECT).

  131 |     public static function process_prefetched_rows($project_id, array $rows, $attributes_override = null) {
  132 |         global $wpdb;
  133 |         $projects = get_option('xabia_projects_config', []);
  134 |         $config = $projects[$project_id] ?? [];

  139 |             if (!is_array($row) || $row === []) {
  140 |                 continue;
  141 |             }
  142 |             if (self::insert_record($project_id, $row, $mapping, $table_target)) {
  143 |                 $count++;

  150 |     /**
  151 |      * NÚCLEO COMÚN: Inserción y Normalización
  152 |      */
  153 |     private static function insert_record($project_id, $row, $mapping, $table_target) {
  154 | 
  155 |         $row = apply_filters('xabia_knowledge_sync_enrich_row', $row, $project_id, $mapping);
  156 |         if (!is_array($row)) {
  157 |             $row = [];
  158 |         }
  159 | 
  160 |         
  161 |         
  162 |         
  163 |         
  164 |         
  165 |         $ente_id = 'global';
  166 |         $ente_display = '';
  167 |         $ente_col = '';
  168 |         $ente_raw_val = '';
  169 | 
  170 |         
  171 |         if (!empty($mapping) && is_array($mapping)) {
  172 |             foreach ($mapping as $attr) {
  173 |                 if (!empty($attr['is_ente'])) {
  174 |                     $candidate = $attr['csv_col'] ?? '';
  175 |                     if ($candidate !== '' && isset($row[$candidate]) && trim((string)$row[$candidate]) !== '') {
  176 |                         $ente_col = $candidate;
  177 |                         $ente_raw_val = trim((string)$row[$candidate]);
  178 |                         $label_col = isset($attr['ente_label_col']) ? trim((string)$attr['ente_label_col']) : '';
  179 |                         if ($label_col !== '' && isset($row[$label_col])) {
  180 |                             $from_label = trim((string)$row[$label_col]);
  181 |                             $ente_display = $from_label !== '' ? $from_label : $ente_raw_val;
  182 |                         } else {
  183 |                             $ente_display = $ente_raw_val;
  184 |                         }
  185 |                         break;
  186 |                     }
  187 |                 }
  188 |             }
  189 |         }
  190 | 
  191 |         
  192 |         if ($ente_col === '' && !empty($mapping) && is_array($mapping)) {
  193 |             foreach ($mapping as $attr) {
  194 |                 $role = strtolower(trim((string)($attr['visual_role'] ?? '')));
  195 |                 if ($role === 'title' || $role === 'título') {
  196 |                     $candidate = $attr['csv_col'] ?? '';
  197 |                     if ($candidate !== '' && isset($row[$candidate]) && trim((string)$row[$candidate]) !== '') {
  198 |                         $ente_col = $candidate;
  199 |                         $ente_raw_val = trim((string)$row[$candidate]);
  200 |                         $ente_display = $ente_raw_val;
  201 |                         break;
  202 |                     }
  203 |                 }
  204 |             }
  205 |         }
  206 | 
  207 |         
  208 |         if ($ente_col === '') {
  209 |             $first_key = function_exists('array_key_first') ? array_key_first((array)$row) : null;
  210 |             if (!empty($first_key) && isset($row[$first_key]) && trim((string)$row[$first_key]) !== '') {
  211 |                 $ente_col = $first_key;
  212 |                 $ente_raw_val = trim((string)$row[$first_key]);
  213 |                 $ente_display = $ente_raw_val;
  214 |             }
  215 |         }
  216 | 
  217 |         if ($ente_display !== '') {
  218 |             $slug_base = $ente_raw_val !== '' ? $ente_raw_val : $ente_display;
  219 |             $ente_id = sanitize_title($slug_base);
  220 |             if ($ente_id === '') {
  221 |                 $ente_id = sanitize_title($ente_display);
  222 |             }
  223 |             if ($ente_id === '') {
  224 |                 $ente_id = 'global';
  225 |             }
  226 |         }
  227 | 
  228 |         
  229 |         $prepared = self::prepare_node_data($row, $mapping);
  230 | 
  231 |         
  232 |         if (!empty($ente_display)) {
  233 |             $prepared['meta_array']['__ente_display'] = $ente_display;
  234 |             $prepared['meta_array']['__ente_col'] = (string)$ente_col;
  235 |             $prepared['meta_array']['__ente_id'] = (string)$ente_id;
  236 |         }
  237 | 
  238 |         if (!class_exists('Xabia_DB', false)) {
  239 |             return false;
  240 |         }
  241 | 
  242 |         return Xabia_DB::insert_knowledge_vector_row(
  243 |             $project_id,
  244 |             $ente_id,
  245 |             (string) $prepared['text_blob'],
  246 |             $prepared['meta_array'],
  247 |             []
  248 |         );
  249 |     }
  250 | 
  251 |     /**
  252 |      * PREPARADOR DE NODOS: limpieza profunda de valores antes de armar content_chunk y meta_data.
  253 |      */
  254 |     private static function prepare_node_data($row, $mapping) {
  255 |         $meta_array = [];
  256 |         $text_parts = [];
  257 | 
  258 |         
  259 |         $clean = function($text) {
  260 |             $text = strip_tags((string)$text); 
  261 |             $text = preg_replace('/\s+/', ' ', $text); 
  262 |             return trim($text);
  263 |         };
  264 | 
  265 |         if (!empty($mapping)) {
  266 |             
  267 |             foreach ($mapping as $attr) {
  268 |                 $col   = $attr['csv_col'] ?? '';
  269 |                 $label = $attr['label'] ?? $col;
  270 |                 
  271 |                 if (isset($row[$col]) && trim($row[$col]) !== '') {
  272 |                     $clean_val = $clean($row[$col]);
  273 |                     $meta_array[sanitize_key($label)] = $clean_val; 
  274 |                     
  275 |                     
  276 |                     $instr = !empty($attr['instruction']) ? " ({$attr['instruction']})" : "";
  277 |                     $text_parts[] = $label . $instr . ': ' . $clean_val;
  278 |                 }
  279 |             }
  280 |         } else {
  281 |             
  282 |             foreach ($row as $k => $v) {
  283 |                 $clean_val = $clean($v);
  284 |                 if ($clean_val === '') continue;
  285 |                 $meta_array[sanitize_key($k)] = $clean_val;
  286 |                 $text_parts[] = $k . ': ' . $clean_val;
  287 |             }
  288 |         }
  289 | 
  290 |         return [
  291 |             'meta_array' => $meta_array,
  292 |             'text_blob'  => implode(' | ', $text_parts),
  293 |         ];
  294 |     }
  295 | }

   29 |      */
   30 |     public static function search_vector(
   31 |         string $project_id,
   32 |         array $query_embedding,
   33 |         string $ente_scope = 'global',
   34 |         bool $strict_ente = false,
   35 |         $max_chunks = null,
   36 |         float $threshold = 0.2,
   37 |         ?string $query_text = null
   38 |     ): array {
   39 |         if (!self::is_hub_rag_enabled($project_id)) {
   40 |             return ['context' => '', 'chunk_count' => 0, 'similarity_avg' => null, 'total_found' => null];
   41 |         }

   43 |         $qv = [];

   46 |                 $qv[] = (float) $v;
   47 |             }
   48 |         }
   49 |         if ($qv === []) {
   50 |             return ['context' => '', 'chunk_count' => 0, 'similarity_avg' => null, 'total_found' => null];
   51 |         }
   52 |         $mc = $max_chunks !== null ? (int) $max_chunks : 4;
   53 |         $body = [
   54 |             'project_id'           => $project_id,
   55 |             'query_embedding'      => $qv,
   56 |             'max_chunks'           => max(1, min(class_exists('Xabia_Brain', false) ? Xabia_Brain::MAX_RAG_CHUNKS : 15, $mc)),
   57 |             'similarity_threshold' => max(0.0, min(1.0, $threshold)),
   58 |         ];
   59 |         if ($strict_ente && $ente_scope !== '' && $ente_scope !== 'global') {
   60 |             $body['ente_id'] = $ente_scope;
   61 |         }
   62 |         $qt = $query_text !== null ? trim(wp_strip_all_tags((string) $query_text)) : '';
   63 |         if ($qt !== '') {
   64 |             $body['query_text'] = mb_substr($qt, 0, 2000);
   65 |         }

   67 |         if (!$out['ok'] || !is_array($out['body'])) {
   68 |             if (function_exists('xabia_trace')) {
   69 |                 xabia_trace('[Xabia Hub RAG] search failed', ['code' => $out['code'], 'snippet' => substr($out['raw'], 0, 400)]);
   70 |             } else {
   71 |                 error_log('[Xabia Hub RAG] search failed HTTP ' . (string) $out['code'] . ' ' . substr($out['raw'], 0, 400));
   72 |             }
   73 | 
   74 |             return ['context' => '', 'chunk_count' => 0, 'similarity_avg' => null, 'total_found' => null];
   75 |         }
   76 |         $b = $out['body'];
   77 |         $results = isset($b['chunks']) && is_array($b['chunks']) ? $b['chunks'] : [];

   82 | 
   83 |         return [

   86 |             'similarity_avg'  => is_numeric($avg) ? (float) $avg : null,

   88 |         ];
   89 |     }

   96 |     public static function sync_vectors_to_hub(string $project_id): array {

  122 |                 'inserted'=> 0,

  132 |             $wpdb->prepare(

  134 |                 $project_id
  135 |             ),
  136 |             ARRAY_A
  137 |         );

  176 |             }

  181 |             ];
  182 |             $did_replace = true;

  223 |                 'detail'  => null,
  224 |             ];
  225 |         }
  226 |

  228 |             'ok'      => true,
  229 |             'message' => __('Conocimiento sincronizado. Xabia ya tiene acceso a estos datos.', 'xabia-intelligence'),
  230 |             'inserted'=> $inserted,
  231 |             'batches' => $batchIndex,
  232 |             'detail'  => null,
  233 |         ];

  236 |     /**
  237 |      * Valor del campo de imagen en meta_data del vector (mapeo SQL / CSV).
  238 |      *
  239 |      * @param array<string, mixed> $meta
  240 |      */
  241 |     private static function extract_imagen_from_meta(array $meta): string {
  242 |         foreach (['imagen', 'image', 'url_imagen', 'imagen_url', 'featured_image', 'foto', 'photo', 'thumbnail'] as $k) {
  243 |             if (!array_key_exists($k, $meta) || $meta[$k] === null || $meta[$k] === '') {
  244 |                 continue;
  245 |             }
  246 |             $v = is_scalar($meta[$k]) ? (string) $meta[$k] : '';
  247 |             $v = trim($v);
  248 |             if ($v !== '') {
  249 |                 return $v;
  250 |             }
  251 |         }
  252 |         foreach ($meta as $k => $v) {
  253 |             if (!is_string($k) || !is_scalar($v)) {
  254 |                 continue;
  255 |             }
  256 |             $kl = strtolower($k);
  257 |             if (strpos($kl, 'imag') !== false || $kl === 'foto' || $kl === 'photo' || strpos($kl, 'thumbnail') !== false) {
  258 |                 $s = trim((string) $v);
  259 |                 if ($s !== '') {
  260 |                     return $s;
  261 |                 }
  262 |             }
  263 |         }
  264 | 
  265 |         return '';
  266 |     }
  267 | 
  268 |     /**
  269 |      * El Hub inyecta en el RAG principalmente content_chunk; meta_json no se expone al modelo salvo reforzos.
  270 |      * Anexamos el valor «imagen» en texto inequívoco para [ACTION:IMG:VALOR].
  271 |      *
  272 |      * @param array<string, mixed> $meta
  273 |      */
  274 |     private static function append_imagen_for_rag_context(string $content_chunk, array $meta): string {
  275 |         $raw = self::extract_imagen_from_meta($meta);
  276 |         if ($raw === '') {
  277 |             return $content_chunk;
  278 |         }
  279 |         if ($content_chunk !== '' && stripos($content_chunk, $raw) !== false) {
  280 |             return $content_chunk;
  281 |         }
  282 |         $suffix = "\n\n=== IMAGEN (mapeo; usar este valor tal cual en [ACTION:IMG:…]) ===\nimagen: " . $raw;
  283 | 
  284 |         return $content_chunk . $suffix;
  285 |     }
  286 | }

  255 |     public function render_wallet_view() {
  256 |         if (!current_user_can('manage_options')) {
  257 |             return;
  258 |         }
  259 |         if (class_exists('Xabia_Digixop_Client', false)) {
  260 |             Xabia_Digixop_Client::refresh_license_meta_from_hub_if_stale();
  261 |         }
  262 |         $wallet = self::get_wallet_summary();
  263 |         $usage = self::get_wallet_usage_30_days();
  264 |         $used30 = (int) $usage['total'];
  265 |         $hubMeta = class_exists('Xabia_Digixop_Client', false) ? Xabia_Digixop_Client::get_cached_license_meta() : null;
  266 |         $remaining = 0;

  268 |             $remaining = (int) $hubMeta['tokens_remaining'];
  269 |         }
  270 |         $capacity = max(1, $remaining + $used30);
  271 |         $percent = min(100, max(0, (int) round(($used30 / $capacity) * 100)));

 4124 |             function xabiaFilterAmeliaEnteList(q) {
 4125 |                 var $ul = $('#xabia-amelia-ente-list');
 4126 |                 $ul.empty();
 4127 |                 if (!xabiaAmeliaServicesCache || !xabiaAmeliaServicesCache.length) {
 4128 |                     $ul.append($('<li class="description" style="padding:8px;"></li>').text('<?php echo esc_js(__('No hay servicios visibles.', 'xabia-intelligence')); ?>'));
 4129 |                     return;
 4130 |                 }
 4131 |                 q = (q || '').toLowerCase();
 4132 |                 var n = 0;
 4133 |                 xabiaAmeliaServicesCache.forEach(function(s) {
 4134 |                     if (!s || s.id == null) return;
 4135 |                     var name = (s.name || '').toString();
 4136 |                     if (q && name.toLowerCase().indexOf(q) === -1) return;
 4137 |                     n++;
 4138 |                     var slug = 'amelia-svc-' + s.id;
 4139 |                     var $li = $('<li style="padding:8px;border-bottom:1px solid #f0f0f1;cursor:pointer;"></li>');
 4140 |                     $li.append($('<strong></strong>').text('ID ' + s.id + ' — ' + name));
 4141 |                     $li.attr('title', '<?php echo esc_js(__('Clic: copiar slug sugerido (?ente_id=)', 'xabia-intelligence')); ?>');
 4142 |                     $li.on('click', function() {
 4143 |                         var hint = '<?php echo esc_js(__('Slug sugerido (modo estricto / Ente):', 'xabia-intelligence')); ?> ' + slug + ' — ?ente_id=' + slug;
 4144 |                         $('#xabia-amelia-ente-hint').text(hint);
 4145 |                         if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) {

 4450 |             function xabiaPlaygroundHistory() {
 4451 |                 var hist = [];

 4453 |                     var $row = $(this);

 4456 |                     content = String(content || '').replace(/^(Tú|Xabia|Nora|Laura|[^:]{1,80}):\s*/i, '').trim();
 4457 |                     if (content) hist.push({ role: role, content: content });
 4458 |                 });

 4460 |             }

 4515 |     private static function get_vector_counts($pid) { 
 4516 |         global $wpdb; 
 4517 |         $t = Xabia_DB::table('knowledge_vectors');
 4518 |         $pending_sql = class_exists('Xabia_DB', false) ? Xabia_DB::knowledge_vectors_sql_has_embedding() : 'vector_data IS NOT NULL';
 4519 |         $total = $wpdb->get_var($wpdb->prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM $t WHERE project_id=%s",$pid));
 4520 |         $ready = $wpdb->get_var($wpdb->prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM $t WHERE project_id=%s AND ({$pending_sql})",$pid)); 
 4521 |         return ['total'=>(int)$total, 'ready'=>(int)$ready]; 
 4522 |     }
 4523 | 
 4524 |     private static function get_today_token_usage($pid) {
 4525 |         global $wpdb;
 4526 |         $t = Xabia_DB::table('usage_logs');
 4527 |         if ($wpdb->get_var("SHOW TABLES LIKE '$t'") !== $t) {
 4528 |             return 0;
 4529 |         }
 4530 |         $start = gmdate('Y-m-d 00:00:00');
 4531 |         $end = gmdate('Y-m-d 23:59:59');
 4532 |         $sum = $wpdb->get_var($wpdb->prepare(
 4533 |             "SELECT COALESCE(SUM(tokens_input + tokens_output),0) FROM $t WHERE project_id=%s AND created_at BETWEEN %s AND %s",
 4534 |             $pid,
 4535 |             $start,
 4536 |             $end
 4537 |         ));
 4538 |         return (int) $sum;
 4539 |     }
 4540 | 
 4541 |     private static function get_wallet_summary(bool $ensure = true): ?array {
 4542 |         global $wpdb;
 4543 |         if (!class_exists('Xabia_DB', false)) {
 4544 |             return null;
 4545 |         }
 4546 |         $table = Xabia_DB::table('wallets');
 4547 |         if ($wpdb->get_var($wpdb->prepare('SHOW TABLES LIKE %s', $table)) !== $table) {
 4548 |             Xabia_DB::install_tables();
 4549 |         }
 4550 |         if ($ensure) {
 4551 |             Xabia_DB::sync_wallet_balance(null);
 4552 |         }
 4553 |         $licenseId = Xabia_DB::wallet_license_id();
 4554 |         $row = $wpdb->get_row($wpdb->prepare("SELECT * FROM $table WHERE license_id = %s", $licenseId), ARRAY_A);

 4572 |     private static function get_wallet_usage_30_days(): array {
 4573 |         global $wpdb;
 4574 |         $table = Xabia_DB::table('usage_logs');
 4575 |         $days = [];
 4576 |         for ($i = 29; $i >= 0; $i--) {
 4577 |             $date = gmdate('Y-m-d', strtotime('-' . $i . ' days'));
 4578 |             $days[$date] = ['date' => $date, 'tokens' => 0];
 4579 |         }
 4580 |         if ($wpdb->get_var($wpdb->prepare('SHOW TABLES LIKE %s', $table)) !== $table) {
 4581 |             return ['days' => array_values($days), 'total' => 0, 'max' => 0];
 4582 |         }
 4583 |         $columns = $wpdb->get_col("SHOW COLUMNS FROM $table", 0);
 4584 |         $columns = is_array($columns) ? array_map('strval', $columns) : [];
 4585 |         $dateCol = in_array('created_at', $columns, true) ? 'created_at' : (in_array('timestamp', $columns, true) ? 'timestamp' : '');
 4586 |         if ($dateCol === '') {
 4587 |             return ['days' => array_values($days), 'total' => 0, 'max' => 0];
 4588 |         }
 4589 |         $tokenExpr = in_array('tokens_count', $columns, true)
 4590 |             ? 'tokens_count'
 4591 |             : '(tokens_input + tokens_output)';
 4592 |         $since = gmdate('Y-m-d 00:00:00', strtotime('-29 days'));
 4593 |         $rows = $wpdb->get_results($wpdb->prepare(
 4594 |             "SELECT DATE($dateCol) AS day_key, COALESCE(SUM($tokenExpr),0) AS tokens
 4595 |              FROM $table
 4596 |              WHERE $dateCol >= %s
 4597 |              GROUP BY DATE($dateCol)",
 4598 |             $since
 4599 |         ), ARRAY_A);
 4600 |         $total = 0;
 4601 |         $max = 0;
 4602 |         foreach ((array) $rows as $row) {
 4603 |             $key = (string) ($row['day_key'] ?? '');
 4604 |             if (!isset($days[$key])) {
 4605 |                 continue;
 4606 |             }
 4607 |             $tokens = (int) ($row['tokens'] ?? 0);
 4608 |             $days[$key]['tokens'] = $tokens;
 4609 |             $total += $tokens;
 4610 |             $max = max($max, $tokens);
 4611 |         }
 4612 | 
 4613 |         return ['days' => array_values($days), 'total' => $total, 'max' => $max];
 4614 |     }

  111 | /**
  112 |  * Normaliza una fila (desde nodo) al formato canónico y hace upsert.
  113 |  * Formato entrante: canonical (ente_id, ente_display, content_chunk, meta_data) o raw (array asociativo + mapping).
  114 |  */
  115 | class Xabia_Central_Normalize {
  116 | 
  117 |     /**
  118 |      * Antepone atribución de nodo al texto canónico (RAG / contexto).
  119 |      */
  120 |     private static function prepend_node_source($content, $node_name) {
  121 |         $content = (string) $content;
  122 |         $node_name = trim((string) $node_name);
  123 |         if ($node_name === '' || trim($content) === '') {
  124 |             return trim($content);
  125 |         }
  126 |         return 'Fuente: ' . $node_name . ' | ' . $content;
  127 |     }
  128 | 
  129 |     /**
  130 |      * Convierte fila a canónico. $row = array asociativo; $mapping = array de {source_key, label, is_ente}; si mapping vacío y row tiene ente_id/content_chunk, se usa como canonical.
  131 |      *
  132 |      * @param string $node_name Nombre legible del nodo federado (atribución en content_chunk).
  133 |      */
  134 |     public static function row_to_canonical($row, $mapping, $default_ente_id = 'global', $node_name = '') {
  135 |         $ente_id = $default_ente_id;
  136 |         $ente_display = '';
  137 |         $parts = [];
  138 |         $meta = [];
  139 | 
  140 |         if (empty($mapping)) {
  141 |             $ente_id = isset($row['ente_id']) && trim((string) $row['ente_id']) !== '' ? sanitize_title($row['ente_id']) : $default_ente_id;
  142 |             $ente_display = isset($row['ente_display']) ? trim(strip_tags($row['ente_display'])) : '';
  143 |             $content = isset($row['content_chunk']) ? trim(strip_tags($row['content_chunk'])) : '';
  144 |             $meta = isset($row['meta_data']) && is_array($row['meta_data']) ? $row['meta_data'] : (is_string($row['meta_data'] ?? '') ? (json_decode($row['meta_data'], true) ?: []) : []);
  145 |             $content = self::prepend_node_source($content, $node_name);
  146 |             return [
  147 |                 'ente_id' => $ente_id ?: $default_ente_id,
  148 |                 'ente_display' => $ente_display,
  149 |                 'content_chunk' => $content,
  150 |                 'meta_data' => $meta,
  151 |             ];
  152 |         }
  153 | 
  154 |         foreach ($mapping as $m) {
  155 |             $key = $m['source_key'] ?? $m['csv_col'] ?? '';
  156 |             if ($key === '' || !isset($row[$key])) continue;
  157 |             $val = trim(strip_tags((string) $row[$key]));
  158 |             $label = $m['label'] ?? $key;
  159 |             if ($m['is_ente'] ?? false) {
  160 |                 $ente_raw = $val;
  161 |                 $label_col = isset($m['ente_label_col']) ? trim((string) $m['ente_label_col']) : '';
  162 |                 if ($label_col !== '' && isset($row[$label_col])) {
  163 |                     $from_label = trim(strip_tags((string) $row[$label_col]));
  164 |                     $ente_display = $from_label !== '' ? $from_label : $ente_raw;
  165 |                 } else {
  166 |                     $ente_display = $ente_raw;
  167 |                 }
  168 |                 $slug_base = $ente_raw !== '' ? $ente_raw : $ente_display;
  169 |                 $ente_id = sanitize_title($slug_base) ?: (sanitize_title($ente_display) ?: $default_ente_id);
  170 |             }
  171 |             $parts[] = $label . ': ' . $val;
  172 |             $meta[$label] = $val;
  173 |         }
  174 |         $content = implode(' | ', $parts);
  175 |         $content = self::prepend_node_source($content, $node_name);
  176 |         if ($ente_display !== '') $meta['__ente_display'] = $ente_display;
  177 |         $meta['__ente_id'] = $ente_id;
  178 |         if (trim((string) $node_name) !== '') {
  179 |             $meta['__federation_node_name'] = trim((string) $node_name);
  180 |         }
  181 | 
  182 |         return [
  183 |             'ente_id' => $ente_id,
  184 |             'ente_display' => $ente_display,
  185 |             'content_chunk' => $content,
  186 |             'meta_data' => $meta,
  187 |         ];
  188 |     }
  189 | 
  190 |     /**
  191 |      * Upsert en xabia_knowledge_vectors. Evita re-embedding: vector_data null (entrenar después si se desea).
  192 |      * Un registro por (project_id, federation_node_id, ente_id); actualiza si existe.
  193 |      *
  194 |      * @param string $node_name Nombre del nodo (atribución en content_chunk vía row_to_canonical).
  195 |      */
  196 |     public static function upsert_batch($project_id, $federation_node_id, $rows, $mapping = [], $node_name = '') {
  197 |         global $wpdb;
  198 |         $t = Xabia_DB::table('knowledge_vectors');
  199 |         $cols = Xabia_DB::knowledge_vectors_column_map();
  200 |         $has_fed = isset($cols['federation_node_id']);
  201 |         $count = 0;
  202 |         foreach ($rows as $row) {
  203 |             $canon = self::row_to_canonical($row, $mapping, 'global', $node_name);
  204 |             if (trim($canon['content_chunk']) === '') {
  205 |                 continue;
  206 |             }
  207 |             $meta_json = wp_json_encode($canon['meta_data'], JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE);
  208 |             if ($meta_json === false) {
  209 |                 $meta_json = '{}';
  210 |             }
  211 |             $ente_id = $canon['ente_id'];
  212 |             if ($has_fed) {
  213 |                 $existing = $wpdb->get_var($wpdb->prepare(
  214 |                     "SELECT id FROM $t WHERE project_id = %s AND federation_node_id = %s AND ente_id = %s LIMIT 1",
  215 |                     $project_id,
  216 |                     $federation_node_id,
  217 |                     $ente_id
  218 |                 ));
  219 |             } else {
  220 |                 $existing = $wpdb->get_var($wpdb->prepare(
  221 |                     "SELECT id FROM $t WHERE project_id = %s AND ente_id = %s LIMIT 1",
  222 |                     $project_id,
  223 |                     $ente_id
  224 |                 ));
  225 |             }
  226 |             $source_file = 'federation:' . $federation_node_id;
  227 |             if ($existing) {
  228 |                 $upd = ['content_chunk' => $canon['content_chunk']];
  229 |                 if (isset($cols['meta_json'])) {
  230 |                     $upd['meta_json'] = $meta_json;
  231 |                 } elseif (isset($cols['meta_data'])) {
  232 |                     $upd['meta_data'] = $meta_json;
  233 |                 }
  234 |                 if (isset($cols['source_file'])) {
  235 |                     $upd['source_file'] = $source_file;
  236 |                 }
  237 |                 $ok = $wpdb->update($t, $upd, ['id' => $existing]);
  238 |             } else {
  239 |                 $ok = Xabia_DB::insert_knowledge_vector_row(
  240 |                     $project_id,
  241 |                     $ente_id,
  242 |                     $canon['content_chunk'],
  243 |                     $canon['meta_data'],
  244 |                     [
  245 |                         'source_file'        => $source_file,
  246 |                         'federation_node_id' => $has_fed ? $federation_node_id : null,
  247 |                     ]
  248 |                 );
  249 |             }
  250 |             if ($ok !== false) {
  251 |                 ++$count;
  252 |             }
  253 |         }
  254 | 
  255 |         return $count;
  256 |     }

    2 | /**
    3 |  * Xabia Central — Sync (pull desde nodos)
    4 |  * Eficacia: una petición por nodo; batch upsert; sin re-embedding hasta que el usuario entrene.
    5 |  */
    6 | 
    7 | if (!defined('ABSPATH')) exit;
    8 | 
    9 | class Xabia_Central_Sync {
   10 | 
   11 |     /**
   12 |      * Sincroniza un proyecto: para cada nodo tipo pull, obtiene datos y upsert.
   13 |      * Retorna total de registros insertados/actualizados.
   14 |      */
   15 |     public static function sync_project($project_id) {
   16 |         $nodes = Xabia_Central_Nodes::get_for_project($project_id);
   17 |         $total = 0;
   18 |         global $wpdb;
   19 |         $t_nodes = Xabia_DB::table('federation_nodes');
   20 | 
   21 |         foreach ($nodes as $node) {
   22 |             if (($node['type'] ?? '') !== 'pull') continue;
   23 |             $config = $node['config'] ?? [];
   24 |             $node_id = $node['node_id'];
   25 |             $endpoint = $config['endpoint_url'] ?? '';
   26 |             $format = $config['format'] ?? 'json';
   27 |             $mapping = $config['mapping'] ?? [];
   28 | 
   29 |             $rows = [];
   30 |             $error = null;
   31 | 
   32 |             if ($endpoint !== '') {
   33 |                 $rows = self::pull_from_endpoint($endpoint, $format, $config, $error);
   34 |             } else {
   35 |                 $error = 'Falta endpoint_url en el nodo';
   36 |             }
   37 | 
   38 |             if ($error !== null) {
   39 |                 $wpdb->update($t_nodes, ['last_error' => substr($error, 0, 255), 'last_sync_at' => current_time('mysql')], ['id' => $node['id']]);
   40 |                 continue;
   41 |             }
   42 | 
   43 |             $node_display_name = trim((string) ($node['name'] ?? ''));
   44 |             if ($node_display_name === '') {
   45 |                 $node_display_name = (string) $node_id;
   46 |             }
   47 |             $count = Xabia_Central_Normalize::upsert_batch($project_id, $node_id, $rows, $mapping, $node_display_name);
   48 |             $total += $count;
   49 |             $wpdb->update($t_nodes, ['last_error' => null, 'last_sync_at' => current_time('mysql')], ['id' => $node['id']]);
   50 |         }
   51 | 
   52 |         return $total;
   53 |     }
   54 | 
   55 |     /**
   56 |      * Obtiene datos desde URL: JSON (array de objetos) o CSV.
   57 |      */
   58 |     private static function pull_from_endpoint($url, $format, $config, &$error) {
   59 |         $error = null;
   60 |         $headers = ['Accept' => 'application/json'];
   61 |         if (!empty($config['auth_header'])) {
   62 |             $headers['Authorization'] = $config['auth_header'];
   63 |         }
   64 |         $response = wp_remote_get($url, [
   65 |             'timeout' => 30,
   66 |             'headers' => $headers,
   67 |             'sslverify' => !empty($config['ssl_verify']),
   68 |         ]);
   69 | 
   70 |         if (is_wp_error($response)) {
   71 |             $error = $response->get_error_message();
   72 |             return [];
   73 |         }
   74 |         $code = wp_remote_retrieve_response_code($response);
   75 |         if ($code < 200 || $code >= 300) {
   76 |             $error = 'HTTP ' . $code;
   77 |             return [];
   78 |         }
   79 |         $body = wp_remote_retrieve_body($response);
   80 |         if ($format === 'csv') {
   81 |             return self::parse_csv_string($body);
   82 |         }
   83 |         $data = json_decode($body, true);
   84 |         if (!is_array($data)) {
   85 |             $error = 'JSON inválido';
   86 |             return [];
   87 |         }
   88 |         if (isset($data['records'])) return $data['records'];
   89 |         if (isset($data['data'])) return $data['data'];
   90 |         if (array_is_list($data)) return $data;
   91 |         return [$data];
   92 |     }
   93 | 
   94 |     private static function parse_csv_string($str) {
   95 |         $lines = preg_split('/\r\n|\r|\n/', trim($str));
   96 |         if (empty($lines)) return [];
   97 |         $sep = (substr_count($lines[0], ';') >= substr_count($lines[0], ',')) ? ';' : ',';
   98 |         $headers = str_getcsv(array_shift($lines), $sep);
   99 |         $headers = array_map('trim', $headers);
  100 |         $out = [];
  101 |         foreach ($lines as $line) {
  102 |             $row = str_getcsv($line, $sep);
  103 |             if (count($row) !== count($headers)) $row = array_pad($row, count($headers), '');
  104 |             $out[] = array_combine($headers, array_slice($row, 0, count($headers)));
  105 |         }
  106 |         return $out;
  107 |     }
  108 | }

   43 |             return true;

   56 |     /**
   57 |      * Motor efectivo para el chat / expansión de tags (respeta addon del proyecto).

   61 |     public static function engine_for_project(string $project_id): string {
   62 |         $projects = get_option('xabia_projects_config', []);
   63 |         $data     = isset($projects[$project_id]) && is_array($projects[$project_id]) ? $projects[$project_id] : [];
   64 |         if (self::project_uses_addon($data, 'mec') && self::is_mec()) {
   65 |             return 'mec';
   66 |         }
   67 |         if (self::project_uses_addon($data, 'amelia') && self::is_amelia()) {
   68 |             return 'amelia';
   69 |         }
   70 |         return self::detect_engine();
   71 |     }
   72 | 
   73 |     /**
   74 |      * Lista servicios Amelia (tabla amelia_services) para admin / buscador.
   75 |      *
   76 |      * @return array<int, array{id:int, name:string}>
   77 |      */
   78 |     public static function get_amelia_services_list(string $search = '', int $limit = 200): array {
   79 |         global $wpdb;
   80 |         if (!self::is_amelia()) {
   81 |             return [];
   82 |         }
   83 |         $table = $wpdb->prefix . 'amelia_services';
   84 |         if ($wpdb->get_var($wpdb->prepare('SHOW TABLES LIKE %s', $table)) !== $table) {
   85 |             return [];
   86 |         }
   87 |         $limit = max(1, min(500, $limit));
   88 |         $like  = '%' . $wpdb->esc_like($search) . '%';
   89 |         $sql   = "SELECT id, name FROM {$table} WHERE status = 'visible'";
   90 |         if ($search !== '') {
   91 |             $sql .= $wpdb->prepare(' AND name LIKE %s', $like);
   92 |         }
   93 |         $sql .= $wpdb->prepare(" ORDER BY name ASC LIMIT %d", $limit);
   94 |         $rows = $wpdb->get_results($sql, ARRAY_A);
   95 |         if (!is_array($rows)) {
   96 |             return [];
   97 |         }
   98 |         $out = [];
   99 |         foreach ($rows as $r) {
  100 |             $out[] = [
  101 |                 'id'   => isset($r['id']) ? (int) $r['id'] : 0,
  102 |                 'name' => isset($r['name']) ? (string) $r['name'] : '',
  103 |             ];
  104 |         }
  105 |         return $out;
  106 |     }
  107 | 
  108 |     /**
  109 |      * Comprueba si hay “hueco” lógico antes de recomendar (extensible vía filtro).
  110 |      *
  111 |      * @param string               $engine   mec|amelia
  112 |      * @param int                  $entity_id ID de evento MEC (post) o servicio Amelia
  113 |      * @param array<string, mixed> $args      from, to (Y-m-d), timezone…
  114 |      *
  115 |      * @return array{available: ?bool, message: string, engine: string}
  116 |      */
  117 |     public static function check_availability(string $engine, int $entity_id, array $args = []): array {
  118 |         $engine = strtolower($engine);
  119 |         $result = apply_filters('xabia_reservas_check_availability', null, $engine, $entity_id, $args);
  120 |         if (is_array($result) && array_key_exists('available', $result)) {
  121 |             return $result + ['engine' => $engine, 'message' => (string) ($result['message'] ?? '')];
  122 |         }
  123 |         if ($entity_id < 1) {
  124 |             return ['available' => false, 'message' => __('ID no válido.', 'xabia-intelligence'), 'engine' => $engine];
  125 |         }
  126 |         if ($engine === 'mec') {
  127 |             return self::check_availability_mec($entity_id, $args);
  128 |         }
  129 |         if ($engine === 'amelia') {
  130 |             return self::check_availability_amelia($entity_id, $args);
  131 |         }
  132 |         return ['available' => null, 'message' => __('Motor de reservas no reconocido.', 'xabia-intelligence'), 'engine' => $engine];
  133 |     }
  134 | 
  135 |     /**
  136 |      * @param array<string, mixed> $args
  137 |      *
  138 |      * @return array{available: ?bool, message: string, engine: string}
  139 |      */
  140 |     private static function check_availability_mec(int $post_id, array $args): array {
  141 |         $pt = get_post_type($post_id);
  142 |         if ($pt !== 'mec-events') {
  143 |             return ['available' => false, 'message' => __('No es un evento MEC.', 'xabia-intelligence'), 'engine' => 'mec'];
  144 |         }
  145 |         $start = get_post_meta($post_id, 'mec_start_date', true);
  146 |         $start = is_string($start) ? trim($start) : '';
  147 |         if ($start === '') {
  148 |             return ['available' => null, 'message' => __('Evento sin mec_start_date en meta.', 'xabia-intelligence'), 'engine' => 'mec'];
  149 |         }
  150 |         $day = preg_replace('/\s.*/', '', $start);
  151 |         $day = preg_match('/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/', $day) ? $day : '';
  152 |         if ($day === '') {
  153 |             return ['available' => null, 'message' => __('Fecha de evento no interpretable.', 'xabia-intelligence'), 'engine' => 'mec'];
  154 |         }
  155 |         $event_ts = strtotime($day . ' 00:00:00');
  156 |         $today_ts = strtotime(current_time('Y-m-d') . ' 00:00:00');
  157 |         if ($event_ts === false || $today_ts === false) {
  158 |             return ['available' => null, 'message' => __('Fecha de evento no interpretable.', 'xabia-intelligence'), 'engine' => 'mec'];
  159 |         }
  160 |         if ($event_ts < $today_ts) {
  161 |             return ['available' => false, 'message' => __('El evento ya pasó (fecha de inicio anterior a hoy).', 'xabia-intelligence'), 'engine' => 'mec'];
  162 |         }
  163 |         if (function_exists('xabia_mec_compute_available_slots')) {
  164 |             $slots_raw = xabia_mec_compute_available_slots($post_id);
  165 |             if ($slots_raw !== '' && is_numeric($slots_raw) && (int) $slots_raw <= 0) {
  166 |                 return [
  167 |                     'available' => false,
  168 |                     'message'   => __('No quedan plazas disponibles para este evento.', 'xabia-intelligence'),
  169 |                     'engine'    => 'mec',
  170 |                 ];
  171 |             }
  172 |         }
  173 |         return ['available' => true, 'message' => '', 'engine' => 'mec'];
  174 |     }
  175 | 
  176 |     /**
  177 |      * @param array<string, mixed> $args
  178 |      *
  179 |      * @return array{available: ?bool, message: string, engine: string}
  180 |      */
  181 |     private static function check_availability_amelia(int $service_id, array $args): array {
  182 |         global $wpdb;
  183 |         $table = $wpdb->prefix . 'amelia_services';
  184 |         if ($wpdb->get_var($wpdb->prepare('SHOW TABLES LIKE %s', $table)) !== $table) {
  185 |             return ['available' => null, 'message' => __('Tabla Amelia no encontrada.', 'xabia-intelligence'), 'engine' => 'amelia'];
  186 |         }
  187 |         $status = $wpdb->get_var($wpdb->prepare("SELECT status FROM {$table} WHERE id = %d", $service_id));
  188 |         if ($status === null) {
  189 |             return ['available' => false, 'message' => __('Servicio no encontrado.', 'xabia-intelligence'), 'engine' => 'amelia'];
  190 |         }
  191 |         if ((string) $status !== 'visible') {
  192 |             return ['available' => false, 'message' => __('Servicio no visible para reserva.', 'xabia-intelligence'), 'engine' => 'amelia'];
  193 |         }
  194 |         return ['available' => true, 'message' => '', 'engine' => 'amelia'];
  195 |     }
  196 | }

    9 |     /**
   10 |      * Seguimiento de intención de compra (clics en añadir al carrito / pack) por proyecto.
   11 |      * window.xabiaWooCartIntent.getItems(projectId), .recordSingle(projectId, id), .recordPack(...), .getSummaryText(projectId), .clear(projectId)
   12 |      */
   13 |     window.xabiaWooCartIntent = (function() {
   14 |         function storageKey(projectId) {
   15 |             return 'xabia_woo_cart_intent_' + String(projectId || 'default');
   16 |         }
   17 |         function load(projectId) {
   18 |             try {
   19 |                 var raw = sessionStorage.getItem(storageKey(projectId));
   20 |                 var arr = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : [];
   21 |                 return Array.isArray(arr) ? arr : [];
   22 |             } catch (e) {
   23 |                 return [];
   24 |             }
   25 |         }
   26 |         function save(projectId, items) {
   27 |             try {
   28 |                 sessionStorage.setItem(storageKey(projectId), JSON.stringify(items.slice(-40)));
   29 |             } catch (e2) {}
   30 |         }
   31 |         function record(projectId, entry) {
   32 |             var items = load(projectId);
   33 |             items.push(entry);
   34 |             save(projectId, items);
   35 |         }
   36 |         return {
   37 |             recordSingle: function(projectId, productId, label) {
   38 |                 var id = parseInt(productId, 10);
   39 |                 if (!id) return;
   40 |                 record(String(projectId || 'default'), { id: id, t: Date.now(), label: label || '', kind: 'single' });
   41 |             },
   42 |             recordPack: function(projectId, ids, quantities, label) {
   43 |                 var pids = (ids || []).map(function(x) { return parseInt(x, 10); }).filter(function(n) { return n > 0; });
   44 |                 if (!pids.length) return;
   45 |                 record(String(projectId || 'default'), {
   46 |                     ids: pids,
   47 |                     qty: quantities || [],
   48 |                     t: Date.now(),
   49 |                     label: label || '',
   50 |                     kind: 'pack'
   51 |                 });
   52 |             },
   53 |             getItems: function(projectId) {
   54 |                 return load(projectId);
   55 |             },
   56 |             clear: function(projectId) {
   57 |                 try {
   58 |                     sessionStorage.removeItem(storageKey(projectId));
   59 |                 } catch (e3) {}
   60 |             },
   61 |             getSummaryText: function(projectId) {
   62 |                 var items = load(projectId);
   63 |                 if (!items.length) return '';
   64 |                 var lines = [];
   65 |                 var byPack = {};
   66 |                 var byId = {};
   67 |                 items.forEach(function(it) {
   68 |                     if (it && Array.isArray(it.ids) && it.ids.length) {
   69 |                         var k = it.ids.join('-');
   70 |                         byPack[k] = (byPack[k] || 0) + 1;
   71 |                     } else if (it && it.id) {
   72 |                         var id = it.id;
   73 |                         byId[id] = (byId[id] || 0) + 1;
   74 |                     }
   75 |                 });
   76 |                 Object.keys(byId).forEach(function(id) {
   77 |                     lines.push('ID ' + id + ' ×' + byId[id]);
   78 |                 });
   79 |                 Object.keys(byPack).forEach(function(k) {
   80 |                     lines.push('Pack [' + k.replace(/-/g, ', ') + '] ×' + byPack[k]);
   81 |                 });
   82 |                 return lines.length ? ('Clics de compra en esta sesión: ' + lines.join('; ')) : '';
   83 |             }
   84 |         };
   85 |     })();

   87 |     /** Código ISO 639-1 (data-lang) → BCP47 para Speech API / utterance (alineado con chatbox.php). */
   88 |     var XABIA_LANG_BCP47 = { es: 'es-ES', eu: 'eu-ES', en: 'en-US', fr: 'fr-FR', ca: 'ca-ES', gl: 'gl-ES', pt: 'pt-PT', de: 'de-DE', it: 'it-IT' };
   89 |     function xabiaBcp47FromLang(code) {
   90 |         var c = String(code || 'es').toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z]/g, '').substring(0, 2) || 'es';
   91 |         return XABIA_LANG_BCP47[c] || (c + '-' + c.toUpperCase());
   92 |     }

   94 |     function parseTtsConfig($box) {
   95 |         var raw = $box.data('tts');
   96 |         if (!raw) return { voice: 'default', rate: 1, clean: { bold: false, italic: false, actions: false, emojis: false, patterns: [] } };
   97 |         try { return typeof raw === 'string' ? JSON.parse(raw) : raw; } catch (e) { return { voice: 'default', rate: 1, clean: {} }; }
   98 |     }
   99 | 
  100 |     function cleanTextForTts(text, clean) {
  101 |         if (!text) return '';
  102 |         text = String(text).replace(/<[^>]+>/g, ' ').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
  103 |         if (!clean || typeof clean !== 'object') return text;
  104 |         if (clean.bold) text = text.replace(/\*\*/g, '');
  105 |         if (clean.italic) text = text.replace(/\*/g, '');
  106 |         if (clean.actions) text = text.replace(/\[(?:ACTION|IMAGE|CART|BOOK)[^\]]*\]/gi, ' ');
  107 |         if (clean.emojis) {
  108 |             text = text.replace(/[\u{1F000}-\u{1FAFF}\u{2600}-\u{27BF}\u{FE0F}\u{200D}]/gu, ' ');
  109 |         }
  110 |         if (Array.isArray(clean.patterns)) {
  111 |             clean.patterns.forEach(function(p) { if (p) text = text.split(p).join(' '); });
  112 |         }
  113 |         return text.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
  114 |     }
  115 | 
  116 |     function scoreVoice(voice, langCode, preference) {
  117 |         var wanted = String(langCode || 'es-ES').toLowerCase();
  118 |         var base = wanted.split('-')[0];
  119 |         var voiceLang = String(voice.lang || '').toLowerCase();
  120 |         var name = String(voice.name || '');
  121 |         var normalizedName = name.normalize ? name.normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '').toLowerCase() : name.toLowerCase();
  122 |         var score = 0;
  123 | 
  124 |         if (voiceLang === wanted) score += 120;
  125 |         else if (voiceLang.indexOf(base + '-') === 0) score += 35;
  126 |         else if (voiceLang.indexOf(base) === 0) score += 15;
  127 | 
  128 |         if (wanted === 'es-es') {
  129 |             if (/spanish \(spain\)|espanol \(espana\)|castellano|google espanol/i.test(normalizedName)) score += 35;
  130 |             if (/paulina|penelope|miguel|sabina|dalia|latino|latin|mexico|mexican|estados unidos|united states|us spanish|es-419/i.test(normalizedName) || /es-(mx|us|419|ar|cl|co|pe|ve|uy|bo|ec|gt|hn|ni|pa|pr|py|sv)/.test(voiceLang)) {
  131 |                 score -= 100;
  132 |             }
  133 |         }
  134 | 
  135 |         if (preference === 'female') {
  136 |             if (/female|woman|mujer|femenin|monica|helena|lucia|lucía|marta|marisol|elvira|laura|sara|carmen|isabel/i.test(normalizedName)) score += 130;
  137 |             if (/male|man|hombre|masculin|jorge|alvaro|alvaro|enrique|diego|pablo|carlos|juan|miguel/i.test(normalizedName)) score -= 180;
  138 |         } else if (preference === 'male') {
  139 |             if (/male|man|hombre|masculin|jorge|alvaro|alvaro|enrique|diego|pablo|carlos|juan|miguel/i.test(normalizedName)) score += 130;
  140 |             if (/female|woman|mujer|femenin|monica|helena|lucia|lucía|marta|marisol|elvira|laura|sara|carmen|isabel/i.test(normalizedName)) score -= 180;
  141 |         }
  142 | 
  143 |         return score;
  144 |     }
  145 | 
  146 |     function pickVoice(langCode, preference) {
  147 |         var list = window.speechSynthesis.getVoices();
  148 |         if (!list || !list.length) return null;
  149 |         var ranked = list.slice().sort(function(a, b) {
  150 |             return scoreVoice(b, langCode, preference) - scoreVoice(a, langCode, preference);
  151 |         });
  152 |         return ranked[0] || list[0];
  153 |     }
  154 | 
  155 |     /**
  156 |      * Convierte la respuesta del bot en HTML: enlaces, teléfono, imágenes, mapa.
  157 |      * [ACTION:CALL:num] -> enlace tel:; [ACTION:URL:url] -> enlace; [ACTION:IMG:url] -> img; [ACTION:MAP:...] -> enlace a mapa.
  158 |      */
  159 |     function renderActions(text) {
  160 |         if (!text) return '';
  161 |         text = String(text);
  162 |         function escAttr(s) {
  163 |             return String(s).replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/"/g, '&quot;').replace(/'/g, '&#39;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;');
  164 |         }
  165 |         text = text.replace(/\[ACTION:CALL:([^\]]+)\]/g, function(_, num) {
  166 |             var tel = (num || '').replace(/\s+/g, '');
  167 |             return '<a href="tel:' + escAttr(tel) + '" class="xabia-action xabia-action-call">📞 Llamar</a>';
  168 |         });
  169 |         text = text.replace(/\[ACTION:URL:([^\]]+)\]/g, function(_, url) {
  170 |             return '<a href="' + escAttr((url || '').trim()) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener" class="xabia-action xabia-action-url">🌐 Abrir enlace</a>';
  171 |         });
  172 |         text = text.replace(/\[ACTION:IMG:([^\]]+)\]/g, function(_, src) {
  173 |             var url = (src || '').trim();
  174 |             if (!url || url.indexOf(' ') !== -1) return '';
  175 |             if (/^https?:\/\//i.test(url)) {
  176 |                 return '<img src="' + escAttr(url) + '" alt="" class="xabia-action-img" loading="lazy" decoding="async">';
  177 |             }
  178 |             if (url.charAt(0) === '/' || url.indexOf('//') === 0) {
  179 |                 return '<img src="' + escAttr(url) + '" alt="" class="xabia-action-img" loading="lazy" decoding="async">';
  180 |             }
  181 |             return '';
  182 |         });
  183 |         text = text.replace(/\[ACTION:MAP:([^\]]+)\]/g, function(_, q) {
  184 |             var query = encodeURIComponent((q || '').trim());
  185 |             return '<a href="https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=' + query + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener" class="xabia-action xabia-action-map">📍 Ver en mapa</a>';
  186 |         });
  187 |         
  188 |         text = text.replace(/\[ACTION:CART:(\d+)\]/g, function(_, id) {
  189 |             var pid = String(id || '').replace(/\D/g, '');
  190 |             if (!pid) return '';
  191 |             var wc = window.xabiaWooCart || {};
  192 |             if (wc.mode === 'redirect' && wc.urlTemplate && String(wc.urlTemplate).indexOf('{id}') !== -1) {
  193 |                 var href = String(wc.urlTemplate).split('{id}').join(pid);
  194 |                 var lbl = wc.checkoutLabel || 'Comprar ahora';
  195 |                 return '<a href="' + escAttr(href) + '" class="xabia-action xabia-action-url xabia-btn-cart-remote" target="_blank" rel="noopener">🛒 ' + escAttr(lbl) + '</a>';
  196 |             }
  197 |             return '<button type="button" class="xabia-btn-cart" data-id="' + escAttr(pid) + '">🛒 Añadir al carrito</button>';
  198 |         });
  199 |         text = text.replace(/\[ACTION:CART_PACK:([^\]]+)\]/g, function(_, spec) {
  200 |             var parts = String(spec || '').trim().split('|');
  201 |             var idsPart = (parts[0] || '').trim();
  202 |             var qtyPart = (parts[1] || '').trim();
  203 |             var ids = idsPart.split(',').map(function(s) { return String(s).replace(/\D/g, ''); }).filter(Boolean);
  204 |             if (!ids.length) return '';
  205 |             var quantities = [];
  206 |             if (qtyPart) {
  207 |                 qtyPart.split(',').forEach(function(s) {
  208 |                     var n = parseInt(String(s).trim(), 10);
  209 |                     quantities.push(isNaN(n) || n < 1 ? 1 : n);
  210 |                 });
  211 |             }
  212 |             while (quantities.length < ids.length) {
  213 |                 quantities.push(1);
  214 |             }
  215 |             quantities = quantities.slice(0, ids.length);
  216 |             var wc = window.xabiaWooCart || {};
  217 |             var tpl = wc.packUrlTemplate;
  218 |             if (!tpl || String(tpl).indexOf('{ids}') === -1) {
  219 |                 return '';
  220 |             }
  221 |             var href = String(tpl).replace('{ids}', ids.join(',')).replace('{quantities}', quantities.join(','));
  222 |             var lbl = wc.packCheckoutLabel || 'Comprar pack';
  223 |             return '<a href="' + escAttr(href) + '" class="xabia-action xabia-action-url xabia-btn-cart-pack-remote" target="_blank" rel="noopener" data-pack-ids="' + escAttr(ids.join(',')) + '">🛒 ' + escAttr(lbl) + '</a>';
  224 |         });
  225 |         function xabiaDecodeUrlB64(b64) {
  226 |             try {
  227 |                 var raw = String(b64 || '');
  228 |                 var pad = raw.length % 4 ? 4 - (raw.length % 4) : 0;
  229 |                 var s = raw.replace(/-/g, '+').replace(/_/g, '/') + (pad ? '==='.slice(0, pad) : '');
  230 |                 return atob(s);
  231 |             } catch (e) { return ''; }
  232 |         }
  233 |         
  234 |         text = text.replace(/\[ACTION:BOOK:[a-z0-9_-]+:(\d+):([^\]]+)\]/gi, function(_, id, b64) {
  235 |             var href = xabiaDecodeUrlB64(b64);
  236 |             if (!href) return '';
  237 |             return '<a href="' + escAttr(href) + '" class="xabia-btn-book" data-id="' + escAttr(String(id)) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">📅 Reservar</a>';
  238 |         });
  239 |         
  240 |         text = text.replace(/\[ACTION:BOOK:amelia:(\d+)\]/g, function(_, id) {
  241 |             var sid = String(id || '').replace(/\D/g, '');
  242 |             if (!sid) return '';
  243 |             return '<button type="button" class="xabia-btn-book xabia-book-amelia" data-engine="amelia" data-service-id="' + escAttr(sid) + '">📅 Reservar</button>';
  244 |         });
  245 |         
  246 |         text = text.replace(/\[ACTION:BOOK:(\d+)\]/g, function(_, id) {
  247 |             var pid = String(id || '').replace(/\D/g, '');
  248 |             if (!pid) return '';
  249 |             var eng = (window.xabiaReservas && window.xabiaReservas.engine) ? String(window.xabiaReservas.engine) : '';
  250 |             if (eng !== 'amelia') {
  251 |                 var home = (window.xabiaReservas && window.xabiaReservas.homeUrl) ? String(window.xabiaReservas.homeUrl) : '';
  252 |                 if (!home) return '';
  253 |                 var base = home.replace(/\/$/, '');
  254 |                 var sep = base.indexOf('?') >= 0 ? '&' : '?';
  255 |                 var href = base + sep + 'p=' + pid;
  256 |                 return '<a href="' + escAttr(href) + '" class="xabia-btn-book" data-id="' + escAttr(pid) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">📅 Reservar</a>';
  257 |             }
  258 |             if (eng === 'amelia') {
  259 |                 return '<button type="button" class="xabia-btn-book xabia-book-amelia" data-engine="amelia" data-service-id="' + escAttr(pid) + '">📅 Reservar</button>';
  260 |             }
  261 |             return '';
  262 |         });
  263 |         return text;
  264 |     }

  266 |     function autoSizeInput($input) {

  268 |         var el = $input[0];

  270 |         el.style.height = Math.min(el.scrollHeight, 160) + 'px';

  274 |     function renderBotHtml(raw) {
  275 |         var html = String(raw || '').replace(/\n/g, '<br>').replace(/\*\*(.*?)\*\*/g, '<strong>$1</strong>');
  276 |         return renderActions(html);

  279 |     function appendBotMessage($box, $history, raw, opts) {
  280 |         opts = opts || {};
  281 |         var name = $box.data('avatarName') || 'Xabia';
  282 |         var $botDiv = $('<div class="xabia-msg bot xabia-msg-typing"></div>').attr('data-raw', raw);
  283 |         var $content = $('<span class="xabia-msg-content"></span>');
  284 |         $botDiv.append($('<strong>').text(name + ': ')).append($content);
  285 |         $history.append($botDiv);
  286 | 
  287 |         var reducedMotion = window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
  288 |         var text = String(raw || '');
  289 |         if (!text || reducedMotion) {
  290 |             $content.html(renderBotHtml(text));
  291 |             finishBotMessage($box, $history, $botDiv, raw, opts);
  292 |             return;
  293 |         }
  294 | 
  295 |         var i = 0;
  296 |         var chunk = text.length > 600 ? 4 : 2;
  297 |         var delay = text.length > 600 ? 8 : 14;
  298 |         function tick() {
  299 |             i = Math.min(text.length, i + chunk);
  300 |             $content.text(text.slice(0, i));
  301 |             $history.scrollTop(1e9);
  302 |             if (i < text.length) {
  303 |                 window.setTimeout(tick, delay);
  304 |                 return;
  305 |             }
  306 |             $content.html(renderBotHtml(text));
  307 |             finishBotMessage($box, $history, $botDiv, raw, opts);
  308 |         }
  309 |         tick();
  310 |     }
  311 | 
  312 |     function finishBotMessage($box, $history, $botDiv, raw, opts) {
  313 |         $botDiv.removeClass('xabia-msg-typing');
  314 |         if (opts.truncated) {
  315 |             $botDiv.append(' ').append($('<button type="button" class="xabia-continue button button-small">Continuar</button>'));
  316 |         }
  317 |         speakText($box, raw);
  318 |         $history.scrollTop(1e9);
  319 |     }
  320 | 
  321 |     function speakText($box, rawText) {
  322 |         if (!$box.data('voice') || !rawText || !window.speechSynthesis) return;
  323 |         var tts = parseTtsConfig($box);
  324 |         var text = cleanTextForTts(rawText, tts.clean);
  325 |         if (!text) return;
  326 |         if (window.speechSynthesis.getVoices().length === 0) {
  327 |             window.speechSynthesis.onvoiceschanged = function() {
  328 |                 window.speechSynthesis.onvoiceschanged = null;
  329 |                 speakText($box, rawText);
  330 |             };
  331 |             return;
  332 |         }
  333 |         window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
  334 |         var u = new window.SpeechSynthesisUtterance(text);
  335 |         u.lang = xabiaBcp47FromLang($box.data('lang'));
  336 |         u.rate = Math.max(0.5, Math.min(2, parseFloat(tts.rate) || 1));
  337 |         var chosen = pickVoice(u.lang, tts.voice);
  338 |         if (chosen) u.voice = chosen;
  339 |         window.speechSynthesis.speak(u);
  340 |     }

  343 |             var isBoxPage = document.body && document.body.classList.contains('xabia-box-page');
  344 |             var urlParams = null;
  345 |             try {
  346 |                 urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
  347 |             } catch (eP) {
  348 |                 urlParams = null;
  349 |             }

  351 |             function xabiaApplyQueryTunnelToBox($b) {
  352 |                 if (!urlParams || !$b || !$b.length) return;
  353 |                 var xp = urlParams.get('x_project');
  354 |                 var eid = urlParams.get('ente_id');
  355 |                 if (xp) {

  451 |             if (entePayload !== '') {

  464 |

  496 |                     $history.append($err);

  266 |     function autoSizeInput($input) {
  267 |         if (!$input || !$input.length) return;
  268 |         var el = $input[0];
  269 |         el.style.height = 'auto';
  270 |         el.style.height = Math.min(el.scrollHeight, 160) + 'px';
  271 |         el.style.overflowY = el.scrollHeight > 160 ? 'auto' : 'hidden';
  272 |     }
