  274 |     function renderBotHtml(raw) {
  275 |         var html = String(raw || '').replace(/\n/g, '<br>').replace(/\*\*(.*?)\*\*/g, '<strong>$1</strong>');
  276 |         return renderActions(html);
  277 |     }
  278 | 
  279 |     function appendBotMessage($box, $history, raw, opts) {
  280 |         opts = opts || {};
  281 |         var name = $box.data('avatarName') || 'Xabia';
  282 |         var $botDiv = $('<div class="xabia-msg bot xabia-msg-typing"></div>').attr('data-raw', raw);
  283 |         var $content = $('<span class="xabia-msg-content"></span>');
  284 |         $botDiv.append($('<strong>').text(name + ': ')).append($content);
  285 |         $history.append($botDiv);
  286 | 
  287 |         var reducedMotion = window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
  288 |         var text = String(raw || '');
  289 |         if (!text || reducedMotion) {
  290 |             $content.html(renderBotHtml(text));
  291 |             finishBotMessage($box, $history, $botDiv, raw, opts);
  292 |             return;
  293 |         }
  294 | 
  295 |         var i = 0;
  296 |         var chunk = text.length > 600 ? 4 : 2;
  297 |         var delay = text.length > 600 ? 8 : 14;
  298 |         function tick() {
  299 |             i = Math.min(text.length, i + chunk);
  300 |             $content.text(text.slice(0, i));
  301 |             $history.scrollTop(1e9);
  302 |             if (i < text.length) {
  303 |                 window.setTimeout(tick, delay);
  304 |                 return;
  305 |             }
  306 |             $content.html(renderBotHtml(text));
  307 |             finishBotMessage($box, $history, $botDiv, raw, opts);
  308 |         }
  309 |         tick();
  310 |     }
  311 | 
  312 |     function finishBotMessage($box, $history, $botDiv, raw, opts) {
  313 |         $botDiv.removeClass('xabia-msg-typing');
  314 |         if (opts.truncated) {
  315 |             $botDiv.append(' ').append($('<button type="button" class="xabia-continue button button-small">Continuar</button>'));
  316 |         }
  317 |         speakText($box, raw);
  318 |         $history.scrollTop(1e9);
  319 |     }
  320 | 
  321 |     function speakText($box, rawText) {
  322 |         if (!$box.data('voice') || !rawText || !window.speechSynthesis) return;
  323 |         var tts = parseTtsConfig($box);
  324 |         var text = cleanTextForTts(rawText, tts.clean);
  325 |         if (!text) return;
  326 |         if (window.speechSynthesis.getVoices().length === 0) {
  327 |             window.speechSynthesis.onvoiceschanged = function() {
  328 |                 window.speechSynthesis.onvoiceschanged = null;
  329 |                 speakText($box, rawText);
  330 |             };
  331 |             return;
  332 |         }
  333 |         window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
  334 |         var u = new window.SpeechSynthesisUtterance(text);
  335 |         u.lang = xabiaBcp47FromLang($box.data('lang'));
  336 |         u.rate = Math.max(0.5, Math.min(2, parseFloat(tts.rate) || 1));
  337 |         var chosen = pickVoice(u.lang, tts.voice);
  338 |         if (chosen) u.voice = chosen;
  339 |         window.speechSynthesis.speak(u);
  340 |     }

  607 |         /**
  608 |          * Modo tótem: inactividad → xabia_clear_session (servidor) y UI al saludo inicial.
  609 |          */
  610 |         function initXabiaTotem($box) {
  611 |             var min = parseInt($box.attr('data-totem-minutes'), 10);
  612 |             if (!min || min <= 0) {
  613 |                 return;
  614 |             }
  615 | 
  616 |             var ms = min * 60 * 1000;
  617 |             var warnAt = Math.max(0, ms - 10000);
  618 |             var endpoint = $box.data('endpoint');
  619 |             var projectId = $box.data('project');
  620 |             var storageKey = 'xabia_totem_' + projectId;
  621 | 
  622 |             var timerId = null;
  623 |             var warnId = null;
  624 | 
  625 |             function parseReset() {
  626 |                 var raw = $box.attr('data-totem-reset');
  627 |                 if (!raw) {
  628 |                     return null;
  629 |                 }
  630 |                 try {
  631 |                     return JSON.parse(raw);
  632 |                 } catch (e2) {
  633 |                     return null;
  634 |                 }
  635 |             }
  636 | 
  637 |             function resetTimers() {
  638 |                 if (timerId) {
  639 |                     clearTimeout(timerId);
  640 |                     timerId = null;
  641 |                 }
  642 |                 if (warnId) {
  643 |                     clearTimeout(warnId);
  644 |                     warnId = null;
  645 |                 }
  646 |                 $box.find('.xabia-totem-warning').hide().empty();
  647 |             }
  648 | 
  649 |             function applyResetHtml() {
  650 |                 var o = parseReset();
  651 |                 var $hist = $box.find('.xabia-chat-history');
  652 |                 if (o && o.greeting_html !== undefined && o.greeting_html !== null) {
  653 |                     var safeName = $('<div/>').text(o.avatar || 'Xabia').html();
  654 |                     $hist.html('<div class="xabia-msg bot"><strong>' + safeName + ':</strong> ' + String(o.greeting_html) + '</div>');
  655 |                 } else {
  656 |                     $hist.find('.xabia-msg').slice(1).remove();
  657 |                 }
  658 |             }
  659 | 
  660 |             function onTimeout() {
  661 |                 resetTimers();
  662 |                 $.post(endpoint, { action: 'xabia_clear_session', project_id: projectId }).always(function() {
  663 |                     try {
  664 |                         sessionStorage.removeItem(storageKey);
  665 |                         localStorage.removeItem(storageKey);
  666 |                         if (window.xabiaWooCartIntent) {
  667 |                             window.xabiaWooCartIntent.clear(projectId);
  668 |                         }
  669 |                     } catch (err) {}
  670 |                     applyResetHtml();
  671 |                 });
  672 |             }
  673 | 
  674 |             function arm() {
  675 |                 resetTimers();
  676 |                 if (warnAt < ms) {
  677 |                     warnId = setTimeout(function() {
  678 |                         $box.find('.xabia-totem-warning').text('La sesión se cerrará pronto por inactividad.').show();
  679 |                     }, warnAt);
  680 |                 }
  681 |                 timerId = setTimeout(onTimeout, ms);
  682 |             }
  683 | 
  684 |             $box.on('click.xabiaTotem mousedown.xabiaTotem touchstart.xabiaTotem', function() {
  685 |                 arm();
  686 |             });
  687 |             $box.find('.xabia-input-field').on('focus.xabiaTotem input.xabiaTotem keydown.xabiaTotem', function() {
  688 |                 arm();
  689 |             });
  690 | 
  691 |             arm();
  692 |         }
  693 | 
  694 |         function tryAddonAutoMessage($box) {
  695 |             var raw = $box.attr('data-qr-auto');
  696 |             if (!raw) {
  697 |                 return;
  698 |             }
  699 |             var o;
  700 |             try {
  701 |                 o = JSON.parse(raw);
  702 |             } catch (e0) {
  703 |                 return;
  704 |             }
  705 |             if (!o || !o.greeting) {
  706 |                 return;
  707 |             }
  708 |             var qrId = String(o.qr_id || '');
  709 |             var key = 'xabia_addon_auto_' + encodeURIComponent(qrId || '1');
  710 |             try {
  711 |                 if (sessionStorage.getItem(key)) {
  712 |                     return;
  713 |                 }
  714 |             } catch (e1) {}
  715 |             if ($box[0] && typeof $box[0].scrollIntoView === 'function') {
  716 |                 $box[0].scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'nearest' });
  717 |             }
  718 |             $box.find('.xabia-input-field').val(String(o.greeting));
  719 |             try {
  720 |                 sessionStorage.setItem(key, '1');
  721 |             } catch (e2) {}
  722 |             $box.find('.xabia-send').trigger('click');
  723 |         }

   53 |         /**
   54 |          * Resuelve rutas de imagen (relativas o solo nombre) a URLs en wp-content/uploads.
   55 |          * Acepta path (uno) o paths[] (lote). Devuelve { urls: { path: url } }.
   56 |          * Rutas con '/' → baseurl + path. Solo nombre → búsqueda en biblioteca de medios (una query por lote).
   57 |          */
   58 |         public static function handle_resolve_image() {
   59 |             $paths = [];
   60 |             if (isset($_REQUEST['paths']) && is_array($_REQUEST['paths'])) {
   61 |                 $paths = array_map(function ($p) {
   62 |                     return self::sanitize_image_path($p);
   63 |                 }, wp_unslash($_REQUEST['paths']));
   64 |             } elseif (isset($_REQUEST['path'])) {
   65 |                 $one = self::sanitize_image_path(wp_unslash($_REQUEST['path']));
   66 |                 if ($one !== '') {
   67 |                     $paths = [$one];
   68 |                 }
   69 |             }
   70 |             $paths = array_unique(array_filter($paths, 'strlen'));
   71 |             $paths = array_slice($paths, 0, 100); 
   72 |             if (empty($paths)) {
   73 |                 wp_send_json_success(['urls' => (object) []]);
   74 |                 return;
   75 |             }
   76 | 
   77 |             $uploads = wp_upload_dir();
   78 |             $baseurl = isset($uploads['baseurl']) ? rtrim($uploads['baseurl'], '/') . '/' : '';
   79 |             $urls = self::resolve_image_paths_batch($paths, $baseurl);
   80 |             wp_send_json_success(['urls' => $urls]);
   81 |         }
   82 | 
   83 |         /**
   84 |          * Sanitiza una ruta de imagen: sin '..', sin protocolo, solo caracteres seguros.
   85 |          */
   86 |         private static function sanitize_image_path($path) {
   87 |             $path = sanitize_text_field(is_string($path) ? $path : '');
   88 |             $path = trim($path, "/ \t\n\r");
   89 |             if ($path === '' || strpos($path, '..') !== false || preg_match('#^https?://#i', $path)) {
   90 |                 return '';
   91 |             }
   92 |             return $path;
   93 |         }
   94 | 
   95 |         /**
   96 |          * Resuelve un lote de paths a URLs. Una sola query para todos los "solo nombre de archivo".
   97 |          */
   98 |         private static function resolve_image_paths_batch(array $paths, $baseurl) {
   99 |             $urls = [];
  100 |             $filenames_only = [];
  101 | 
  102 |             foreach ($paths as $path) {
  103 |                 if (strpos($path, '/') !== false) {
  104 |                     $urls[$path] = $baseurl . $path;
  105 |                 } else {
  106 |                     $filenames_only[] = $path;
  107 |                     $urls[$path] = $baseurl . $path; 
  108 |                 }
  109 |             }
  110 | 
  111 |             if (empty($filenames_only)) {
  112 |                 return $urls;
  113 |             }
  114 | 
  115 |             global $wpdb;
  116 |             $meta_table = $wpdb->postmeta;
  117 |             $posts_table = $wpdb->posts;
  118 | 
  119 |             
  120 |             $placeholders = [];
  121 |             $values = [];
  122 |             foreach ($filenames_only as $name) {
  123 |                 $placeholders[] = '(m.meta_value = %s OR m.meta_value LIKE %s)';
  124 |                 $values[] = $name;
  125 |                 $values[] = '%/' . $wpdb->esc_like($name);
  126 |             }
  127 |             $where = implode(' OR ', $placeholders);
  128 |             $sql = "SELECT m.meta_value AS file_path, p.ID
  129 |                     FROM {$meta_table} m
  130 |                     INNER JOIN {$posts_table} p ON p.ID = m.post_id AND p.post_type = 'attachment'
  131 |                     WHERE m.meta_key = '_wp_attached_file' AND ({$where})
  132 |                     ORDER BY p.ID DESC";
  133 | 
  134 |             $rows = $wpdb->get_results($wpdb->prepare($sql, $values), ARRAY_A);
  135 |             $seen = [];
  136 |             foreach ($rows as $row) {
  137 |                 $file_path = $row['file_path'];
  138 |                 $basename = basename($file_path);
  139 |                 if (!isset($seen[$basename])) {
  140 |                     $seen[$basename] = true;
  141 |                     $url = wp_get_attachment_url((int) $row['ID']);
  142 |                     if ($url && isset($urls[$basename])) {
  143 |                         $urls[$basename] = $url;
  144 |                     }
  145 |                 }
  146 |             }
  147 |             
  148 |             foreach ($filenames_only as $name) {
  149 |                 if (isset($seen[$name])) {
  150 |                     continue;
  151 |                 }
  152 |                 $id = $wpdb->get_var($wpdb->prepare(
  153 |                     "SELECT post_id FROM {$meta_table} WHERE meta_key = '_wp_attached_file' AND meta_value = %s LIMIT 1",
  154 |                     $name
  155 |                 ));
  156 |                 if ($id) {
  157 |                     $url = wp_get_attachment_url((int) $id);
  158 |                     if ($url) {
  159 |                         $urls[$name] = $url;
  160 |                     }
  161 |                 }
  162 |             }
  163 | 
  164 |             return $urls;
  165 |         }
  166 | 
  167 |         /**
  168 |          * Resuelve una pista textual (p. ej. "Garaixe", nombre de casa) a URL de imagen.
  169 |          * Orden: filtro xabia_resolve_img_hint → adjunto por título → miniatura de entrada publicada → nombre de archivo en uploads.
  170 |          *
  171 |          * @param string $hint Texto dentro de [ACTION:IMG:…] que no es ID ni URL.
  172 |          */
  173 |         private static function resolve_img_hint_to_url($hint) {
  174 |             $hint = trim((string) $hint);
  175 |             if ($hint === '' || strlen($hint) > 120) {
  176 |                 return '';
  177 |             }
  178 |             global $wpdb;
  179 |             $like = '%' . $wpdb->esc_like($hint) . '%';
  180 | 
  181 |             $aid = $wpdb->get_var($wpdb->prepare(
  182 |                 "SELECT ID FROM {$wpdb->posts} WHERE post_type = 'attachment' AND post_status = 'inherit'
  183 |                  AND post_title LIKE %s ORDER BY ID DESC LIMIT 1",
  184 |                 $like
  185 |             ));
  186 |             if ($aid) {
  187 |                 $u = wp_get_attachment_url((int) $aid);
  188 |                 if (is_string($u) && $u !== '') {
  189 |                     return $u;
  190 |                 }
  191 |             }
  192 | 
  193 |             $thumb = $wpdb->get_var($wpdb->prepare(
  194 |                 "SELECT pm.meta_value FROM {$wpdb->posts} p
  195 |                  INNER JOIN {$wpdb->postmeta} pm ON pm.post_id = p.ID AND pm.meta_key = '_thumbnail_id'
  196 |                  WHERE p.post_status = 'publish' AND p.post_title LIKE %s
  197 |                  ORDER BY p.post_modified DESC LIMIT 1",
  198 |                 $like
  199 |             ));
  200 |             if ($thumb) {
  201 |                 $u = wp_get_attachment_url((int) $thumb);
  202 |                 if (is_string($u) && $u !== '') {
  203 |                     return $u;
  204 |                 }
  205 |             }
  206 | 
  207 |             $uploads = wp_upload_dir();
  208 |             $baseurl = isset($uploads['baseurl']) ? rtrim((string) $uploads['baseurl'], '/') . '/' : '';
  209 |             if ($baseurl !== '') {
  210 |                 $slug = sanitize_file_name($hint);
  211 |                 if ($slug !== '') {
  212 |                     foreach (['jpg', 'jpeg', 'png', 'webp', 'gif'] as $ext) {
  213 |                         $try = $slug . '.' . $ext;
  214 |                         $batch = self::resolve_image_paths_batch([$try], $baseurl);
  215 |                         if (!empty($batch[$try]) && preg_match('#^https?://#i', (string) $batch[$try])) {
  216 |                             return (string) $batch[$try];
  217 |                         }
  218 |                     }
  219 |                 }
  220 |             }
  221 | 
  222 |             return '';
  223 |         }

  225 |         /**

  229 |         public static function handle_clear_session() {
  230 |             $project_id = sanitize_text_field($_POST['project_id'] ?? '');
  231 |             if (empty($project_id)) {
  232 |                 wp_send_json_success(['ok' => true]);
  233 |                 return;
  234 |             }
  235 |             if (!session_id() && !headers_sent()) {
  236 |                 session_start();
  237 |             }
  238 |             if (isset($_SESSION['xabia_chat_history'][$project_id])) {
  239 |                 unset($_SESSION['xabia_chat_history'][$project_id]);
  240 |             }
  241 |             if (isset($_SESSION['xabia_last_search'][$project_id])) {
  242 |                 unset($_SESSION['xabia_last_search'][$project_id]);
  243 |             }
  244 |             wp_send_json_success(['ok' => true]);
  245 |         }

  247 |         private static function daily_token_limit_for_project(array $config): int {
  248 |             $limit = isset($config['rules']['daily_token_limit']) ? (int) $config['rules']['daily_token_limit'] : 0;
  249 |             if ($limit < 0) {
  250 |                 $limit = 0;
  251 |             }
  252 |             if ($limit === 0) {
  253 |                 $limit = (int) apply_filters('xabia_daily_token_limit_default', 20000, $config);
  254 |             }

  258 |         private static function consumed_tokens_today(string $project_id): int {
  259 |             global $wpdb;
  260 |             $table = Xabia_DB::table('usage_logs');
  261 |             if ($wpdb->get_var("SHOW TABLES LIKE '$table'") !== $table) {
  262 |                 return 0;
  263 |             }
  264 |             $start = gmdate('Y-m-d 00:00:00');
  265 |             $end = gmdate('Y-m-d 23:59:59');
  266 |             $sum = $wpdb->get_var($wpdb->prepare(
  267 |                 "SELECT COALESCE(SUM(tokens_input + tokens_output),0) FROM $table WHERE project_id = %s AND created_at BETWEEN %s AND %s",
  268 |                 $project_id,
  269 |                 $start,
  270 |                 $end
  271 |             ));
  272 |             return (int) $sum;
  273 |         }
  274 | 
  275 |         private static function estimate_cost_usd(string $model, int $in, int $out): float {
  276 |             $m = strtolower($model);
  277 |             
  278 |             $in_rate = 0.00015;
  279 |             $out_rate = 0.00060;
  280 |             if (str_contains($m, 'mini') || str_contains($m, 'flash')) {
  281 |                 $in_rate = 0.00005;
  282 |                 $out_rate = 0.00020;
  283 |             }
  284 |             $in_rate = (float) apply_filters('xabia_model_input_rate_per_1k', $in_rate, $model);
  285 |             $out_rate = (float) apply_filters('xabia_model_output_rate_per_1k', $out_rate, $model);
  286 |             return (($in / 1000) * $in_rate) + (($out / 1000) * $out_rate);
  287 |         }
  288 | 
  289 |         private static function log_usage_metrics(string $project_id, string $user_message = ''): void {
  290 |             if (!is_array(self::$last_generation_metrics)) {
  291 |                 return;
  292 |             }
  293 |             global $wpdb;
  294 |             $table = Xabia_DB::table('usage_logs');
  295 |             if ($wpdb->get_var("SHOW TABLES LIKE '$table'") !== $table) {
  296 |                 return;
  297 |             }
  298 |             $sensitive = class_exists('Xabia_Router') ? Xabia_Router::detect_sensitive($user_message) : false;
  299 |             $fingerprint = hash('sha256', strtolower(trim($user_message)));
  300 |             $tokens_input = (int) (self::$last_generation_metrics['prompt_tokens'] ?? 0);
  301 |             $tokens_output = (int) (self::$last_generation_metrics['completion_tokens'] ?? 0);
  302 |             $tokens_count = $tokens_input + $tokens_output;
  303 |             $wpdb->insert($table, [
  304 |                 'project_id'     => $project_id,
  305 |                 'model_used'     => (string) (self::$last_generation_metrics['model'] ?? 'unknown'),
  306 |                 'tokens_input'   => $tokens_input,
  307 |                 'tokens_output'  => $tokens_output,

  340 |         private static function maybe_summarize_history(array $history, string $project_id, array $config): array {
  341 |             if (count($history) <= 10) {
  342 |                 return $history;
  343 |             }
  344 |             $slice = array_slice($history, -10);
  345 |             $lines = [];
  346 |             foreach ($slice as $m) {
  347 |                 if (!is_array($m)) {
  348 |                     continue;
  349 |                 }
  350 |                 $r = ($m['role'] ?? '') === 'assistant' ? 'Asistente' : 'Usuario';
  351 |                 $c = trim((string) ($m['content'] ?? ''));
  352 |                 if ($c === '') {
  353 |                     continue;
  354 |                 }
  355 |                 $lines[] = $r . ': ' . substr($c, 0, 220);
  356 |             }
  357 |             if ($lines === []) {
  358 |                 return array_slice($history, -6);
  359 |             }
  360 |             $summary_prompt = [
  361 |                 ['role' => 'system', 'content' => 'Resume en exactamente 2 líneas los acuerdos y contexto útil de la conversación. No inventes datos.'],
  362 |                 ['role' => 'user', 'content' => implode("\n", $lines)],
  363 |             ];
  364 |             $summary = self::call_openai($summary_prompt, 120, 'gpt-4o-mini', 0.1, $project_id, $config);
  365 |             if (!is_string($summary) || trim($summary) === '') {
  366 |                 return array_slice($history, -6);
  367 |             }
  368 |             return array_merge([
  369 |                 ['role' => 'system', 'content' => 'Resumen de contexto previo: ' . trim($summary)],
  370 |             ], array_slice($history, -6));
  371 |         }

  373 |         private static function normalize_history_messages($raw): array {
  374 |             if (!is_array($raw)) {
  375 |                 return [];
  376 |             }
  377 |             $out = [];
  378 |             foreach ($raw as $m) {
  379 |                 if (!is_array($m) || !isset($m['role'], $m['content'])) {
  380 |                     continue;
  381 |                 }
  382 |                 $role = (string) $m['role'];
  383 |                 if (!in_array($role, ['user', 'assistant', 'system'], true)) {
  384 |                     continue;
  385 |                 }
  386 |                 $content = trim(wp_strip_all_tags((string) $m['content']));
  387 |                 if ($content === '') {
  388 |                     continue;
  389 |                 }
  390 |                 $out[] = ['role' => $role, 'content' => $content];
  391 |             }
  392 |             return $out;
  393 |         }
  394 | 
  395 |         private static function merge_history_messages(array $session_history, array $posted_history): array {
  396 |             $merged = array_merge(
  397 |                 self::normalize_history_messages($session_history),
  398 |                 self::normalize_history_messages($posted_history)
  399 |             );
  400 |             $deduped = [];
  401 |             foreach ($merged as $msg) {
  402 |                 $last = end($deduped);
  403 |                 if (is_array($last) && ($last['role'] ?? '') === $msg['role'] && ($last['content'] ?? '') === $msg['content']) {
  404 |                     continue;
  405 |                 }
  406 |                 $deduped[] = $msg;
  407 |             }
  408 |             return array_slice($deduped, -12);
  409 |         }

  411 |         private static function chat_max_tokens($max_tokens): int {
  412 |             $n = (int) $max_tokens;
  413 |             if ($n > 3000) {
  414 |                 return 3000;
  415 |             }
  416 |             return max(800, $n);
  417 |         }

  422 |         }

 2045 |                 return $declarations;

 2056 |                 if ($name === '') {
 2057 |                     continue;
 2058 |                 }

 2169 |         /**
 2170 |          * Bucle Vertex: functionCall → xabia_federation_ask_node → functionResponse → siguiente generateContent.
 2171 |          *
 2172 |          * @param array<int, array<string, mixed>> $messages
 2173 |          */
 2174 |         private static function call_google_vertex_federation_tool_loop(array $messages, int $max_tokens, array $config, float $temperature, string $wp_project_id) {
 2175 |             self::$last_generation_finish_reason = '';
 2176 |             $max_tokens = self::chat_max_tokens($max_tokens);
 2177 |             $auth = self::get_google_vertex_auth($config);
 2178 |             if ($auth === null) {
 2179 |                 return 'Error: credenciales Vertex no disponibles (JSON o vendor).';
 2180 |             }
 2181 | 
 2182 |             $msgs = array_values($messages);
 2183 |             $conscious = class_exists('Xabia_Federation_Nexus', false) ? Xabia_Federation_Nexus::federation_vertex_consciousness_block() : '';
 2184 |             $instr = class_exists('Xabia_Federation_Nexus', false) ? Xabia_Federation_Nexus::federation_tool_instruction_block() : '';
 2185 |             $extra = [];
 2186 |             if ($conscious !== '') {
 2187 |                 $extra[] = $conscious;
 2188 |             }
 2189 |             if ($instr !== '') {
 2190 |                 $extra[] = $instr;
 2191 |             }
 2192 |             $extra_s = implode("\n\n", array_filter($extra, 'strlen'));
 2193 |             if ($extra_s !== '') {
 2194 |                 $system_i = null;
 2195 |                 foreach ($msgs as $i => $m) {
 2196 |                     if (is_array($m) && (($m['role'] ?? '') === 'system')) {
 2197 |                         $system_i = $i;
 2198 |                         break;
 2199 |                     }
 2200 |                 }
 2201 |                 if ($system_i !== null) {
 2202 |                     $msgs[$system_i]['content'] = trim((string) ($msgs[$system_i]['content'] ?? '')) . "\n\n" . $extra_s;
 2203 |                 } else {
 2204 |                     array_unshift($msgs, ['role' => 'system', 'content' => $extra_s]);
 2205 |                 }
 2206 |             }

    8 | class Xabia_Wallet_API {
    9 |     public static function init(): void {
   10 |         add_action('rest_api_init', [self::class, 'register_routes']);
   11 |     }
   12 | 
   13 |     public static function register_routes(): void {
   14 |         if (!self::legacy_bridge_enabled()) {
   15 |             return;
   16 |         }
   17 |         register_rest_route('xabia/v1', '/wallet/recharge', [
   18 |             'methods' => 'POST',
   19 |             'callback' => [self::class, 'handle_recharge'],
   20 |             'permission_callback' => '__return_true',
   21 |             'args' => [
   22 |                 'license_key' => ['required' => true],
   23 |                 'token_amount' => ['required' => true],
   24 |                 'timestamp' => ['required' => true],
   25 |                 'signature' => ['required' => true],
   26 |             ],
   27 |         ]);
   28 |     }
   29 | 
   30 |     private static function legacy_bridge_enabled(): bool {
   31 |         /**
   32 |          * Keep disabled by default to enforce centralized top-ups in hub webhook pipeline.
   33 |          */
   34 |         return (bool) apply_filters('xabia_legacy_wallet_recharge_bridge_enabled', false);
   35 |     }
   36 | 
   37 |     /**
   38 |      * @param WP_REST_Request $request
   39 |      * @return WP_REST_Response
   40 |      */
   41 |     public static function handle_recharge($request) {
   42 |         $licenseKey = trim((string) $request->get_param('license_key'));
   43 |         $tokenAmount = (int) $request->get_param('token_amount');
   44 |         $timestamp = trim((string) $request->get_param('timestamp'));
   45 |         $signature = trim((string) $request->get_param('signature'));
   46 | 
   47 |         if ($licenseKey === '' || $tokenAmount < 1 || $timestamp === '' || $signature === '') {
   48 |             return new WP_REST_Response([
   49 |                 'success' => false,
   50 |                 'message' => 'Missing or invalid recharge parameters.',
   51 |             ], 400);
   52 |         }
   53 | 
   54 |         $configuredLicense = trim((string) get_option('xabia_digixop_license_key', ''));
   55 |         if ($configuredLicense === '' || !hash_equals($configuredLicense, $licenseKey)) {
   56 |             return new WP_REST_Response([
   57 |                 'success' => false,
   58 |                 'message' => 'Forbidden.',
   59 |             ], 403);
   60 |         }
   61 | 
   62 |         $ts = is_numeric($timestamp) ? (int) $timestamp : 0;
   63 |         if ($ts < 1 || abs(time() - $ts) > 15 * MINUTE_IN_SECONDS) {
   64 |             return new WP_REST_Response([
   65 |                 'success' => false,
   66 |                 'message' => 'Invalid or expired timestamp.',
   67 |             ], 403);
   68 |         }
   69 | 
   70 |         $expected = hash_hmac('sha256', $licenseKey . $tokenAmount . $timestamp, $licenseKey);
   71 |         if (!hash_equals($expected, $signature)) {
   72 |             return new WP_REST_Response([
   73 |                 'success' => false,
   74 |                 'message' => 'Forbidden.',
   75 |             ], 403);
   76 |         }
   77 |         $signatureHash = hash('sha256', $signature);
   78 | 
   79 |         global $wpdb;
   80 |         Xabia_DB::install_tables();
   81 |         $walletTable = Xabia_DB::table('wallets');
   82 |         $historyTable = Xabia_DB::table('recharge_history');
   83 |         $licenseId = Xabia_DB::wallet_license_id_for_key($licenseKey);
   84 |         $licenseHash = hash('sha256', $licenseKey);
   85 |         $now = gmdate('Y-m-d H:i:s');
   86 |         self::cleanup_old_signature_hashes($historyTable);
   87 | 
   88 |         $alreadyProcessed = $wpdb->get_var($wpdb->prepare(
   89 |             "SELECT id FROM $historyTable WHERE signature_hash = %s LIMIT 1",
   90 |             $signatureHash
   91 |         ));
   92 |         if ($alreadyProcessed) {
   93 |             $currentBalance = (int) $wpdb->get_var($wpdb->prepare(
   94 |                 "SELECT tokens_remaining FROM $walletTable WHERE license_id = %s",
   95 |                 $licenseId
   96 |             ));
   97 |             return new WP_REST_Response([
   98 |                 'success' => true,
   99 |                 'message' => 'Recharge already processed.',
  100 |                 'license_id' => $licenseId,
  101 |                 'tokens_added' => 0,
  102 |                 'new_balance' => $currentBalance,
  103 |                 'duplicate' => true,
  104 |             ], 200);
  105 |         }
  106 | 
  107 |         $row = $wpdb->get_row($wpdb->prepare(
  108 |             "SELECT tokens_remaining FROM $walletTable WHERE license_id = %s",
  109 |             $licenseId
  110 |         ), ARRAY_A);
  111 |         if (!is_array($row)) {
  112 |             $wpdb->insert($walletTable, [
  113 |                 'license_id' => $licenseId,
  114 |                 'license_key_hash' => $licenseHash,
  115 |                 'tokens_remaining' => 0,
  116 |                 'tokens_used_total' => 0,
  117 |                 'updated_at' => $now,
  118 |             ]);
  119 |         }
  120 | 
  121 |         $updated = $wpdb->query($wpdb->prepare(
  122 |             "UPDATE $walletTable
  123 |              SET tokens_remaining = tokens_remaining + %d,
  124 |                  license_key_hash = %s,
  125 |                  updated_at = %s
  126 |              WHERE license_id = %s",
  127 |             $tokenAmount,
  128 |             $licenseHash,
  129 |             $now,
  130 |             $licenseId
  131 |         ));
  132 |         if ($updated === false) {
  133 |             return new WP_REST_Response([
  134 |                 'success' => false,
  135 |                 'message' => 'Could not update wallet balance.',
  136 |             ], 500);
  137 |         }
  138 | 
  139 |         $newBalance = (int) $wpdb->get_var($wpdb->prepare(
  140 |             "SELECT tokens_remaining FROM $walletTable WHERE license_id = %s",
  141 |             $licenseId
  142 |         ));
  143 |         $source = isset($_SERVER['REMOTE_ADDR']) ? 'api_recharge:' . sanitize_text_field((string) $_SERVER['REMOTE_ADDR']) : 'api_recharge';
  144 |         $wpdb->insert($historyTable, [
  145 |             'license_id' => $licenseId,
  146 |             'license_key_hash' => $licenseHash,
  147 |             'amount' => $tokenAmount,

  152 |         ]);

  155 |             Xabia_Digixop_Client::merge_license_meta_from_api_payload([
  156 |                 'tokens_remaining' => $newBalance,
  157 |             ]);
  158 |         }
  159 | 
  160 |         return new WP_REST_Response([
  161 |             'success' => true,
  162 |             'message' => 'Recharge completed.',
  163 |             'license_id' => $licenseId,
  164 |             'tokens_added' => $tokenAmount,
  165 |             'new_balance' => $newBalance,
  166 |         ], 200);
  167 |     }
  168 | 
  169 |     private static function cleanup_old_signature_hashes(string $historyTable): void {

    1 | <?php
    2 | 
    3 | declare(strict_types=1);
    4 | 
    5 | /**
    6 |  * Arranque del hub central Xabia (fuera de WordPress).
    7 |  */
    8 | 
    9 | $centralRoot = __DIR__;
   10 | 
   11 | spl_autoload_register(static function (string $class): void {
   12 |     $prefix = 'XabiaCentral\\';
   13 |     if (strncmp($class, $prefix, strlen($prefix)) !== 0) {
   14 |         return;
   15 |     }
   16 |     $rel = substr($class, strlen($prefix));
   17 |     $path = __DIR__ . '/src/' . str_replace('\\', '/', $rel) . '.php';
   18 |     if (is_readable($path)) {
   19 |         require $path;
   20 |     }
   21 | });
   22 | 
   23 | /** Carga .env desde central-api/.env (KEY=valor, sin comillas obligatorias) */
   24 | $envFile = $centralRoot . '/.env';
   25 | if (is_readable($envFile)) {
   26 |     foreach (file($envFile, FILE_IGNORE_NEW_LINES | FILE_SKIP_EMPTY_LINES) ?: [] as $line) {
   27 |         $line = trim($line);
   28 |         if ($line === '' || str_starts_with($line, '#')) {
   29 |             continue;
   30 |         }
   31 |         if (!str_contains($line, '=')) {
   32 |             continue;
   33 |         }
   34 |         [$k, $v] = explode('=', $line, 2);
   35 |         $k = trim($k);
   36 |         $v = trim($v);
   37 |         if ($k !== '' && getenv($k) === false) {
   38 |             putenv(sprintf('%s=%s', $k, $v));
   39 |             $_ENV[$k] = $v;
   40 |         }
   41 |     }
   42 | }

    7 | final class Router
    8 | {
    9 |     /**
   10 |      * Resuelve la ruta interna (/xabia/v1/…) ignorando el prefijo público (Slim setBasePath,
   11 |      * subcarpeta public/, o URL limpia sin index.php).
   12 |      */
   13 |     public static function dispatch(): void
   14 |     {
   15 |         try {
   16 |             $path = self::normalizeRoutePath();
   17 | 
   18 |             if (self::isLicenseValidatePath($path)) {
   19 |                 LicenseValidateHandler::handle();
   20 | 
   21 |                 return;
   22 |             }
   23 |             if ($path === '/xabia/v1/proxy') {
   24 |                 ProxyHandler::handle();
   25 | 
   26 |                 return;
   27 |             }
   28 |             if ($path === '/xabia/v1/usage') {
   29 |                 UsageReportHandler::handle();
   30 | 
   31 |                 return;
   32 |             }
   33 |             if ($path === '/xabia/v1/webhooks/polar') {
   34 |                 Handlers\PolarWebhookHandler::handle();
   35 | 
   36 |                 return;
   37 |             }
   38 |             if ($path === '/xabia/v1/knowledge/sync' || $path === '/v1/knowledge/sync') {
   39 |                 KnowledgeSyncHandler::handle();
   40 | 
   41 |                 return;
   42 |             }
   43 |             if ($path === '/xabia/v1/knowledge/search' || $path === '/v1/knowledge/search') {
   44 |                 KnowledgeSearchHandler::handle();
   45 | 
   46 |                 return;
   47 |             }
   48 | 
   49 |             Json::respond(404, ['error' => ['message' => 'Not Found', 'path' => $path]]);
   50 |         } catch (\Throwable $e) {
   51 |             Json::respond(500, [
   52 |                 'error' => [
   53 |                     'message' => 'Error interno del hub',
   54 |                     'type'    => 'xabia_hub_exception',
   55 |                     'detail'  => Env::str('XABIA_DEBUG') !== '' ? $e->getMessage() : null,
   56 |                 ],
   57 |             ]);
   58 |         }
   59 |     }

   61 |     /**
   62 |      * Rutas que disparan la validación de licencia (mismo handler).
   63 |      * Canónica: POST/GET /xabia/v1/license/validate
   64 |      * Alias: /license/check, /xabia/v1/license/check (compat. clientes que usen path corto).
   65 |      *
   66 |      * @return list<string>
   67 |      */
   68 |     private static function licenseValidatePaths(): array
   69 |     {
   70 |         return [
   71 |             '/xabia/v1/license/validate',
   72 |             '/xabia/v1/license/check',

   82 | 
   83 |     /**
   84 |      * Equivale a ignorar el BasePath del front controller (p. ej. Slim $app->setBasePath('/api-xabia-saas')
   85 |      * o /api-xabia-saas/public) y /index.php cuando la URL lo incluye.
   86 |      */
   87 |     private static function normalizeRoutePath(): string
   88 |     {
   89 |         $raw = parse_url($_SERVER['REQUEST_URI'] ?? '/', PHP_URL_PATH);
   90 |         $path = is_string($raw) && $raw !== '' ? $raw : '/';
   91 |         $path = '/' . trim($path, '/');
   92 | 
   93 |         $envBase = Env::str('XABIA_PUBLIC_BASE');
   94 |         if ($envBase !== '') {
   95 |             $b = rtrim($envBase, '/');
   96 |             if ($b !== '' && ($path === $b || str_starts_with($path, $b . '/'))) {
   97 |                 $path = $path === $b ? '/' : substr($path, strlen($b));
   98 |                 $path = '/' . trim((string) $path, '/');
   99 |             }
  100 |         } else {
  101 |             $script = $_SERVER['SCRIPT_NAME'] ?? '';
  102 |             if ($script !== '' && $script !== '/') {
  103 |                 $physicalDir = rtrim(str_replace('\\', '/', dirname($script)), '/');
  104 |                 if ($physicalDir !== '' && $physicalDir !== '.' && ($path === $physicalDir || str_starts_with($path, $physicalDir . '/'))) {
  105 |                     $path = $path === $physicalDir ? '/' : substr($path, strlen($physicalDir));
  106 |                     $path = '/' . trim((string) $path, '/');
  107 |                 }
  108 |             }
  109 |         }
  110 | 
  111 |         $prefixes = [
  112 |             '/api/public/index.php',
  113 |             '/api/public',
  114 |             '/api/index.php',
  115 |             '/api-xabia-saas/public/index.php',
  116 |             '/api-xabia-saas/public',
  117 |             '/api-xabia-saas',
  118 |             '/api',
  119 |         ];
  120 |         for ($i = 0; $i < 4; $i++) {
  121 |             $changed = false;
  122 |             foreach ($prefixes as $prefix) {
  123 |                 if ($prefix === '') {
  124 |                     continue;
  125 |                 }
  126 |                 if ($path === $prefix) {
  127 |                     $path = '/';
  128 |                     $changed = true;
  129 | 
  130 |                     break;
  131 |                 }
  132 |                 if (str_starts_with($path, $prefix . '/')) {
  133 |                     $path = substr($path, strlen($prefix));
  134 |                     $path = '/' . trim((string) $path, '/');
  135 |                     $changed = true;
  136 | 
  137 |                     break;
  138 |                 }
  139 |             }
  140 |             if (!$changed) {
  141 |                 break;
  142 |             }
  143 |         }
  144 | 
  145 |         if (str_starts_with($path, '/index.php')) {
  146 |             $path = substr($path, strlen('/index.php')) ?: '/';
  147 |             $path = '/' . trim((string) $path, '/');
  148 |         } elseif (str_contains($path, '/index.php/')) {
  149 |             $pos = strpos($path, '/index.php/');
  150 |             if ($pos !== false) {
  151 |                 $path = substr($path, $pos + strlen('/index.php')) ?: '/';
  152 |                 $path = '/' . trim((string) $path, '/');
  153 |             }
  154 |         }
  155 | 
  156 |         if ($path === '' || $path === '/') {
  157 |             return '/';
  158 |         }
  159 | 
  160 |         return '/' . trim($path, '/');
  161 |     }
  162 | }

  208 |     private static function respondLicenseValidationFailure(string $key, string $domainRaw): void

  219 |                 'reason'  => 'license_key_unknown',

  225 |

  227 |         }

  229 |         $registeredDomains = array_values(array_unique(array_filter(array_map(
  230 |             static fn (array $r): string => trim((string) ($r['client_domain'] ?? '')),
  231 |             $all

  233 |         $matchedMeta = null;
  234 |         foreach ($all as $r) {
  235 |             if (!is_array($r)) {
  236 |                 continue;
  237 |             }

  239 |                 $matchedMeta = $r;
  240 |

  242 |             }

  247 |                 $key,

  251 |                 ['source' => 'validate_handler']
  252 |             );

  254 |                 'message'             => 'El dominio indicado no coincide con ninguna fila de esta clave en xabia_licenses.',
  255 |                 'reason'              => 'unauthorized_domain',
  256 |                 'registered_domain'   => $registeredSummary,
  257 |                 'registered_domains'  => $registeredDomains,
  258 |             ]));
  259 | 
  260 |             return;
  261 |         }
  262 |         if (!LicenseRepository::isLicenseRowActive($matchedMeta)) {
  263 |             Json::respond(200, array_merge($base, [
  264 |                 'message' => 'La licencia existe pero está inactiva, suspendida o caducada.',
  265 |                 'reason'  => 'license_not_active',
  266 |                 'status'  => (string) ($matchedMeta['status'] ?? ''),
  267 |             ]));
  268 | 
  269 |             return;
  270 |         }
  271 |         if (!LicenseRepository::hasWalletForLicenseKey($key)) {
  272 |             Json::respond(200, array_merge($base, [
  273 |                 'message' => 'La licencia y el dominio son correctos pero falta la fila en xabia_wallets para el license_id de facturación (MIN(id) por clave).',
  274 |                 'reason'  => 'wallet_missing',
  275 |             ]));
  276 | 
  277 |             return;
  278 |         }
  279 | 
  280 |         Json::respond(200, array_merge($base, [
  281 |             'message' => 'No se pudo completar la validación (revisa tablas y restricciones).',
  282 |             'reason'  => 'validation_incomplete',
  283 |         ]));
  284 |     }

  286 |     /**
  287 |      * $_GET a veces llega vacío con ciertos rewrites/Nginx; rellenamos desde REQUEST_URI y QUERY_STRING.
  288 |      *
  289 |      * @return array<string, string|array<mixed>>
  290 |      */
  291 |     private static function mergedQueryParams(): array
  292 |     {
  293 |         $merged = [];
  294 |         $uri = (string) ($_SERVER['REQUEST_URI'] ?? '');
  295 |         $fromUri = parse_url($uri, PHP_URL_QUERY);
  296 |         if (is_string($fromUri) && $fromUri !== '') {
  297 |             $parsed = [];
  298 |             parse_str($fromUri, $parsed);
  299 |             if (is_array($parsed)) {
  300 |                 $merged = array_merge($merged, $parsed);
  301 |             }
  302 |         }
  303 |         foreach (['REDIRECT_QUERY_STRING', 'QUERY_STRING'] as $serverKey) {
  304 |             $qs = (string) ($_SERVER[$serverKey] ?? '');
  305 |             if ($qs === '') {
  306 |                 continue;
  307 |             }
  308 |             $parsed = [];
  309 |             parse_str($qs, $parsed);
  310 |             if (is_array($parsed) && $parsed !== []) {
  311 |                 $merged = array_merge($merged, $parsed);
  312 |             }
  313 |         }
  314 |         if (is_array($_GET) && $_GET !== []) {
  315 |             $merged = array_merge($merged, $_GET);
  316 |         }
  317 | 
  318 |         return $merged;
  319 |     }
  320 | 
  321 |     /**
  322 |      * @param list<mixed> $candidates
  323 |      */
  324 |     private static function firstString(array $candidates): string
  325 |     {
  326 |         foreach ($candidates as $v) {
  327 |             if (!is_string($v)) {
  328 |                 continue;
  329 |             }
  330 |             $t = trim($v);
  331 |             if ($t !== '') {
  332 |                 return $t;
  333 |             }
  334 |         }
  335 | 
  336 |         return '';
  337 |     }

    1 | <?php
    2 | 
    3 | declare(strict_types=1);
    4 | 
    5 | namespace XabiaCentral;
    6 | 
    7 | use PDO;
    8 | use Throwable;
    9 | 
   10 | final class LicenseRepository
   11 | {
   12 |     /**
   13 |      * Búsqueda por clave: WHERE license_key = valor enviado (trim), sin traducciones.
   14 |      *
   15 |      * @return array{id:int,license_key:string,client_domain:string,plan_type:string,expiry_date:?string,status:string,tokens_remaining:int,tokens_used_total:int,wallet_license_id:int}|null
   16 |      */
   17 |     public static function findActiveWithWalletByKey(string $licenseKey, string $incomingDomainOrUrl = ''): ?array
   18 |     {
   19 |         $licenseKey = PolarLicenseKey::normalize($licenseKey);
   20 |         if ($licenseKey === '') {
   21 |             return null;
   22 |         }
   23 |         $params = [':k' => $licenseKey];
   24 |         $whereDomain = '';
   25 |         $candidates = self::domainCandidates($incomingDomainOrUrl);
   26 |         if ($candidates !== []) {
   27 |             $placeholders = [];
   28 |             foreach ($candidates as $i => $candidate) {
   29 |                 $ph = ':d' . $i;
   30 |                 $placeholders[] = $ph;
   31 |                 $params[$ph] = $candidate;
   32 |             }
   33 |             $whereDomain = ' AND l.client_domain IN (' . implode(', ', $placeholders) . ')';
   34 |         }
   35 |         $sql = 'SELECT l.id, l.license_key, l.client_domain, l.plan_type, l.expiry_date, l.status,
   36 |                        (SELECT MIN(lx.id) FROM xabia_licenses lx WHERE lx.license_key = l.license_key) AS wallet_license_id,
   37 |                        w.tokens_remaining, w.tokens_used_total
   38 |                 FROM xabia_licenses l
   39 |                 INNER JOIN xabia_wallets w ON w.license_id = (SELECT MIN(lx.id) FROM xabia_licenses lx WHERE lx.license_key = l.license_key)
   40 |                 WHERE l.license_key = :k' . $whereDomain . '
   41 |                 ORDER BY l.id DESC
   42 |                 LIMIT 1';
   43 |         $st = Db::pdo()->prepare($sql);
   44 |         $st->execute($params);
   45 |         $row = $st->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
   46 |         if ($row === false) {
   47 |             return null;
   48 |         }
   49 |         $row['id'] = (int) $row['id'];
   50 |         $row['wallet_license_id'] = isset($row['wallet_license_id']) ? (int) $row['wallet_license_id'] : (int) $row['id'];
   51 |         $row['tokens_remaining'] = (int) $row['tokens_remaining'];
   52 |         $row['tokens_used_total'] = (int) $row['tokens_used_total'];
   53 | 
   54 |         return $row;
   55 |     }
   56 | 
   57 |     /**
   58 |      * Todas las filas de licencia con esa clave (puede haber varios client_domain).
   59 |      *
   60 |      * @return list<array{id:int,license_key:string,client_domain:string,plan_type:string,expiry_date:?string,status:string}>
   61 |      */
   62 |     public static function findAllByLicenseKey(string $licenseKey): array
   63 |     {
   64 |         $licenseKey = PolarLicenseKey::normalize($licenseKey);
   65 |         if ($licenseKey === '') {
   66 |             return [];
   67 |         }
   68 |         try {
   69 |             $st = Db::pdo()->prepare(
   70 |                 'SELECT id, license_key, client_domain, plan_type, expiry_date, status
   71 |                  FROM xabia_licenses
   72 |                  WHERE license_key = :k
   73 |                  ORDER BY id ASC'
   74 |             );
   75 |             $st->execute([':k' => $licenseKey]);
   76 |             $rows = $st->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
   77 |         } catch (Throwable $e) {
   78 |             return [];
   79 |         }
   80 | 
   81 |         return is_array($rows) ? $rows : [];
   82 |     }
   83 | 
   84 |     /**
   85 |      * Indica si existe fila en xabia_wallets para el license_id de facturación (MIN(id) por license_key).
   86 |      */
   87 |     public static function hasWalletForLicenseKey(string $licenseKey): bool
   88 |     {
   89 |         $licenseKey = PolarLicenseKey::normalize($licenseKey);
   90 |         if ($licenseKey === '') {
   91 |             return false;
   92 |         }
   93 |         try {
   94 |             $st = Db::pdo()->prepare(
   95 |                 'SELECT 1 FROM xabia_wallets w
   96 |                  WHERE w.license_id = (SELECT MIN(l.id) FROM xabia_licenses l WHERE l.license_key = :k)
   97 |                  LIMIT 1'
   98 |             );
   99 |             $st->execute([':k' => $licenseKey]);
  100 | 
  101 |             return $st->fetchColumn() !== false;
  102 |         } catch (Throwable $e) {
  103 |             return false;
  104 |         }
  105 |     }
  106 | 
  107 |     public static function isLicenseRowActive(array $row): bool
  108 |     {
  109 |         if (($row['status'] ?? '') !== 'active') {
  110 |             return false;
  111 |         }
  112 |         $exp = $row['expiry_date'] ?? null;
  113 |         if ($exp !== null && $exp !== '') {
  114 |             $ts = strtotime((string) $exp);
  115 |             if ($ts !== false && $ts < time()) {
  116 |                 return false;
  117 |             }
  118 |         }
  119 | 
  120 |         return true;
  121 |     }
  122 | 
  123 |     /**
  124 |      * Consumo Hub (proxy, knowledge, usage): alineado con /license/validate — Core caducado o status distinto de
  125 |      * "active" no bloquea si hay fila cartera con tokens_remaining > 0. Solo isSuspended corta antes (en el caller).
  126 |      */
  127 |     public static function allowsHubTokenConsumption(array $domainLicenseRow, ?array $walletJoinedRow): bool
  128 |     {
  129 |         if (self::isLicenseRowActive($domainLicenseRow)) {
  130 |             return true;
  131 |         }
  132 |         if ($walletJoinedRow === null) {
  133 |             return false;
  134 |         }

  144 |     /**
  145 |      * Activaciones de addon vigentes: status active y sin caducar (expiry_date NULL o fecha >= hoy).
  146 |      *
  147 |      * @return list<array{addon_slug: string, expiry_date: ?string, created_at: ?string}>
  148 |      */
  149 |     public static function activeAddonActivationsWithExpiryForLicense(string $licenseKey): array
  150 |     {
  151 |         $licenseKey = PolarLicenseKey::normalize($licenseKey);
  152 |         if ($licenseKey === '') {
  153 |             return [];
  154 |         }
  155 |         try {
  156 |             $st = Db::pdo()->prepare(
  157 |                 "SELECT addon_slug, expiry_date, created_at
  158 |                  FROM xabia_addon_activations
  159 |                  WHERE license_key = :k
  160 |                    AND status = 'active'
  161 |                    AND (expiry_date IS NULL OR expiry_date >= CURRENT_DATE)
  162 |                  ORDER BY addon_slug ASC, id ASC"
  163 |             );
  164 |             $st->execute([':k' => $licenseKey]);
  165 |             $rows = $st->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
  166 |         } catch (Throwable $e) {
  167 |             return [];
  168 |         }
  169 |         if (!is_array($rows) || $rows === []) {
  170 |             return [];
  171 |         }
  172 |         /** @var array<string, array{expiry_date: ?string, created_at: ?string}> $merged */
  173 |         $merged = [];
  174 |         foreach ($rows as $r) {
  175 |             if (!is_array($r)) {
  176 |                 continue;
  177 |             }
  178 |             $slug = trim((string) ($r['addon_slug'] ?? ''));
  179 |             if ($slug === '') {
  180 |                 continue;
  181 |             }
  182 |             $expRaw = $r['expiry_date'] ?? null;
  183 |             $exp = ($expRaw !== null && $expRaw !== '') ? (string) $expRaw : null;
  184 |             $creRaw = $r['created_at'] ?? null;
  185 |             $cre = ($creRaw !== null && $creRaw !== '') ? (string) $creRaw : null;
  186 |             if (!isset($merged[$slug])) {
  187 |                 $merged[$slug] = ['expiry_date' => $exp, 'created_at' => $cre];
  188 | 
  189 |                 continue;
  190 |             }
  191 |             if ($exp === null || $exp === '') {
  192 |                 $merged[$slug]['expiry_date'] = null;
  193 |             } else {
  194 |                 $prev = $merged[$slug]['expiry_date'];
  195 |                 if ($prev === null || $prev === '') {
  196 |                     $merged[$slug]['expiry_date'] = $exp;
  197 |                 } else {
  198 |                     $tPrev = strtotime((string) $prev);
  199 |                     $tNew = strtotime((string) $exp);
  200 |                     if ($tPrev !== false && $tNew !== false && $tNew > $tPrev) {
  201 |                         $merged[$slug]['expiry_date'] = $exp;
  202 |                     }
  203 |                 }
  204 |             }
  205 |             if ($cre !== null && $cre !== '') {
  206 |                 $prevCre = $merged[$slug]['created_at'];
  207 |                 if ($prevCre === null || $prevCre === '') {
  208 |                     $merged[$slug]['created_at'] = $cre;
  209 |                 } else {
  210 |                     $tPrev = strtotime((string) $prevCre);
  211 |                     $tNew = strtotime((string) $cre);
  212 |                     if ($tPrev !== false && $tNew !== false && $tNew < $tPrev) {
  213 |                         $merged[$slug]['created_at'] = $cre;
  214 |                     }
  215 |                 }
  216 |             }
  217 |         }
  218 |         $out = [];
  219 |         foreach ($merged as $slug => $data) {
  220 |             $out[] = [
  221 |                 'addon_slug'  => $slug,
  222 |                 'expiry_date' => $data['expiry_date'],
  223 |                 'created_at'  => $data['created_at'],
  224 |             ];
  225 |         }
  226 |         usort($out, static fn (array $a, array $b): int => strcmp($a['addon_slug'], $b['addon_slug']));
  227 | 
  228 |         return $out;
  229 |     }
  230 | 
  231 |     /**
  232 |      * Slugs de addons activos y no caducados (misma regla que activeAddonActivationsWithExpiryForLicense).
  233 |      *
  234 |      * @return list<string>
  235 |      */
  236 |     public static function activeAddonsForLicense(string $licenseKey): array
  237 |     {
  238 |         $rows = self::activeAddonActivationsWithExpiryForLicense($licenseKey);
  239 | 
  240 |         return array_values(array_map(static fn (array $r): string => $r['addon_slug'], $rows));
  241 |     }

  243 |     /**
  244 |      * @return list<string>
  245 |      */
  246 |     private static function domainCandidates(string $incomingDomainOrUrl): array
  247 |     {
  248 |         $host = Domain::normalize($incomingDomainOrUrl);
  249 |         if ($host === '') {
  250 |             return [];
  251 |         }
  252 |         $seen = [];
  253 |         $out = [];
  254 |         $add = static function (string $d) use (&$seen, &$out): void {
  255 |             $d = strtolower(trim($d));
  256 |             if ($d === '') {
  257 |                 return;
  258 |             }
  259 |             if (isset($seen[$d])) {
  260 |                 return;
  261 |             }
  262 |             $seen[$d] = true;
  263 |             $out[] = $d;
  264 |             if (str_starts_with($d, 'www.')) {
  265 |                 $bare = substr($d, 4);
  266 |                 if ($bare !== '' && !isset($seen[$bare])) {
  267 |                     $seen[$bare] = true;
  268 |                     $out[] = $bare;
  269 |                 }
  270 |             } else {
  271 |                 $www = 'www.' . $d;
  272 |                 if (!isset($seen[$www])) {
  273 |                     $seen[$www] = true;
  274 |                     $out[] = $www;
  275 |                 }
  276 |             }
  277 |         };
  278 | 
  279 |         $parts = explode('.', $host);
  280 |         $n = count($parts);
  281 |         for ($start = 0; $start < $n; ++$start) {
  282 |             $suffix = implode('.', array_slice($parts, $start));
  283 |             if ($n - $start < 2) {
  284 |                 continue;
  285 |             }
  286 |             $add($suffix);
  287 |         }
  288 | 
  289 |         return $out;
  290 |     }

  293 |      * expiry_date ISO8601 para el plugin (compatible con merge_license_meta_from_api_payload).

  295 |     public static function expiryAsIso(?string $dbDatetime): ?string
  296 |     {
  297 |         if ($dbDatetime === null || $dbDatetime === '') {
  298 |             return null;
  299 |         }
  300 |         $ts = strtotime($dbDatetime);
  301 |         if ($ts === false) {
  302 |             return null;
  303 |         }
  304 | 
  305 |         return gmdate('c', $ts);
  306 |     }

  383 |     /**
  384 |      * Crea fila de licencia + wallet en 0; license_key debe ser la clave Polar (XABIA--…).
  385 |      */
  386 |     public static function createLicenseWithWalletFromPolar(string $billingEmail, string $clientDomain, string $licenseKey): int
  387 |     {
  388 |         $billingEmail = strtolower(trim($billingEmail));
  389 |         $clientDomain = trim($clientDomain);
  390 |         $licenseKey = PolarLicenseKey::normalize($licenseKey);
  391 |         if ($billingEmail === '' || $clientDomain === '' || $licenseKey === '') {
  392 |             return 0;
  393 |         }
  394 |         if (!PolarLicenseKey::isValidFormat($licenseKey)) {
  395 |             return 0;
  396 |         }
  397 |         $pdo = Db::pdo();
  398 |         try {
  399 |             $st = $pdo->prepare(
  400 |                 'INSERT INTO xabia_licenses (license_key, client_domain, billing_email, plan_type, expiry_date, status)
  401 |                  VALUES (:k, :d, :e, \'standard\', NULL, \'active\')'
  402 |             );
  403 |             $st->execute([
  404 |                 ':k' => $licenseKey,
  405 |                 ':d' => $clientDomain,
  406 |                 ':e' => $billingEmail,
  407 |             ]);
  408 |             $id = (int) $pdo->lastInsertId();
  409 |             if ($id < 1) {
  410 |                 return 0;
  411 |             }
  412 |             $w = $pdo->prepare(
  413 |                 'INSERT INTO xabia_wallets (license_id, tokens_remaining, tokens_used_total) VALUES (:id, 0, 0)'
  414 |             );
  415 |             $w->execute([':id' => $id]);
  416 | 
  417 |             return $id;
  418 |         } catch (Throwable $e) {
  419 |             $rows = self::findAllByLicenseKey($licenseKey);
  420 |             if ($rows === []) {
  421 |                 return 0;
  422 |             }
  423 |             $minId = min(array_map(static fn (array $r): int => (int) ($r['id'] ?? 0), $rows));
  424 |             if ($minId < 1) {
  425 |                 return 0;
  426 |             }
  427 |             if (!self::hasWalletForLicenseKey($licenseKey)) {
  428 |                 try {
  429 |                     $w = $pdo->prepare(
  430 |                         'INSERT INTO xabia_wallets (license_id, tokens_remaining, tokens_used_total) VALUES (:id, 0, 0)'
  431 |                     );
  432 |                     $w->execute([':id' => $minId]);
  433 |                 } catch (Throwable $e2) {
  434 |                 }
  435 |             }
  436 | 
  437 |             return $minId;
  438 |         }
  439 |     }

  441 |     public static function addTokensToWalletForLicenseKey(string $licenseKey, int $delta): void
  442 |     {
  443 |         if ($delta === 0) {
  444 |             return;
  445 |         }
  446 |         $licenseKey = PolarLicenseKey::normalize($licenseKey);
  447 |         if ($licenseKey === '') {
  448 |             return;
  449 |         }
  450 |         $st = Db::pdo()->prepare(
  451 |             'UPDATE xabia_wallets w
  452 |              INNER JOIN (
  453 |                  SELECT MIN(id) AS mid FROM xabia_licenses WHERE license_key = :k
  454 |              ) x ON x.mid = w.license_id
  455 |              SET w.tokens_remaining = w.tokens_remaining + :d'
  456 |         );
  457 |         $st->execute([':k' => $licenseKey, ':d' => $delta]);
  458 |     }

  460 |     /**
  461 |      * Extiende la caducidad del Core: base = max(ahora, max(expiry existente)), luego +N años.
  462 |      */
  463 |     public static function extendCoreExpiryForLicenseKey(string $licenseKey, int $years = 1): void
  464 |     {
  465 |         $licenseKey = PolarLicenseKey::normalize($licenseKey);
  466 |         if ($licenseKey === '') {
  467 |             return;
  468 |         }
  469 |         if ($years < 1) {
  470 |             $years = 1;
  471 |         }
  472 |         $rows = self::findAllByLicenseKey($licenseKey);
  473 |         $maxTs = time();
  474 |         foreach ($rows as $r) {
  475 |             $e = $r['expiry_date'] ?? null;
  476 |             if ($e !== null && $e !== '') {
  477 |                 $t = strtotime((string) $e);
  478 |                 if ($t !== false && $t > $maxTs) {
  479 |                     $maxTs = $t;
  480 |                 }
  481 |             }
  482 |         }
  483 |         $newTs = strtotime('+' . $years . ' years', $maxTs);
  484 |         if ($newTs === false) {
  485 |             return;
  486 |         }
  487 |         $mysql = gmdate('Y-m-d H:i:s', $newTs);
  488 |         try {
  489 |             $st = Db::pdo()->prepare('UPDATE xabia_licenses SET expiry_date = :ex, status = \'active\' WHERE license_key = :k');
  490 |             $st->execute([':ex' => $mysql, ':k' => $licenseKey]);
  491 |         } catch (Throwable $e) {
  492 |         }
  493 |     }

  149 |     public static function activeAddonActivationsWithExpiryForLicense(string $licenseKey): array

  384 |      * Crea fila de licencia + wallet en 0; license_key debe ser la clave Polar (XABIA--…).
  385 |      */
  386 |     public static function createLicenseWithWalletFromPolar(string $billingEmail, string $clientDomain, string $licenseKey): int
  387 |     {
  388 |         $billingEmail = strtolower(trim($billingEmail));
  389 |         $clientDomain = trim($clientDomain);
  390 |         $licenseKey = PolarLicenseKey::normalize($licenseKey);
  391 |         if ($billingEmail === '' || $clientDomain === '' || $licenseKey === '') {
  392 |             return 0;
  393 |         }
  394 |         if (!PolarLicenseKey::isValidFormat($licenseKey)) {
  395 |             return 0;
  396 |         }
  397 |         $pdo = Db::pdo();
  398 |         try {
  399 |             $st = $pdo->prepare(
  400 |                 'INSERT INTO xabia_licenses (license_key, client_domain, billing_email, plan_type, expiry_date, status)
  401 |                  VALUES (:k, :d, :e, \'standard\', NULL, \'active\')'
  402 |             );
  403 |             $st->execute([
  404 |                 ':k' => $licenseKey,
  405 |                 ':d' => $clientDomain,
  406 |                 ':e' => $billingEmail,
  407 |             ]);
  408 |             $id = (int) $pdo->lastInsertId();
  409 |             if ($id < 1) {
  410 |                 return 0;
  411 |             }
  412 |             $w = $pdo->prepare(
  413 |                 'INSERT INTO xabia_wallets (license_id, tokens_remaining, tokens_used_total) VALUES (:id, 0, 0)'
  414 |             );
  415 |             $w->execute([':id' => $id]);
  416 | 
  417 |             return $id;
  418 |         } catch (Throwable $e) {
  419 |             $rows = self::findAllByLicenseKey($licenseKey);
  420 |             if ($rows === []) {
  421 |                 return 0;
  422 |             }
  423 |             $minId = min(array_map(static fn (array $r): int => (int) ($r['id'] ?? 0), $rows));
  424 |             if ($minId < 1) {
  425 |                 return 0;
  426 |             }
  427 |             if (!self::hasWalletForLicenseKey($licenseKey)) {
  428 |                 try {
  429 |                     $w = $pdo->prepare(
  430 |                         'INSERT INTO xabia_wallets (license_id, tokens_remaining, tokens_used_total) VALUES (:id, 0, 0)'
  431 |                     );
  432 |                     $w->execute([':id' => $minId]);
  433 |                 } catch (Throwable $e2) {
  434 |                 }
  435 |             }
  436 | 
  437 |             return $minId;
  438 |         }
  439 |     }

  441 |     public static function addTokensToWalletForLicenseKey(string $licenseKey, int $delta): void
  442 |     {
  443 |         if ($delta === 0) {
  444 |             return;
  445 |         }
  446 |         $licenseKey = PolarLicenseKey::normalize($licenseKey);
  447 |         if ($licenseKey === '') {
  448 |             return;
  449 |         }
  450 |         $st = Db::pdo()->prepare(
  451 |             'UPDATE xabia_wallets w
  452 |              INNER JOIN (
  453 |                  SELECT MIN(id) AS mid FROM xabia_licenses WHERE license_key = :k
  454 |              ) x ON x.mid = w.license_id
  455 |              SET w.tokens_remaining = w.tokens_remaining + :d'
  456 |         );
  457 |         $st->execute([':k' => $licenseKey, ':d' => $delta]);
  458 |     }

  460 |     /**
  461 |      * Extiende la caducidad del Core: base = max(ahora, max(expiry existente)), luego +N años.
  462 |      */
  463 |     public static function extendCoreExpiryForLicenseKey(string $licenseKey, int $years = 1): void
  464 |     {
  465 |         $licenseKey = PolarLicenseKey::normalize($licenseKey);
  466 |         if ($licenseKey === '') {
  467 |             return;
  468 |         }
  469 |         if ($years < 1) {
  470 |             $years = 1;
  471 |         }
  472 |         $rows = self::findAllByLicenseKey($licenseKey);
  473 |         $maxTs = time();
  474 |         foreach ($rows as $r) {
  475 |             $e = $r['expiry_date'] ?? null;
  476 |             if ($e !== null && $e !== '') {
  477 |                 $t = strtotime((string) $e);
  478 |                 if ($t !== false && $t > $maxTs) {
  479 |                     $maxTs = $t;
  480 |                 }
  481 |             }
  482 |         }
  483 |         $newTs = strtotime('+' . $years . ' years', $maxTs);
  484 |         if ($newTs === false) {
  485 |             return;
  486 |         }
  487 |         $mysql = gmdate('Y-m-d H:i:s', $newTs);
  488 |         try {
  489 |             $st = Db::pdo()->prepare('UPDATE xabia_licenses SET expiry_date = :ex, status = \'active\' WHERE license_key = :k');
  490 |             $st->execute([':ex' => $mysql, ':k' => $licenseKey]);
  491 |         } catch (Throwable $e) {
  492 |         }
  493 |     }
  494 |

    1 | <?php
    2 | 
    3 | declare(strict_types=1);
    4 | 
    5 | namespace XabiaCentral;
    6 | 
    7 | /**
    8 |  * Alinea el dominio declarado por el cliente (body / X-Xabia-Source) con Origin/Referer
    9 |  * cuando estas cabeceras están presentes, para detectar copias de WordPress que reenvían otra identidad.
   10 |  */
   11 | final class LicenseDomainEnforcer
   12 | {
   13 |     /**
   14 |      * @return array{ok: true, effective_claim: string}|array{
   15 |      *   ok: false,
   16 |      *   code: string,
   17 |      *   pirate_domain: string,
   18 |      *   origin_host: string,
   19 |      *   referer_host: string
   20 |      * }
   21 |      */
   22 |     public static function reconcileClaimedWithHeaders(string $claimedUrlOrHost): array
   23 |     {
   24 |         $claimed = trim($claimedUrlOrHost);
   25 |         $originRaw = trim((string) ($_SERVER['HTTP_ORIGIN'] ?? ''));
   26 |         $refererRaw = trim((string) ($_SERVER['HTTP_REFERER'] ?? ''));
   27 | 
   28 |         $originHost = $originRaw !== '' ? Domain::normalize($originRaw) : '';
   29 |         $refererHost = $refererRaw !== '' ? Domain::normalize($refererRaw) : '';
   30 | 
   31 |         if ($originHost === '' && $refererHost === '') {
   32 |             return ['ok' => true, 'effective_claim' => $claimed];
   33 |         }
   34 | 
   35 |         if ($originHost !== '' && $refererHost !== ''
   36 |             && !Domain::domainsMatch($originHost, $refererHost)) {
   37 |             return [
   38 |                 'ok'            => false,
   39 |                 'code'          => 'header_origin_referer_mismatch',
   40 |                 'pirate_domain' => $refererHost,
   41 |                 'origin_host'  => $originHost,
   42 |                 'referer_host' => $refererHost,
   43 |             ];
   44 |         }
   45 | 
   46 |         $httpHost = $originHost !== '' ? $originHost : $refererHost;
   47 |         if ($httpHost !== '' && !Domain::domainsMatch($httpHost, $claimed)) {
   48 |             return [
   49 |                 'ok'            => false,
   50 |                 'code'          => 'claim_header_mismatch',
   51 |                 'pirate_domain' => $httpHost,
   52 |                 'origin_host'   => $originHost,
   53 |                 'referer_host'  => $refererHost,
   54 |             ];
   55 |         }
   56 | 
   57 |         return ['ok' => true, 'effective_claim' => $claimed];
   58 |     }
   59 | }

    7 | use DateInterval;
    8 | use DateTimeImmutable;
