   10 | use Throwable;
   11 | use XabiaCentral\Domain;
   12 | use XabiaCentral\Env;
   13 | use XabiaCentral\Json;
   14 | use XabiaCentral\LicenseRepository;
   15 | use XabiaCentral\PolarLicenseKey;
   16 | use XabiaCentral\PolarProductMap;
   17 | use XabiaCentral\StandardWebhooks;
   18 | 
   19 | /**
   20 |  * Webhook Polar (Standard Webhooks).
   21 |  * La licencia en xabia_licenses.license_key es el identificador que envía Polar (p. ej. XABIA--…); no se generan claves locales.
   22 |  *
   23 |  * Prioridad metadata (1:1 DTP): si existe product_type = initial | renewal | topup (checkout/order o línea de pedido),
   24 |  * esa tipología manda sobre el significado inferido solo por UUID.
   25 |  * Sin product_type: se usa PolarProductMap por prod UUID (comportamiento histórico).
   26 |  *
   27 |  * - order.paid / order.updated (pagado): packs, core one-shot, addons.
   28 |  * - subscription.created / subscription.updated / subscription.active: core y addons recurrentes.
   29 |  * - benefit_grant.created / updated: clave en payload de beneficio (Polar Licenses).
   30 |  */
   31 | final class PolarWebhookHandler
   32 | {
   33 |     /**
   34 |      * Eventos que pueden volver a procesarse con el mismo Webhook-Id (upsert de add-on/core es idempotente).
   35 |      * Excluye order.paid para no duplicar tokens.
   36 |      */
   37 |     private static function polarEventAllowsDuplicateReplay(string $eventType): bool
   38 |     {
   39 |         return in_array($eventType, [
   40 |             'order.updated',
   41 |             'subscription.created',
   42 |             'subscription.updated',
   43 |             'subscription.active',
   44 |             'benefit_grant.created',
   45 |             'benefit_grant.updated',
   46 |         ], true);
   47 |     }
   48 | 
   49 |     public static function handle(): void
   50 |     {
   51 |         if (strtoupper((string) ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] ?? '')) !== 'POST') {
   52 |             Json::respond(405, ['error' => ['message' => 'Method Not Allowed', 'type' => 'method']]);
   53 | 
   54 |             return;
   55 |         }
   56 |         $raw = (string) file_get_contents('php://input');
   57 |         $secret = Env::str('POLAR_WEBHOOK_SECRET');
   58 |         if ($secret === '' || !StandardWebhooks::verifySymmetricV1($raw, $_SERVER, $secret)) {
   59 |             Json::respond(403, ['error' => ['message' => 'Firma webhook inválida', 'type' => 'polar_signature']]);
   60 | 
   61 |             return;
   62 |         }
   63 |         $payload = json_decode($raw, true);
   64 |         if (!is_array($payload)) {
   65 |             Json::respond(400, ['error' => ['message' => 'JSON inválido', 'type' => 'parse']]);
   66 | 
   67 |             return;
   68 |         }
   69 |         $type = isset($payload['type']) && is_string($payload['type']) ? trim($payload['type']) : '';
   70 |         $data = isset($payload['data']) && is_array($payload['data']) ? $payload['data'] : [];
   71 | 
   72 |         $webhookId = isset($_SERVER['HTTP_WEBHOOK_ID']) && is_string($_SERVER['HTTP_WEBHOOK_ID'])
   73 |             ? trim($_SERVER['HTTP_WEBHOOK_ID']) : '';
   74 |         if ($webhookId !== '' && LicenseRepository::webhookDeliveryExists($webhookId)) {
   75 |             // Re-ejecutar fulfillment idempotente (add-ons / core) tras fixes o replays de Polar.
   76 |             // No incluir order.paid: podría duplicar entrega de tokens.
   77 |             if (self::polarEventAllowsDuplicateReplay($type)) {
   78 |                 try {
   79 |                     self::fulfill($type, $data);
   80 |                 } catch (Throwable $e) {
   81 |                     if (Env::str('XABIA_DEBUG') !== '') {
   82 |                         Json::respond(500, [
   83 |                             'error' => [
   84 |                                 'message' => 'Error al reprocesar webhook duplicado',
   85 |                                 'type'    => 'polar_fulfillment_duplicate',
   86 |                                 'detail'  => $e->getMessage(),
   87 |                             ],
   88 |                         ]);
   89 | 
   90 |                         return;
   91 |                     }
   92 |                     Json::respond(500, ['error' => ['message' => 'Error al reprocesar webhook duplicado', 'type' => 'polar_fulfillment_duplicate']]);
   93 | 
   94 |                     return;
   95 |                 }
   96 |             }
   97 |             Json::respond(202, ['ok' => true, 'duplicate' => true]);
   98 | 
   99 |             return;
  100 |         }
  101 | 
  102 |         $fulfillTypes = [
  103 |             'order.paid', 'order.updated',
  104 |             'subscription.created', 'subscription.updated', 'subscription.active',
  105 |             'benefit_grant.created', 'benefit_grant.updated',
  106 |             'subscription.canceled', 'subscription.revoked',
  107 |             'benefit_grant.revoked',
  108 |         ];
  109 |         if (!in_array($type, $fulfillTypes, true)) {
  110 |             if ($webhookId !== '') {
  111 |                 LicenseRepository::recordWebhookDelivery($webhookId);
  112 |             }
  113 |             Json::respond(202, ['ok' => true, 'ignored' => true, 'type' => $type]);
  114 | 
  115 |             return;
  116 |         }
  117 | 
  118 |         try {
  119 |             self::fulfill($type, $data);
  120 |             if ($webhookId !== '') {
  121 |                 LicenseRepository::recordWebhookDelivery($webhookId);
  122 |             }
  123 |         } catch (Throwable $e) {
  124 |             if (Env::str('XABIA_DEBUG') !== '') {
  125 |                 Json::respond(500, [
  126 |                     'error' => [
  127 |                         'message' => 'Error al procesar webhook',
  128 |                         'type'    => 'polar_fulfillment',
  129 |                         'detail'  => $e->getMessage(),
  130 |                     ],
  131 |                 ]);
  132 | 
  133 |                 return;
  134 |             }
  135 |             Json::respond(500, ['error' => ['message' => 'Error al procesar webhook', 'type' => 'polar_fulfillment']]);
  136 | 
  137 |             return;
  138 |         }
  139 | 
  140 |         Json::respond(202, ['ok' => true]);
  141 |     }
  142 | 
  143 |     /**
  144 |      * @param array<string, mixed> $data
  145 |      */
  146 |     private static function fulfill(string $eventType, array $data): void
  147 |     {
  148 |         $isRevocationEvent = in_array($eventType, ['subscription.canceled', 'subscription.revoked', 'benefit_grant.revoked'], true);
  149 |         if ($eventType === 'order.paid' || $eventType === 'order.updated') {
  150 |             if (!self::isOrderPaidState($data)) {
  151 |                 $oid = self::extractOrderIdForLog($data);
  152 |                 error_log('[xabia-polar] skip: pedido no marcado como pagado (order_id=' . $oid . ', event=' . $eventType . ')');
  153 | 
  154 |                 return;
  155 |             }
  156 |         } elseif ($eventType === 'subscription.created') {
  157 |             if (!self::isSubscriptionInitiallyActive($data)) {
  158 |                 $st = self::extractSubscriptionStatus($data);
  159 |                 error_log('[xabia-polar] skip: suscripción creada sin estado activo inicial (status=' . $st . ')');
  160 | 
  161 |                 return;
  162 |             }
  163 |         } elseif ($eventType === 'subscription.updated' || $eventType === 'subscription.active') {
  164 |             if (!self::isSubscriptionActiveOrTrialing($data)) {
  165 |                 $st = self::extractSubscriptionStatus($data);
  166 |                 error_log('[xabia-polar] skip: suscripción no activa (status=' . $st . ', event=' . $eventType . ')');
  167 | 
  168 |                 return;
  169 |             }
  170 |         }
  171 | 
  172 |         $email = self::extractCustomerEmail($data);
  173 |         if ($email === '') {
  174 |             error_log('[xabia-polar] skip: no customer email (' . $eventType . ')');
  175 | 
  176 |             return;
  177 |         }
  178 |         $meta = self::extractMetadata($data);
  179 |         $polarExtracted = self::extractPolarLicenseKeyForWebhook($meta, $data);
  180 |         $licenseKey = self::resolveLicenseKey($email, $meta, $data, $polarExtracted);
  181 |         if ($licenseKey === '') {
  182 |             error_log('[xabia-polar] skip: could not resolve license_key for ' . $email);
  183 | 
  184 |             return;
  185 |         }
  186 |         $productIds = self::extractPolarProductIds($data);
  187 |         if ($productIds === []) {
  188 |             error_log('[xabia-polar] note: no product ids in payload (' . $eventType . ') — licencia puede haberse creado; entrega de producto omitida.');
  189 |         }
  190 |         $clientUrl = self::metadataString($meta, ['client_url', 'site_url', 'domain']);
  191 |         $polarRef = self::metadataString($meta, ['polar_order_id', 'order_id'])
  192 |             ?: (isset($data['id']) && is_string($data['id']) ? $data['id'] : '');
  193 | 
  194 |         $tokensDelivered = 0;
  195 |         $webhookId = isset($_SERVER['HTTP_WEBHOOK_ID']) && is_string($_SERVER['HTTP_WEBHOOK_ID'])
  196 |             ? trim($_SERVER['HTTP_WEBHOOK_ID']) : '';
  197 |         foreach ($productIds as $pid) {
  198 |             $mapRule = PolarProductMap::resolve($pid);
  199 |             $itemMeta = self::mergedMetadataForPolarProduct($data, $pid);
  200 |             $metaProductType = self::parseMetaProductType($itemMeta);
  201 |             if ($metaProductType !== null) {
  202 |                 self::fulfillByMetaProductType(
  203 |                     $eventType,
  204 |                     $data,
  205 |                     $licenseKey,
  206 |                     $pid,
  207 |                     $metaProductType,
  208 |                     $mapRule,
  209 |                     $itemMeta,
  210 |                     $isRevocationEvent,
  211 |                     $clientUrl,
  212 |                     $webhookId,
  213 |                     $tokensDelivered
  214 |                 );
  215 | 
  216 |                 continue;
  217 |             }
  218 | 
  219 |             if ($mapRule === null) {
  220 |                 if (Env::str('XABIA_DEBUG') !== '') {
  221 |                     error_log('[xabia-polar] unmapped product id (PolarProductMap): ' . $pid);
  222 |                 }
  223 | 
  224 |                 continue;
  225 |             }
  226 |             self::fulfillPolarTypedRule(
  227 |                 $eventType,
  228 |                 $data,
  229 |                 $licenseKey,
  230 |                 $pid,
  231 |                 $mapRule,
  232 |                 $isRevocationEvent,
  233 |                 $clientUrl,
  234 |                 $webhookId,
  235 |                 $tokensDelivered
  236 |             );
  237 |         }
  238 |         if ($eventType === 'order.paid' && $tokensDelivered > 0) {
  239 |             $orderId = self::extractOrderIdForLog($data);
  240 |             error_log(
  241 |                 '[xabia-polar] Pago confirmado para el pedido [' . $orderId . ']. Entregando ' . (string) $tokensDelivered . ' tokens.'
  242 |             );
  243 |         }
  244 |         if ($polarRef !== '') {
  245 |             error_log('[xabia-polar] fulfilled ' . $eventType . ' ref=' . $polarRef . ' license_key=' . $licenseKey);
  246 |         }
  247 |     }
  248 | 
  249 |     /**
  250 |      * product_type en metadata (initial|renewal|topup): rama DTP; puede combinar meta dinámica + PolarProductMap.
  251 |      *
  252 |      * @param array<string, mixed>|null $mapRule
  253 |      * @param array<string, mixed>      $meta
  254 |      */
  255 |     private static function fulfillByMetaProductType(
  256 |         string $eventType,
  257 |         array $data,
  258 |         string $licenseKey,
  259 |         string $pid,
  260 |         string $metaProductType,
  261 |         ?array $mapRule,
  262 |         array $meta,
  263 |         bool $isRevocationEvent,
  264 |         string $clientUrl,
  265 |         string $webhookId,
  266 |         int &$tokensDelivered
  267 |     ): void {
  268 |         if ($metaProductType === 'topup') {
  269 |             if ($eventType !== 'order.paid') {
  270 |                 return;
  271 |             }
  272 |             $amount = self::metaPositiveInt($meta, ['tokens', 'token_amount', 'token_pack', 'topup_tokens', 'amount']);
  273 |             if ($amount < 1 && $mapRule !== null && (($mapRule['type'] ?? '') === 'tokens')) {
  274 |                 $amount = (int) ($mapRule['amount'] ?? 0);
  275 |             }
  276 |             if ($amount < 1) {
  277 |                 if (Env::str('XABIA_DEBUG') !== '') {
  278 |                     error_log('[xabia-polar] product_type=topup sin cantidad válida (tokens/token_amount/…) pid=' . $pid);
  279 |                 }
  280 | 
  281 |                 return;
  282 |             }
  283 |             $businessKey = self::buildTopupBusinessKey($data, $licenseKey, $pid, $amount);
  284 |             if (LicenseRepository::webhookBusinessEventExists('polar', $businessKey)) {
  285 |                 return;
  286 |             }
  287 |             LicenseRepository::addTokensToWalletForLicenseKey($licenseKey, $amount);
  288 |             LicenseRepository::recordWebhookBusinessEvent('polar', $businessKey, $webhookId);
  289 |             $tokensDelivered += $amount;
  290 | 
  291 |             return;
  292 |         }
  293 | 
  294 |         $effective = self::resolveEffectiveRuleForInitialRenewal($mapRule, $meta);
  295 |         if ($effective === null) {
  296 |             if (Env::str('XABIA_DEBUG') !== '') {
  297 |                 error_log('[xabia-polar] product_type=' . $metaProductType . ' sin regla (map/meta) pid=' . $pid);
  298 |             }
  299 | 
  300 |             return;
  301 |         }
  302 | 
  303 |         self::fulfillPolarTypedRule(
  304 |             $eventType,
  305 |             $data,
  306 |             $licenseKey,
  307 |             $pid,
  308 |             $effective,
  309 |             $isRevocationEvent,
  310 |             $clientUrl,
  311 |             $webhookId,
  312 |             $tokensDelivered
  313 |         );
  314 |     }
  315 | 
  316 |     /**
  317 |      * Fulfillment según regla type=tokens|addon|core (mapa o sintética desde meta).
  318 |      *
  319 |      * @param array{type: string, amount?: int, addon_slug?: string, extend_years?: int} $rule
  320 |      */
  321 |     private static function fulfillPolarTypedRule(
  322 |         string $eventType,
  323 |         array $data,
  324 |         string $licenseKey,
  325 |         string $pid,
  326 |         array $rule,
  327 |         bool $isRevocationEvent,
  328 |         string $clientUrl,
  329 |         string $webhookId,
  330 |         int &$tokensDelivered
  331 |     ): void {
  332 |         $t = (string) ($rule['type'] ?? '');
  333 |         if ($eventType === 'subscription.created' && $t === 'tokens') {
  334 |             return;
  335 |         }
  336 |         if ($t === 'tokens') {
  337 |             if ($eventType !== 'order.paid') {
  338 |                 return;
  339 |             }
  340 |             $amount = (int) ($rule['amount'] ?? 0);
  341 |             if ($amount > 0) {
  342 |                 $businessKey = self::buildTopupBusinessKey($data, $licenseKey, $pid, $amount);
  343 |                 if (!LicenseRepository::webhookBusinessEventExists('polar', $businessKey)) {
  344 |                     LicenseRepository::addTokensToWalletForLicenseKey($licenseKey, $amount);
  345 |                     LicenseRepository::recordWebhookBusinessEvent('polar', $businessKey, $webhookId);
  346 |                     $tokensDelivered += $amount;
  347 |                 }
  348 |             }
  349 | 
  350 |             return;
  351 |         }
  352 |         if ($t === 'addon') {
  353 |             if ($isRevocationEvent) {
  354 |                 $slug = trim((string) ($rule['addon_slug'] ?? ''));
  355 |                 if ($slug !== '') {
  356 |                     LicenseRepository::setAddonActivationStatusForLicense($licenseKey, $slug, 'expired');
  357 |                 }
  358 | 
  359 |                 return;
  360 |             }
  361 |             if (self::polarEventFulfillsAddonOrCore($eventType)) {
  362 |                 $slug = trim((string) ($rule['addon_slug'] ?? ''));
  363 |                 if ($slug !== '') {
  364 |                     $exp = self::polarSubscriptionLikeEvent($eventType)
  365 |                         ? self::addonExpiryFromPolarSubscriptionPayload($data)
  366 |                         : self::addonExpiryDateOneYear();
  367 |                     LicenseRepository::upsertAddonActivation(
  368 |                         $licenseKey,
  369 |                         $pid,
  370 |                         $slug,
  371 |                         $exp,
  372 |                         $clientUrl,
  373 |                         'polar'
  374 |                     );
  375 |                 }
  376 |             }
  377 | 
  378 |             return;
  379 |         }
  380 |         if ($t === 'core') {
  381 |             if ($isRevocationEvent) {
  382 |                 LicenseRepository::setLicenseStatusForLicenseKey($licenseKey, 'expired');
  383 | 
  384 |                 return;
  385 |             }
  386 |             if (self::polarEventFulfillsAddonOrCore($eventType)) {
  387 |                 $years = (int) ($rule['extend_years'] ?? 1);
  388 |                 if ($years < 1) {
  389 |                     $years = 1;
  390 |                 }
  391 |                 LicenseRepository::extendCoreExpiryForLicenseKey($licenseKey, $years);
  392 |             }
  393 |         }
  394 |     }
  395 | 
  396 |     /**
  397 |      * @param array<string, mixed>|null $mapRule
  398 |      * @param array<string, mixed>      $meta
  399 |      *
  400 |      * @return array{type: string, amount?: int, addon_slug?: string, extend_years?: int}|null
  401 |      */
  402 |     private static function resolveEffectiveRuleForInitialRenewal(?array $mapRule, array $meta): ?array
  403 |     {
  404 |         if ($mapRule !== null && isset($mapRule['type']) && is_string($mapRule['type'])) {
  405 |             if ($mapRule['type'] === 'tokens') {
  406 |                 return self::syntheticRuleFromPolarMeta($meta);
  407 |             }
  408 | 
  409 |             return $mapRule;
  410 |         }
  411 | 
  412 |         return self::syntheticRuleFromPolarMeta($meta);
  413 |     }
  414 | 
  415 |     /**
  416 |      * Regla inferida solo desde metadata (productos dinámicos en Polar).
  417 |      *
  418 |      * @param array<string, mixed> $meta
  419 |      *
  420 |      * @return array{type: string, amount?: int, addon_slug?: string, extend_years?: int}|null
  421 |      */
  422 |     private static function syntheticRuleFromPolarMeta(array $meta): ?array
  423 |     {
  424 |         $slug = self::metadataString($meta, ['addon_slug', 'xabia_addon_slug']);
  425 |         if ($slug !== '') {
  426 |             return ['type' => 'addon', 'addon_slug' => $slug];
  427 |         }
  428 |         $years = self::metaPositiveInt($meta, ['extend_years', 'core_extend_years', 'extension_years']);
  429 |         if ($years > 0) {
  430 |             return ['type' => 'core', 'extend_years' => $years];
  431 |         }
  432 |         $kind = strtolower(self::metadataString($meta, ['product_kind', 'kind', 'license_kind']));
  433 |         if ($kind === 'core') {
  434 |             $y = self::metaPositiveInt($meta, ['extend_years', 'core_extend_years']);
  435 | 
  436 |             return ['type' => 'core', 'extend_years' => $y > 0 ? $y : 1];
  437 |         }
  438 | 
  439 |         return null;
  440 |     }
  441 | 
  442 |     /**
  443 |      * @param array<string, mixed> $meta
  444 |      *
  445 |      * @return 'initial'|'renewal'|'topup'|null
  446 |      */
  447 |     private static function parseMetaProductType(array $meta): ?string
  448 |     {
  449 |         foreach (['product_type', 'productType', 'purchase_type'] as $key) {
  450 |             if (!array_key_exists($key, $meta)) {
  451 |                 continue;
  452 |             }
  453 |             $v = $meta[$key];
  454 |             $raw = strtolower(trim(is_string($v) ? $v : (is_int($v) || is_float($v) ? (string) $v : '')));
  455 |             if (in_array($raw, ['initial', 'renewal', 'topup'], true)) {
  456 |                 return $raw;
  457 |             }
  458 |         }
  459 | 
  460 |         return null;
  461 |     }
  462 | 
  463 |     /**
  464 |      * @param array<string, mixed> $meta
  465 |      * @param list<string>         $keys
  466 |      */
  467 |     private static function metaPositiveInt(array $meta, array $keys): int
  468 |     {
  469 |         foreach ($keys as $k) {
  470 |             if (!array_key_exists($k, $meta)) {
  471 |                 continue;
  472 |             }
  473 |             $v = $meta[$k];
  474 |             if (is_int($v)) {
  475 |                 return max(0, $v);
  476 |             }
  477 |             if (is_float($v)) {
  478 |                 return max(0, (int) round($v));
  479 |             }
  480 |             if (is_string($v)) {
  481 |                 $s = trim($v);
  482 |                 if ($s !== '' && preg_match('/^-?\d+$/', $s) === 1) {
  483 |                     return max(0, (int) $s);
  484 |                 }
  485 |             }
  486 |         }
  487 | 
  488 |         return 0;
  489 |     }
  490 | 
  491 |     /**
  492 |      * Metadata de checkout fusionada; si hay líneas de pedido con este product_id, sus metadatos tienen prioridad.
  493 |      *
  494 |      * @param array<string, mixed> $data
  495 |      */
  496 |     private static function mergedMetadataForPolarProduct(array $data, string $pid): array
  497 |     {
  498 |         $base = self::extractMetadata($data);
  499 |         foreach (['order', 'checkout'] as $wrap) {
  500 |             if (!isset($data[$wrap]) || !is_array($data[$wrap])) {

--- ÚLTIMAS 500 LÍNEAS (510–1009) ---
  510 |                         continue;
  511 |                     }
  512 |                     if (!self::lineItemMatchesPolarProduct($item, $pid)) {
  513 |                         continue;
  514 |                     }
  515 |                     $base = array_merge($base, self::extractMetadata($item));
  516 |                 }
  517 |             }
  518 |         }
  519 |         foreach (['items', 'order_items', 'lines', 'line_items'] as $ik) {
  520 |             if (!isset($data[$ik]) || !is_array($data[$ik])) {
  521 |                 continue;
  522 |             }
  523 |             foreach ($data[$ik] as $item) {
  524 |                 if (!is_array($item)) {
  525 |                     continue;
  526 |                 }
  527 |                 if (!self::lineItemMatchesPolarProduct($item, $pid)) {
  528 |                     continue;
  529 |                 }
  530 |                 $base = array_merge($base, self::extractMetadata($item));
  531 |             }
  532 |         }
  533 | 
  534 |         return $base;
  535 |     }
  536 | 
  537 |     /**
  538 |      * @param array<string, mixed> $item
  539 |      */
  540 |     private static function lineItemMatchesPolarProduct(array $item, string $pid): bool
  541 |     {
  542 |         $keys = ['product_id'];
  543 |         foreach ($keys as $k) {
  544 |             if (isset($item[$k]) && (is_string($item[$k]) || is_int($item[$k]) || is_float($item[$k]))) {
  545 |                 $raw = trim((string) $item[$k]);
  546 |                 if ($raw !== '' && self::polarProductIdsEqual($raw, $pid)) {
  547 |                     return true;
  548 |                 }
  549 |             }
  550 |         }
  551 |         if (isset($item['product']) && is_array($item['product']) && isset($item['product']['id'])) {
  552 |             $rid = $item['product']['id'];
  553 |             if (is_string($rid) || is_int($rid)) {
  554 |                 $raw = trim((string) $rid);
  555 |                 if ($raw !== '' && self::polarProductIdsEqual($raw, $pid)) {
  556 |                     return true;
  557 |                 }
  558 |             }
  559 |         }
  560 | 
  561 |         return false;
  562 |     }
  563 | 
  564 |     private static function polarProductIdsEqual(string $a, string $b): bool
  565 |     {
  566 |         return self::normalizePolarProductUuid($a) === self::normalizePolarProductUuid($b);
  567 |     }
  568 | 
  569 |     private static function normalizePolarProductUuid(string $id): string
  570 |     {
  571 |         $id = strtolower(trim($id));
  572 |         if ($id === '') {
  573 |             return '';
  574 |         }
  575 |         if (str_starts_with($id, 'prod_')) {
  576 |             $id = substr($id, 5);
  577 |         }
  578 | 
  579 |         return $id;
  580 |     }
  581 | 
  582 |     /**
  583 |      * @param array<string, mixed> $data
  584 |      */
  585 |     private static function buildTopupBusinessKey(array $data, string $licenseKey, string $productId, int $amount): string
  586 |     {
  587 |         $orderId = '';
  588 |         foreach (['id', 'order_id', 'number'] as $k) {
  589 |             if (isset($data[$k]) && (is_string($data[$k]) || is_int($data[$k]))) {
  590 |                 $v = trim((string) $data[$k]);
  591 |                 if ($v !== '') {
  592 |                     $orderId = strtolower($v);
  593 | 
  594 |                     break;
  595 |                 }
  596 |             }
  597 |         }
  598 |         if ($orderId === '') {
  599 |             $orderId = sha1(json_encode($data, JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE | JSON_UNESCAPED_SLASHES) ?: '');
  600 |         }
  601 | 
  602 |         return 'polar:topup:' . sha1(strtolower($licenseKey) . '|' . strtolower($productId) . '|' . (string) $amount . '|' . $orderId);
  603 |     }

  605 |     /**
  606 |      * Eventos Polar que deben aplicar entrega de addon o extensión Core (alineado con $fulfillTypes).
  607 |      */
  608 |     private static function polarEventFulfillsAddonOrCore(string $eventType): bool
  609 |     {
  610 |         return in_array($eventType, [
  611 |             'order.paid',
  612 |             'order.updated',
  613 |             'subscription.created',
  614 |             'subscription.updated',
  615 |             'subscription.active',
  616 |             'benefit_grant.created',
  617 |             'benefit_grant.updated',
  618 |         ], true);
  619 |     }

  621 |     /**
  622 |      * Usar fecha de periodo de suscripción en payload (si existe) para caducidad del addon.
  623 |      */
  624 |     private static function polarSubscriptionLikeEvent(string $eventType): bool
  625 |     {
  626 |         return in_array($eventType, [
  627 |             'subscription.created',
  628 |             'subscription.updated',
  629 |             'subscription.active',
  630 |         ], true);
  631 |     }

  633 |     /**
  634 |      * order.paid implica pago; si Polar envía `status`, debe ser un estado pagado.
  635 |      *
  636 |      * @param array<string, mixed> $data
  637 |      */
  638 |     private static function isOrderPaidState(array $data): bool
  639 |     {
  640 |         $status = $data['status'] ?? null;
  641 |         if ($status === null || $status === '') {
  642 |             return true;
  643 |         }
  644 |         if (!is_string($status)) {
  645 |             return true;
  646 |         }
  647 |         $s = strtolower(trim($status));
  648 | 
  649 |         return in_array($s, ['paid', 'complete', 'completed', 'succeeded', 'success'], true);
  650 |     }

  652 |     /**
  653 |      * @param array<string, mixed> $data
  654 |      */
  655 |     private static function extractOrderIdForLog(array $data): string
  656 |     {
  657 |         foreach (['id', 'order_id', 'number'] as $k) {
  658 |             if (isset($data[$k]) && (is_string($data[$k]) || is_int($data[$k]))) {
  659 |                 $v = trim((string) $data[$k]);
  660 |                 if ($v !== '') {
  661 |                     return $v;
  662 |                 }
  663 |             }
  664 |         }
  665 | 
  666 |         return 'desconocido';
  667 |     }

  669 |     /**
  670 |      * Suscripción recién creada: solo cumplir Core/Addon si el estado es activo (o equivalente).
  671 |      *
  672 |      * @param array<string, mixed> $data
  673 |      */
  674 |     private static function isSubscriptionInitiallyActive(array $data): bool
  675 |     {

  677 |     }

  679 |     /**
  680 |      * Estado actual de suscripción (created / updated).
  681 |      *
  682 |      * @param array<string, mixed> $data
  683 |      */
  684 |     private static function isSubscriptionActiveOrTrialing(array $data): bool
  685 |     {
  686 |         $sub = isset($data['subscription']) && is_array($data['subscription']) ? $data['subscription'] : $data;
  687 |         $status = $sub['status'] ?? $data['status'] ?? null;
  688 |         if ($status === null || $status === '') {
  689 |             return true;
  690 |         }
  691 |         if (!is_string($status)) {
  692 |             return true;
  693 |         }
  694 |         $s = strtolower(trim($status));
  695 | 
  696 |         return in_array($s, ['active', 'trialing'], true);
  697 |     }

  699 |     /**
  700 |      * @param array<string, mixed> $data
  701 |      */
  702 |     private static function extractSubscriptionStatus(array $data): string
  703 |     {
  704 |         $sub = isset($data['subscription']) && is_array($data['subscription']) ? $data['subscription'] : $data;
  705 |         $status = $sub['status'] ?? $data['status'] ?? null;
  706 | 
  707 |         return is_string($status) ? $status : '';
  708 |     }
  709 | 
  710 |     /**
  711 |      * @param array<string, mixed> $meta
  712 |      * @param array<string, mixed> $data
  713 |      */
  714 |     private static function resolveLicenseKey(string $email, array $meta, array $data, string $polarFromPayload): string
  715 |     {
  716 |         $fromMeta = self::metadataString($meta, ['license_key', 'xabia_license_key', 'digixop_license_key']);
  717 |         if ($fromMeta !== '' && LicenseRepository::findAllByLicenseKey($fromMeta) !== []) {
  718 |             LicenseRepository::touchBillingEmailForLicenseKey($fromMeta, $email);
  719 | 
  720 |             return $fromMeta;
  721 |         }
  722 |         $existing = LicenseRepository::findLicenseKeyByBillingEmail($email);
  723 |         if ($existing !== null) {
  724 |             return $existing;
  725 |         }
  726 |         $polar = $polarFromPayload;
  727 |         if ($polar === '' && $fromMeta !== '' && PolarLicenseKey::isValidFormat($fromMeta)) {
  728 |             $polar = PolarLicenseKey::normalize($fromMeta);
  729 |         }
  730 |         if ($polar === '') {
  731 |             return '';
  732 |         }
  733 |         $domain = self::resolveClientDomain($meta, $data);
  734 |         $id = LicenseRepository::createLicenseWithWalletFromPolar($email, $domain, $polar);
  735 |         if ($id < 1) {
  736 |             return '';
  737 |         }
  738 |         error_log('[xabia-polar] auto_created_license email=' . $email . ' domain=' . $domain . ' key=' . $polar);
  739 | 
  740 |         return $polar;
  741 |     }
  742 | 
  743 |     private static function isPolarLicenseKeyString(string $s): bool
  744 |     {
  745 |         return PolarLicenseKey::isValidFormat($s);
  746 |     }
  747 | 
  748 |     /**
  749 |      * Clave Polar XABIA--… en payload (license_id/benefit_id, benefits[], metadata o recorrido profundo).
  750 |      *
  751 |      * @param array<string, mixed> $meta
  752 |      * @param array<string, mixed> $data
  753 |      */
  754 |     private static function extractPolarLicenseKeyForWebhook(array $meta, array $data): string
  755 |     {
  756 |         foreach (['license_id', 'benefit_id'] as $k) {
  757 |             if (isset($data[$k]) && is_string($data[$k]) && self::isPolarLicenseKeyString($data[$k])) {
  758 |                 return trim($data[$k]);
  759 |             }
  760 |         }
  761 |         foreach (['benefits', 'benefit_grants', 'license_keys'] as $block) {
  762 |             if (!isset($data[$block]) || !is_array($data[$block])) {
  763 |                 continue;
  764 |             }
  765 |             foreach ($data[$block] as $item) {
  766 |                 if (!is_array($item)) {
  767 |                     continue;
  768 |                 }
  769 |                 foreach (['license_id', 'benefit_id', 'id', 'key', 'license_key'] as $ik) {
  770 |                     if (isset($item[$ik]) && is_string($item[$ik]) && self::isPolarLicenseKeyString($item[$ik])) {
  771 |                         return trim($item[$ik]);
  772 |                     }
  773 |                 }
  774 |             }
  775 |         }
  776 |         foreach (['license_key', 'xabia_license_key', 'digixop_license_key'] as $mk) {
  777 |             if (isset($meta[$mk]) && is_string($meta[$mk]) && self::isPolarLicenseKeyString($meta[$mk])) {
  778 |                 return trim($meta[$mk]);
  779 |             }
  780 |         }
  781 |         $found = self::scanValueForPolarLicenseKey($meta, 0);
  782 |         if ($found !== '') {
  783 |             return $found;
  784 |         }
  785 | 
  786 |         return self::scanValueForPolarLicenseKey($data, 0);
  787 |     }
  788 | 
  789 |     /**
  790 |      * @param mixed $value
  791 |      */
  792 |     private static function scanValueForPolarLicenseKey($value, int $depth): string
  793 |     {
  794 |         if ($depth > 8) {
  795 |             return '';
  796 |         }
  797 |         if (is_string($value)) {
  798 |             $t = trim($value);
  799 |             if (self::isPolarLicenseKeyString($t)) {
  800 |                 return $t;
  801 |             }
  802 |             $extracted = PolarLicenseKey::extractFromText($t);
  803 | 
  804 |             return $extracted !== '' ? $extracted : '';
  805 |         }
  806 |         if (!is_array($value)) {
  807 |             return '';
  808 |         }
  809 |         foreach ($value as $v) {
  810 |             $f = self::scanValueForPolarLicenseKey($v, $depth + 1);
  811 |             if ($f !== '') {
  812 |                 return $f;
  813 |             }
  814 |         }
  815 | 
  816 |         return '';
  817 |     }
  818 | 
  819 |     /**
  820 |      * @param array<string, mixed> $meta
  821 |      * @param array<string, mixed> $data
  822 |      */
  823 |     private static function resolveClientDomain(array $meta, array $data): string
  824 |     {
  825 |         $raw = self::metadataString($meta, ['client_domain', 'site_url', 'domain', 'site']);
  826 |         if ($raw === '' && isset($data['metadata']) && is_array($data['metadata'])) {
  827 |             $raw = self::metadataString($data['metadata'], ['client_domain', 'site_url', 'domain', 'site']);
  828 |         }
  829 |         $host = Domain::normalize($raw);
  830 |         if ($host !== '') {
  831 |             return $host;
  832 |         }
  833 | 
  834 |         return 'pending.invalid';
  835 |     }
  836 | 
  837 |     /**
  838 |      * @param array<string, mixed> $data
  839 |      */
  840 |     private static function extractCustomerEmail(array $data): string
  841 |     {
  842 |         foreach (['customer_email', 'email'] as $k) {
  843 |             if (isset($data[$k]) && is_string($data[$k])) {
  844 |                 $e = strtolower(trim($data[$k]));
  845 |                 if ($e !== '') {
  846 |                     return $e;
  847 |                 }
  848 |             }
  849 |         }
  850 |         if (isset($data['customer']) && is_array($data['customer'])) {
  851 |             $c = $data['customer'];
  852 |             if (isset($c['email']) && is_string($c['email'])) {
  853 |                 $e = strtolower(trim($c['email']));
  854 |                 if ($e !== '') {
  855 |                     return $e;
  856 |                 }
  857 |             }
  858 |         }
  859 |         foreach (['user', 'grantee'] as $gk) {
  860 |             if (isset($data[$gk]) && is_array($data[$gk]) && isset($data[$gk]['email']) && is_string($data[$gk]['email'])) {
  861 |                 $e = strtolower(trim($data[$gk]['email']));
  862 |                 if ($e !== '') {
  863 |                     return $e;
  864 |                 }
  865 |             }
  866 |         }
  867 |         if (isset($data['order']) && is_array($data['order'])) {
  868 |             $o = $data['order'];
  869 |             if (isset($o['customer_email']) && is_string($o['customer_email'])) {
  870 |                 $e = strtolower(trim($o['customer_email']));
  871 |                 if ($e !== '') {
  872 |                     return $e;
  873 |                 }
  874 |             }
  875 |             if (isset($o['customer']) && is_array($o['customer']) && isset($o['customer']['email']) && is_string($o['customer']['email'])) {
  876 |                 $e = strtolower(trim($o['customer']['email']));
  877 |                 if ($e !== '') {
  878 |                     return $e;
  879 |                 }
  880 |             }
  881 |         }
  882 | 
  883 |         return '';
  884 |     }
  885 | 
  886 |     /**
  887 |      * @param array<string, mixed> $data
  888 |      * @return array<string, mixed>
  889 |      */
  890 |     private static function extractMetadata(array $data): array
  891 |     {
  892 |         $out = [];
  893 |         foreach (['metadata', 'custom_data', 'customer_metadata', 'meta'] as $k) {
  894 |             if (isset($data[$k]) && is_array($data[$k])) {
  895 |                 $out = array_merge($out, $data[$k]);
  896 |             }
  897 |         }
  898 | 
  899 |         return $out;
  900 |     }
  901 | 
  902 |     /**
  903 |      * @param array<string, mixed>    $meta
  904 |      * @param list<string> $keys
  905 |      */
  906 |     private static function metadataString(array $meta, array $keys): string
  907 |     {
  908 |         foreach ($keys as $k) {
  909 |             if (isset($meta[$k]) && is_string($meta[$k])) {
  910 |                 $s = trim($meta[$k]);
  911 |                 if ($s !== '') {
  912 |                     return $s;
  913 |                 }
  914 |             }
  915 |         }
  916 | 
  917 |         return '';
  918 |     }
  919 | 
  920 |     /**
  921 |      * @param array<string, mixed> $data
  922 |      * @return list<string>
  923 |      */
  924 |     private static function extractPolarProductIds(array $data): array
  925 |     {
  926 |         $out = [];
  927 |         $add = static function (mixed $raw) use (&$out): void {
  928 |             if ($raw === null || (!is_string($raw) && !is_int($raw) && !is_float($raw))) {
  929 |                 return;
  930 |             }
  931 |             $id = trim((string) $raw);
  932 |             if ($id === '') {
  933 |                 return;
  934 |             }
  935 |             if (str_starts_with($id, 'prod_')) {
  936 |                 $out[] = strtolower($id);
  937 | 
  938 |                 return;
  939 |             }
  940 |             if (preg_match('/^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i', $id) === 1) {
  941 |                 $out[] = 'prod_' . strtolower($id);
  942 |             }
  943 |         };
  944 | 
  945 |         /**
  946 |          * Recorrido acotado: Polar anida product_id / product.id (p. ej. bajo price, checkout, customer).
  947 |          *
  948 |          * @param array<string, mixed> $node
  949 |          */
  950 |         $walk = static function (array $node, int $depth) use (&$walk, $add): void {
  951 |             if ($depth > 16) {
  952 |                 return;
  953 |             }
  954 |             foreach ($node as $k => $v) {
  955 |                 if ($k === 'product_id') {
  956 |                     $add($v);
  957 |                 }
  958 |                 if ($k === 'product' && is_array($v) && array_key_exists('id', $v)) {
  959 |                     $add($v['id']);
  960 |                 }
  961 |                 if (is_array($v)) {
  962 |                     $walk($v, $depth + 1);
  963 |                 }
  964 |             }
  965 |         };
  966 | 
  967 |         $walk($data, 0);
  968 | 
  969 |         return array_values(array_unique($out));
  970 |     }
  971 | 
  972 |     private static function addonExpiryDateOneYear(): string
  973 |     {
  974 |         $utc = new DateTimeImmutable('now', new DateTimeZone('UTC'));
  975 | 
  976 |         return $utc->add(new DateInterval('P1Y'))->format('Y-m-d');
  977 |     }
  978 | 
  979 |     /**
  980 |      * Fecha de fin de periodo de la suscripción Polar (si viene en el payload); si no, +1 año.
  981 |      *
  982 |      * @param array<string, mixed> $data
  983 |      */
  984 |     private static function addonExpiryFromPolarSubscriptionPayload(array $data): string
  985 |     {
  986 |         $sub = isset($data['subscription']) && is_array($data['subscription']) ? $data['subscription'] : $data;
  987 |         foreach (['current_period_end', 'current_period_end_at', 'ends_at', 'end_at'] as $k) {
  988 |             if (!isset($sub[$k])) {
  989 |                 continue;
  990 |             }
  991 |             $v = $sub[$k];
  992 |             if (is_int($v)) {
  993 |                 if ($v > 0) {
  994 |                     return gmdate('Y-m-d', $v);
  995 |                 }
  996 | 
  997 |                 continue;
  998 |             }
  999 |             if (is_string($v) && trim($v) !== '') {
 1000 |                 $ts = strtotime($v);
 1001 |                 if ($ts !== false) {
 1002 |                     return gmdate('Y-m-d', $ts);
 1003 |                 }
 1004 |             }
 1005 |         }
 1006 | 
 1007 |         return self::addonExpiryDateOneYear();
 1008 |     }
 1009 | }

  195 |             $all

  203 |                 $summary,

  205 |                     'source'        => 'signed_hub_post',
  206 |                     'enforcer_code' => $recon['code'],
  207 |                 ]

  215 |                 'registered_domain'  => $summary,

   11 |     public static function handle(): void
   12 |     {
   13 |         if (($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] ?? '') !== 'POST') {
   14 |             Json::respond(405, ['error' => ['message' => 'Method Not Allowed', 'type' => 'method']]);
   15 | 
   16 |             return;
   17 |         }
   18 |         $rawBody = (string) file_get_contents('php://input');
   19 |         $ctx = SignedHubPostAuth::validate($rawBody);
   20 |         if ($ctx === null) {
   21 |             return;
   22 |         }
   23 |         $licenseKey = $ctx['license_key'];
   24 |         $sourceUrl = $ctx['source_url'];
   25 |         $row = $ctx['row'];
   26 |         $billingLicenseId = $ctx['billing_license_id'];
   27 |

   29 |         if ($remaining <= 0) {
   30 |             self::respond402();
   31 | 
   32 |             return;
   33 |         }
   34 | 
   35 |         $input = $rawBody === '' ? [] : json_decode($rawBody, true);

   37 |             Json::respond(400, ['error' => ['message' => 'JSON inválido', 'type' => 'invalid_request']]);
   38 | 
   39 |             return;
   40 |         }
   41 | 
   42 |         $route = OpenAiForwarder::resolveOpenAiRoute($input);
   43 |         if ($route === null) {
   44 |             Json::respond(400, [
   45 |                 'error' => [

   47 |                     'type'    => 'invalid_request',
   48 |                 ],
   49 |             ]);
   50 | 
   51 |             return;
   52 |         }

   60 |         }
   61 | 
   62 |         // Idioma preferido del cliente (p. ej. BCP-47 desde document.documentElement.lang); lo consume VertexForwarder.

   65 |         }

   98 |                     ['vertex_http' => $code, 'upstream_raw' => substr($up['raw'] ?? '', 0, 500)]
   99 |                 );

  101 |                 // Evitar bloquear la respuesta por fallo de logging.
  102 |             }

  140 |                         [
  141 |                             'upstream_http' => $code,
  142 |                             'plan_type'   => substr((string) ($row['plan_type'] ?? ''), 0, 64),
  143 |                             'vertex_tier' => $isIaLite ? 'ia_lite' : $vertexTier,
  144 |                             'note'        => $isIaLite ? 'Respuesta resuelta por IA-Lite Avirato sin llamada a Vertex' : 'Respuesta sin usage total_tokens',
  145 |                         ]
  146 |                     );
  147 |                 } catch (\Throwable) {
  148 |                     // Evitar bloquear la respuesta por fallo de logging.
  149 |                 }
  150 |                 $decoded['tokens_remaining'] = $remaining;
  151 |             }
  152 |             if ($expIso !== null) {
  153 |                 $decoded['expiry_date'] = $expIso;
  154 |             }
  155 |         } else {
  156 |             try {
  157 |                 $errMsg = '';
  158 |                 if (is_array($decoded) && isset($decoded['error']['message']) && is_string($decoded['error']['message'])) {
  159 |                     $errMsg = $decoded['error']['message'];
  160 |                 }
  161 |                 WalletRepository::logUsage(
  162 |                     $billingLicenseId,
  163 |                     $activityType . '_error',
  164 |                     max(0, $usageTokens),
  165 |                     $sourceUrl,
  166 |                     null,
  167 |                     [
  168 |                         'vertex_http'  => $code,
  169 |                         'plan_type'    => substr((string) ($row['plan_type'] ?? ''), 0, 64),
  170 |                         'vertex_tier'  => $vertexTier,
  171 |                         'error_message'=> substr($errMsg, 0, 500),
  172 |                     ]
  173 |                 );
  174 |             } catch (\Throwable) {
  175 |                 // Evitar bloquear la respuesta por fallo de logging.
  176 |             }
  177 |             $decoded['tokens_remaining'] = $remaining;
  178 |             if ($expIso !== null) {
  179 |                 $decoded['expiry_date'] = $expIso;
  180 |             }
  181 |         }
  182 | 
  183 |         http_response_code($code);
  184 |         header('Content-Type: application/json; charset=utf-8');
  185 |         echo json_encode($decoded, JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE | JSON_UNESCAPED_SLASHES);
  186 |     }

  194 |                 'code'    => 'insufficient_balance',
  195 |             ],
  196 |             'digixop_insufficient_balance' => true,
  197 |         ]);
  198 |     }

  203 |     /**
  204 |      * @param array<string, mixed> $input
  205 |      */
  206 |     private static function inputRequestsFederationTool(array $input): bool
  207 |     {
  208 |         $tools = $input['tools'] ?? null;
  209 |         if (!is_array($tools) || $tools === []) {
  210 |             return false;
  211 |         }
  212 |         foreach ($tools as $t) {
  213 |             if (!is_array($t) || ($t['type'] ?? '') !== 'function') {
  214 |                 continue;
  215 |             }
  216 |             $fn = $t['function'] ?? null;
  217 |             if (!is_array($fn)) {
  218 |                 continue;
  219 |             }
  220 |             if (($fn['name'] ?? '') === 'ask_federated_node') {
  221 |                 return true;
  222 |             }
  223 |         }
  224 | 
  225 |         return false;
  226 |     }

  228 |     /**

  232 |     private static function injectFederatedMasterSystemInstruction(array $messages): array
  233 |     {
  234 |         $snippet = 'Eres el nodo maestro de una red federada. Tienes permiso para consultar otros nodos usando la herramienta ask_federated_node si la respuesta no está en tu conocimiento local.   240 |                 $m['content'] = $prev === '' ? $snippet : $prev . "\n\n" . $snippet;

  244 |         }
  245 |         if (!$done) {
  246 |             array_unshift($out, ['role' => 'system', 'content' => $snippet]);
  247 |         }
  248 | 
  249 |         return $out;
  250 |     }

  252 |     private static function normalizeUserLang(mixed $raw): string

  282 |         $roomFilter = self::firstScalar([

  300 |     /**
  301 |      * @param list<mixed> $values
  302 |      */
  303 |     private static function firstScalar(array $values): string
  304 |     {
  305 |         foreach ($values as $value) {
  306 |             if (!is_scalar($value)) {
  307 |                 continue;
  308 |             }
  309 |             $value = trim((string) $value);
  310 |             if ($value !== '') {
  311 |                 return $value;
  312 |             }
  313 |         }
  314 | 
  315 |         return '';
  316 |     }

  318 |     /**
  319 |      * @param array<string, mixed> $decoded
  320 |      */
  321 |     private static function extractUsageTotalTokens(array $decoded): int
  322 |     {
  323 |         $u = $decoded['usage'] ?? null;
  324 |         if (!is_array($u)) {
  325 |             return 0;
  326 |         }
  327 |         $total = (int) ($u['total_tokens'] ?? 0);
  328 |         if ($total > 0) {
  329 |             return $total;
  330 |         }
  331 |         $p = (int) ($u['prompt_tokens'] ?? 0);
  332 |         $c = (int) ($u['completion_tokens'] ?? 0);
  333 | 
  334 |         return $p + $c;
  335 |     }

    5 | namespace XabiaCentral;

    9 | /**
   10 |  * Cartera y log de uso del Hub (SaaS). La tabla `xabia_usage_log` debe ser la del
   11 |  * esquema central (license_id, activity_type, tokens_count, …). No usar el modelo
   12 |  * del plugin WordPress en la misma tabla física; ver central-api/migrations/ y
   13 |  * HUB_DATABASE_ISOLATION.md si WP y el Hub comparten MySQL.
   14 |  */
   15 | final class WalletRepository
   16 | {
   17 |     /**
   18 |      * Inserta un registro en xabia_usage_log (esquema Hub) sin modificar saldo.
   19 |      */
   20 |     public static function logUsage(int $licenseId, string $activityType, int $tokens, ?string $sourceUrl, ?string $projectId, ?array $meta): bool
   21 |     {
   22 |         $pdo = Db::pdo();
   23 |         $log = $pdo->prepare(
   24 |             'INSERT INTO xabia_usage_log (license_id, activity_type, tokens_count, source_url, project_id, meta_json, `timestamp`)
   25 |              VALUES (:lid, :act, :cnt, :src, :pid, :meta, UTC_TIMESTAMP())'
   26 |         );
   27 | 
   28 |         return $log->execute([
   29 |             ':lid'  => $licenseId,
   30 |             ':act'  => substr($activityType, 0, 64),
   31 |             ':cnt'  => max(0, $tokens),
   32 |             ':src'  => $sourceUrl !== null && $sourceUrl !== '' ? substr($sourceUrl, 0, 512) : null,
   33 |             ':pid'  => $projectId !== null && $projectId !== '' ? substr($projectId, 0, 191) : null,
   34 |             ':meta' => $meta !== null && $meta !== [] ? json_encode($meta, JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE) : null,
   35 |         ]);
   36 |     }
   37 | 
   38 |     /**
   39 |      * Descuenta tokens (no negativos en saldo). Devuelve el nuevo tokens_remaining o null si falla.
   40 |      */
   41 |     public static function deduct(int $licenseId, int $tokens, string $activityType, ?string $sourceUrl, ?string $projectId, ?array $meta): ?int
   42 |     {
   43 |         if ($tokens < 1) {
   44 |             return self::getRemaining($licenseId);
   45 |         }
   46 |         $pdo = Db::pdo();
   47 |         $pdo->beginTransaction();
   48 |         try {
   49 |             $st = $pdo->prepare('SELECT tokens_remaining FROM xabia_wallets WHERE license_id = :id FOR UPDATE');
   50 |             $st->execute([':id' => $licenseId]);
   51 |             $row = $st->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
   52 |             if ($row === false) {
   53 |                 $pdo->rollBack();
   54 | 
   55 |                 return null;
   56 |             }
   57 |             $remaining = (int) $row['tokens_remaining'];
   58 |             $newRemaining = max(0, $remaining - $tokens);
   59 |             $st2 = $pdo->prepare(
   60 |                 'UPDATE xabia_wallets SET
   61 |                     tokens_remaining = :rem,
   62 |                     tokens_used_total = tokens_used_total + :used
   63 |                  WHERE license_id = :id'
   64 |             );
   65 |             $st2->execute([
   66 |                 ':rem'  => $newRemaining,
   67 |                 ':used' => $tokens,
   68 |                 ':id'   => $licenseId,
   69 |             ]);
   70 |             self::logUsage($licenseId, $activityType, $tokens, $sourceUrl, $projectId, $meta);
   71 |             $pdo->commit();
   72 | 
   73 |             return $newRemaining;
   74 |         } catch (\Throwable $e) {
   75 |             if ($pdo->inTransaction()) {
   76 |                 $pdo->rollBack();
   77 |             }
   78 |             throw $e;
   79 |         }
   80 |     }
   81 | 
   82 |     public static function getRemaining(int $licenseId): ?int
   83 |     {
   84 |         $st = Db::pdo()->prepare('SELECT tokens_remaining FROM xabia_wallets WHERE license_id = :id');
   85 |         $st->execute([':id' => $licenseId]);
   86 |         $row = $st->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
   87 |         if ($row === false) {
   88 |             return null;
   89 |         }
   90 | 
   91 |         return (int) $row['tokens_remaining'];
   92 |     }
   93 | }

    7 | /**
    8 |  * Reportes del plugin (embeddings batch, etc.). Valida licencia en xabia_licenses,
    9 |  * descuenta xabia_wallets por id numérico y registra filas en xabia_usage_log (esquema Hub).
   10 |  */
   11 | final class UsageReportHandler
   12 | {
   13 |     public static function handle(): void
   14 |     {
   15 |         if (($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] ?? '') !== 'POST') {
   16 |             Json::respond(405, ['error' => ['message' => 'Method Not Allowed', 'type' => 'method']]);
   17 | 
   18 |             return;
   19 |         }
   20 |         $input = Json::readBody();
   21 |         if ($input === null) {
   22 |             Json::respond(400, ['ok' => false, 'message' => 'JSON inválido']);
   23 | 
   24 |             return;
   25 |         }
   26 |         $key = trim((string) ($input['license_key'] ?? ''));
   27 |         $siteUrl = trim((string) ($input['site_url'] ?? ''));
   28 |         if ($key === '' || $siteUrl === '') {
   29 |             Json::respond(400, ['ok' => false, 'message' => 'Faltan license_key o site_url']);
   30 | 
   31 |             return;
   32 |         }
   33 | 
   34 |         $recon = LicenseDomainEnforcer::reconcileClaimedWithHeaders($siteUrl);
   35 |         if (!$recon['ok']) {
   36 |             self::respondUsageUnauthorizedFromEnforcer($key, $siteUrl, $recon);
   37 | 
   38 |             return;
   39 |         }
   40 |         $siteUrl = $recon['effective_claim'];
   41 | 
   42 |         $all = LicenseRepository::findAllByLicenseKey($key);
   43 |         if ($all === []) {
   44 |             Json::respond(403, ['ok' => false, 'message' => 'Licencia inválida']);
   45 | 
   46 |             return;
   47 |         }
   48 | 
   49 |         $domainRow = null;
   50 |         foreach ($all as $r) {
   51 |             if (!is_array($r)) {
   52 |                 continue;
   53 |             }
   54 |             if (LicenseRepository::domainMatchesLicense($r, $siteUrl)) {
   55 |                 $domainRow = $r;
   56 | 
   57 |                 break;
   58 |             }
   59 |         }
   60 | 
   61 |         if ($domainRow === null) {
   62 |             $regs = array_values(array_unique(array_filter(array_map(
   63 |                 static fn (array $r): string => trim((string) ($r['client_domain'] ?? '')),
   64 |                 $all
   65 |             ))));
   66 |             SecurityLog::licenseUnauthorizedDomain(
   67 |                 $key,
   68 |                 Domain::normalize($siteUrl),
   69 |                 $siteUrl,
   70 |                 $regs[0] ?? '',
   71 |                 ['source' => 'usage_report']
   72 |             );
   73 |             Json::respond(403, [
   74 |                 'ok'                   => false,
   75 |                 'message'              => 'Dominio no autorizado para esta licencia',
   76 |                 'reason'               => 'unauthorized_domain',
   77 |                 'registered_domain'    => $regs[0] ?? '',
   78 |                 'registered_domains'   => $regs,
   79 |             ]);
   80 | 
   81 |             return;
   82 |         }
   83 | 
   84 |         if (LicenseRepository::isSuspended($domainRow)) {
   85 |             Json::respond(403, [
   86 |                 'ok'      => false,
   87 |                 'message' => 'La licencia está suspendida.',
   88 |                 'reason'  => 'license_suspended',
   89 |             ]);
   90 | 
   91 |             return;
   92 |         }
   93 | 
   94 |         $row = LicenseRepository::findActiveWithWalletByKey($key, $siteUrl);
   95 |         if ($row !== null && LicenseRepository::isSuspended($row)) {
   96 |             Json::respond(403, [
   97 |                 'ok'      => false,
   98 |                 'message' => 'La licencia está suspendida.',
   99 |                 'reason'  => 'license_suspended',
  100 |             ]);
  101 | 
  102 |             return;
  103 |         }
  104 | 
  105 |         if (!LicenseRepository::allowsHubTokenConsumption($domainRow, $row)) {
  106 |             Json::respond(403, ['ok' => false, 'message' => 'Licencia inválida']);
  107 | 
  108 |             return;
  109 |         }
  110 | 
  111 |         if ($row === null) {
  112 |             Json::respond(403, ['ok' => false, 'message' => 'Licencia inválida']);
  113 | 
  114 |             return;
  115 |         }
  116 | 
  117 |         $pt = (int) ($input['prompt_tokens'] ?? 0);
  118 |         $ct = (int) ($input['completion_tokens'] ?? 0);
  119 |         $total = (int) ($input['total_tokens'] ?? 0);
  120 |         if ($total < 1) {
  121 |             $total = $pt + $ct;
  122 |         }
  123 |         if ($total < 1) {
  124 |             Json::respond(200, [
  125 |                 'ok'                => true,
  126 |                 'tokens_remaining'  => (int) $row['tokens_remaining'],
  127 |                 'expiry_date'       => LicenseRepository::expiryAsIso($row['expiry_date'] !== null ? (string) $row['expiry_date'] : null),
  128 |                 'message'           => 'Sin tokens a descontar',
  129 |             ]);
  130 | 
  131 |             return;
  132 |         }
  133 | 
  134 |         $context = (string) ($input['context'] ?? 'usage_report');
  135 |         $projectId = isset($input['project_id']) ? (string) $input['project_id'] : null;
  136 |         $billingLicenseId = (int) ($row['wallet_license_id'] ?? $row['id'] ?? 0);
  137 |         if ($billingLicenseId < 1) {
  138 |             $billingLicenseId = (int) $row['id'];
  139 |         }
  140 |         $after = WalletRepository::deduct(
  141 |             $billingLicenseId,
  142 |             $total,
  143 |             substr($context, 0, 64),
  144 |             $siteUrl,
  145 |             $projectId,
  146 |             ['plugin_version' => (string) ($input['plugin_version'] ?? '')]
  147 |         );
  148 |         if ($after === null) {
  149 |             Json::respond(500, ['ok' => false, 'message' => 'Error al actualizar saldo']);
  150 | 
  151 |             return;
  152 |         }
  153 | 
  154 |         Json::respond(200, [
  155 |             'ok'               => true,
  156 |             'tokens_remaining' => $after,
  157 |             'expiry_date'      => LicenseRepository::expiryAsIso($row['expiry_date'] !== null ? (string) $row['expiry_date'] : null),
  158 |         ]);
  159 |     }

  176 |                 $originalSiteUrl,
  177 |                 $summary,
  178 |                 [
  179 |                     'source'        => 'usage_report',
  180 |                     'enforcer_code' => $recon['code'],
  181 |                 ]
  182 |             );
  183 |         }
  184 |         Json::respond(403, [
  185 |             'ok'                  => false,
  186 |             'message'             => 'Dominio no autorizado para esta licencia',
  187 |             'reason'              => 'unauthorized_domain',
  188 |             'registered_domain'   => $summary,
  189 |             'registered_domains'  => $regs,

    1 | <?php
    2 | 
    3 | declare(strict_types=1);
    4 | 
    5 | namespace XabiaCentral;
    6 | 
    7 | /**
    8 |  * Firma HMAC de peticiones POST desde WordPress (misma clave que el proxy).
    9 |  */
   10 | final class HubClientSignature
   11 | {
   12 |     public static function verify(string $rawBody, string $licenseKey, string $sourceUrl, string $secret): bool
   13 |     {
   14 |         if ($secret === '') {
   15 |             return false;
   16 |         }
   17 |         $timestamp = trim((string) ($_SERVER['HTTP_X_XABIA_TIMESTAMP'] ?? ''));
   18 |         $signature = trim((string) ($_SERVER['HTTP_X_XABIA_SIGNATURE'] ?? ''));
   19 |         if ($timestamp === '' || $signature === '') {
   20 |             return false;
   21 |         }
   22 |         $ts = is_numeric($timestamp) ? (int) $timestamp : 0;
   23 |         if ($ts < 1 || abs(time() - $ts) > 15 * 60) {
   24 |             return false;
   25 |         }
   26 |         $payload = $licenseKey . "\n" . $sourceUrl . "\n" . $timestamp . "\n" . $rawBody;
   27 |         $expected = hash_hmac('sha256', $payload, $secret);
   28 |         $candidate = str_starts_with($signature, 'sha256=') ? substr($signature, 7) : $signature;
   29 | 
   30 |         return hash_equals($expected, $candidate);
   31 |     }
   32 | }

Líneas totales (aprox.): 49

--- PRIMERAS 49 LÍNEAS (1–49) ---
    1 | <?php
    2 | 
    3 | declare(strict_types=1);
    4 | 
    5 | namespace XabiaCentral;
    6 | 
    7 | final class Domain
    8 | {
    9 |     /**
   10 |      * Normaliza URL completa o host a hostname en minúsculas (sin puerto opcional se conserva host).
   11 |      */
   12 |     public static function normalize(string $urlOrHost): string
   13 |     {
   14 |         $s = trim($urlOrHost);
   15 |         if ($s === '') {
   16 |             return '';
   17 |         }
   18 |         if (preg_match('#^https?://#i', $s) === 1) {
   19 |             $host = parse_url($s, PHP_URL_HOST);
   20 |             return $host !== null && $host !== false ? strtolower($host) : '';
   21 |         }
   22 |         $s = preg_replace('#/.*$#', '', $s) ?? $s;
   23 |         $s = preg_replace('#:\d+$#', '', $s) ?? $s;
   24 | 
   25 |         return strtolower($s);
   26 |     }
   27 | 
   28 |     public static function domainsMatch(string $registered, string $incoming): bool
   29 |     {
   30 |         $stripWww = static function (string $h): string {
   31 |             return str_starts_with($h, 'www.') ? substr($h, 4) : $h;
   32 |         };
   33 | 
   34 |         $a = $stripWww(self::normalize($registered));
   35 |         $b = $stripWww(self::normalize($incoming));
   36 |         if ($a === '' || $b === '') {
   37 |             return false;
   38 |         }
   39 |         if ($a === $b) {
   40 |             return true;
   41 |         }
   42 |         // Licencia de “sitio” (apex): cualquier subdominio del host registrado cuenta como el mismo dominio.
   43 |         if (str_ends_with($b, '.' . $a)) {
   44 |             return true;
   45 |         }
   46 | 
   47 |         return false;
   48 |     }
   49 | }

   18 |         }

   22 |         if ($dsn === '') {

   24 |         }

   26 |             PDO::ATTR_ERRMODE            => PDO::ERRMODE_EXCEPTION,
   27 |             PDO::ATTR_DEFAULT_FETCH_MODE => PDO::FETCH_ASSOC,
   28 |         ]);
   29 |

   31 |     }

    1 | <?php
    2 | 
    3 | declare(strict_types=1);
    4 | 
    5 | namespace XabiaCentral;
    6 | 
    7 | /**
    8 |  * Lectura de variables de entorno compatible con hostings donde {@see putenv()} está deshabilitado:
    9 |  * {@see bootstrap.php} rellena $_ENV desde .env, pero {@see getenv()} puede seguir vacío.
   10 |  */
   11 | final class Env
   12 | {
   13 |     public static function str(string $key, string $default = ''): string
   14 |     {
   15 |         if (isset($_ENV[$key]) && is_string($_ENV[$key])) {
   16 |             $v = trim($_ENV[$key]);
   17 |             if ($v !== '') {
   18 |                 return $v;
   19 |             }
   20 |         }
   21 |         if (isset($_SERVER[$key]) && is_string($_SERVER[$key])) {
   22 |             $v = trim($_SERVER[$key]);
   23 |             if ($v !== '') {
   24 |                 return $v;
   25 |             }
   26 |         }
   27 |         $g = getenv($key);
   28 |         if (is_string($g)) {
   29 |             $v = trim($g);
   30 |             if ($v !== '') {
   31 |                 return $v;
   32 |             }
   33 |         }
   34 | 
   35 |         return $default;
   36 |     }
   37 | }

    1 | <?php
    2 | 
    3 | declare(strict_types=1);
    4 | 
    5 | namespace XabiaCentral;
    6 | 
    7 | /**
    8 |  * Formato de clave emitida por Polar en portal / beneficios (prefijo XABIA--).
    9 |  */
   10 | final class PolarLicenseKey
   11 | {
   12 |     /**
   13 |      * Trim, quita BOM y espacios raros al inicio/final.
   14 |      */
   15 |     public static function normalize(string $key): string
   16 |     {
   17 |         $key = trim($key);
   18 |         if ($key === '') {
   19 |             return '';
   20 |         }
   21 |         $key = preg_replace('/^\x{FEFF}/u', '', $key) ?? $key;
   22 | 
   23 |         return trim($key);
   24 |     }
   25 | 
   26 |     /**
   27 |      * Valida formato tras normalizar. Polar puede usar UUID; permitimos . _ en el sufijo por evolución del proveedor.
   28 |      */
   29 |     public static function isValidFormat(string $key): bool
   30 |     {
   31 |         $key = self::normalize($key);
   32 | 
   33 |         return $key !== '' && preg_match('/^XABIA--[A-Z0-9][A-Z0-9._-]*$/i', $key) === 1;
   34 |     }
   35 | 
   36 |     /**
   37 |      * Extrae la primera coincidencia XABIA--… dentro de un texto más largo (metadata, JSON serializado).
   38 |      */
   39 |     public static function extractFromText(string $haystack): string
   40 |     {
   41 |         $haystack = trim($haystack);
   42 |         if ($haystack === '') {
   43 |             return '';
   44 |         }
   45 |         if (self::isValidFormat($haystack)) {
   46 |             return self::normalize($haystack);
   47 |         }
   48 |         if (preg_match('/\b(XABIA--[A-Z0-9][A-Z0-9._-]*)\b/i', $haystack, $m)) {
   49 |             return self::normalize($m[1]);
   50 |         }
   51 | 
   52 |         return '';
   53 |     }
   54 | }

    1 | <?php

    5 | namespace XabiaCentral;

    7 | /**
    8 |  * Mapeo Polar product.id → lógica interna del hub.
    9 |  *
   10 |  * Añade entradas reales (claves = IDs `prod_…` del dashboard de Polar).
   11 |  * Los UUID de xabia-woo y xabia-smart-qr son marcadores: cámbialos por los del producto al publicarlo en Polar.
   12 |  *
   13 |  * @phpstan-type TokenEntry array{type: 'tokens', amount: int}
   14 |  * @phpstan-type AddonEntry array{type: 'addon', addon_slug: string}
   15 |  * @phpstan-type CoreEntry array{type: 'core', extend_years?: int}
   16 |  */
   17 | final class PolarProductMap
   18 | {
   19 |     /**
   20 |      * @var array<string, array{type: string, amount?: int, addon_slug?: string, extend_years?: int}>
   21 |      */
   22 |     private const ENTRIES = [
   23 |         // --- PACKS DE TOKENS ---
   24 |         'a6e9f15a-f4ac-4bd4-b6f9-c3d9f69ce53e'    => ['type' => 'tokens', 'amount' => 5000000],   // 29€
   25 |         '842d765b-a6cd-4be9-8058-d29a23029884'   => ['type' => 'tokens', 'amount' => 20000000],  // 79€
   26 |         '040d72f1-e302-4f79-81bd-a97987d74635' => ['type' => 'tokens', 'amount' => 100000000], // 249€
   27 | 
   28 |         // --- LICENCIAS CORE ---
   29 |         '80a7bbd7-6d9f-41c4-b6a6-ad9e181cd991'       => ['type' => 'core', 'extend_years' => 1],    // 199€
   30 |         '4db9de15-39e7-4d5b-814f-be8e334d874e' => ['type' => 'core', 'extend_years' => 1],    // 69€/año
   31 | 
   32 |         // --- ADDONS / PLUGINS ---
   33 |         '98a1013f-0439-4428-a1af-0e064d9a352d'   => ['type' => 'addon', 'addon_slug' => 'xabia-avirato'], // 49€/año
   34 |         '8078756b-c566-4557-a55d-3712d8e47c44'  => ['type' => 'addon', 'addon_slug' => 'xabia-mec'], // MEC 49€/año
   35 |         // Woo / Smart QR: sustituir claves por el UUID real del producto en Polar (sin prefijo prod_).
   36 |         'c1f8a2b0-7e3d-4c9a-8b1e-0d2f3a4b5c6d'  => ['type' => 'addon', 'addon_slug' => 'xabia-woo'],
   37 |         'b7e4c9d2-5a8f-41e0-9c3b-6d1e2f4a8b0c'  => ['type' => 'addon', 'addon_slug' => 'xabia-smart-qr'],
   38 |     ];
   39 | 
   40 |     /**
   41 |      * @return array{type: string, amount?: int, addon_slug?: string, extend_years?: int}|null
   42 |      */
   43 |     public static function resolve(string $polarProductId): ?array
   44 |     {
   45 |         $id = trim($polarProductId);
   46 |         if ($id === '') {
   47 |             return null;
   48 |         }
   49 |         // Polar envía product_id como prod_<uuid>; las claves del mapa son el UUID en minúsculas.
   50 |         if (str_starts_with($id, 'prod_')) {
   51 |             $id = substr($id, 5);
   52 |         }
   53 |         $id = strtolower($id);
   54 |         if (!isset(self::ENTRIES[$id])) {
   55 |             return null;
   56 |         }
   57 |         $e = self::ENTRIES[$id];
   58 |         if (!is_array($e) || !isset($e['type']) || !is_string($e['type'])) {
   59 |             return null;
   60 |         }
   61 | 
   62 |         return $e;
   63 |     }
   64 | }

    1 | <?php
    2 | 
    3 | declare(strict_types=1);
    4 | 
    5 | namespace XabiaCentral;
    6 | 
    7 | /**
    8 |  * Verificación de firmas según Standard Webhooks (symmetric / v1, HMAC-SHA256).
    9 |  * Polar: {@link https://polar.sh/docs/integrate/webhooks/delivery}
   10 |  */
   11 | final class StandardWebhooks
   12 | {
   13 |     private const TIMESTAMP_TOLERANCE_SEC = 600;
   14 | 
   15 |     /**
   16 |      * @param array<string, mixed> $server típicamente $_SERVER
   17 |      */
   18 |     public static function verifySymmetricV1(string $rawBody, array $server, string $secretFromEnv): bool
   19 |     {
   20 |         $secretFromEnv = trim($secretFromEnv);
   21 |         if ($secretFromEnv === '') {
   22 |             return false;
   23 |         }
   24 |         $id = self::header($server, 'webhook-id');
   25 |         $ts = self::header($server, 'webhook-timestamp');
   26 |         $sigHeader = self::header($server, 'webhook-signature');
   27 |         if ($id === '' || $ts === '' || $sigHeader === '') {
   28 |             return false;
   29 |         }
   30 |         if (!ctype_digit($ts)) {
   31 |             return false;
   32 |         }
   33 |         $tsInt = (int) $ts;
   34 |         if (abs(time() - $tsInt) > self::TIMESTAMP_TOLERANCE_SEC) {
   35 |             return false;
   36 |         }
   37 |         $keyMaterial = self::decodeSigningSecret($secretFromEnv);
   38 |         if ($keyMaterial === '') {
   39 |             return false;
   40 |         }
   41 |         $signedContent = $id . '.' . $ts . '.' . $rawBody;
   42 |         $mac = hash_hmac('sha256', $signedContent, $keyMaterial, true);
   43 |         if ($mac === false) {
   44 |             return false;
   45 |         }
   46 |         $expectedB64 = base64_encode($mac);
   47 |         foreach (preg_split('/\s+/', trim($sigHeader)) ?: [] as $part) {
   48 |             $part = trim((string) $part);
   49 |             if ($part === '' || !str_starts_with($part, 'v1,')) {
   50 |                 continue;
   51 |             }
   52 |             $their = substr($part, 3);
   53 |             if ($their !== '' && hash_equals($expectedB64, $their)) {
   54 |                 return true;
   55 |             }
   56 |         }
   57 | 
   58 |         return false;
   59 |     }
   60 | 
   61 |     /**
   62 |      * @param array<string, mixed> $server
   63 |      */
   64 |     private static function header(array $server, string $name): string
   65 |     {
   66 |         $key = 'HTTP_' . strtoupper(str_replace('-', '_', $name));
   67 | 
   68 |         return isset($server[$key]) && is_string($server[$key]) ? trim($server[$key]) : '';
   69 |     }
   70 | 
   71 |     private static function decodeSigningSecret(string $secret): string
   72 |     {
   73 |         if (str_starts_with($secret, 'whsec_')) {
   74 |             $b64 = substr($secret, strlen('whsec_'));
   75 |             $raw = base64_decode($b64, true);
   76 | 
   77 |             return $raw !== false ? $raw : '';
   78 |         }
   79 |         $try = base64_decode($secret, true);
   80 | 
   81 |         return ($try !== false && $try !== '') ? $try : $secret;
   82 |     }
   83 | }

    7 | /**
    8 |  * Motor exclusivo Vertex AI (Gemini + text-embedding-004).
    9 |  * Traduce peticiones estilo OpenAI y devuelve JSON compatible con el plugin Xabia.
   10 |  */
   11 | final class VertexForwarder
   12 | {
   13 |     private const EMBEDDING_MODEL = 'text-embedding-004';
   14 | 
   15 |     /**
   16 |      * @param string $vertexTier Nivel en hub: 'pro' o 'flash' (por defecto ambos resuelven a gemini-2.5-flash por coste). Derivado de xabia_licenses.plan_type en ProxyHandler.
   17 |      *
   18 |      * @return array{http_code:int, decoded:?array, raw:string}
   19 |      */
   20 |     public static function forwardOpenAiCompatible(array $input, string $vertexTier): array
   21 |     {
   22 |         $acc = GoogleServiceAccountAuth::loadServiceAccountJson();
   23 |         if ($acc === null) {
   24 |             return self::err(503, 'GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS o GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS_JSON no configurado');
   25 |         }
   26 |         $token = GoogleServiceAccountAuth::fetchAccessToken();
   27 |         if ($token === null) {
   28 |             return self::err(503, 'No se pudo obtener access_token de Google');
   29 |         }
   30 |         $vertexProjectId = Env::str('XABIA_VERTEX_PROJECT_ID');
   31 |         if ($vertexProjectId === '') {
   32 |             $vertexProjectId = $acc['project_id'];
   33 |         }
   34 |         $location = Env::str('XABIA_VERTEX_LOCATION', 'europe-west1');
   35 |         $location = trim($location);
   36 |         if ($location === '') {
   37 |             $location = 'europe-west1';
   38 |         }
   39 |         $locations = self::locationCandidates($location);
   40 | 
   41 |         $usePro = ($vertexTier === 'pro');
   42 |         $flashModel = Env::str('XABIA_VERTEX_MODEL_FLASH', 'gemini-2.5-flash');
   43 |         $proModel = Env::str('XABIA_VERTEX_MODEL_PRO', 'gemini-2.5-flash');
   44 |         $geminiModel = $usePro ? trim((string) $proModel) : trim((string) $flashModel);
   45 |         if ($geminiModel === '') {
   46 |             $geminiModel = 'gemini-2.5-flash';
   47 |         }
   48 |         $models = self::geminiModelCandidates($geminiModel, $usePro);
   49 | 
   50 |         if (isset($input['messages']) && is_array($input['messages'])) {
   51 |             return self::forwardChatWithFallback($vertexProjectId, $locations, $token, $models, $input);
   52 |         }
   53 |         if (isset($input['input']) && isset($input['model'])) {
   54 |             // RGPD: por defecto misma región que el chat (p. ej. europe-west1). Opcional: XABIA_VERTEX_EMBEDDING_LOCATION.
   55 |             $embLoc = trim(Env::str('XABIA_VERTEX_EMBEDDING_LOCATION'));

   58 |             }
