   61 |             }
   62 |             $embLocations = self::embeddingLocationCandidates($embLoc);
   63 | 
   64 |             return self::forwardEmbeddingWithFallback($vertexProjectId, $embLocations, $token, $input);
   65 |         }
   66 | 
   67 |         return self::err(400, 'Cuerpo no reconocido para Vertex');
   68 |     }
   69 | 
   70 |     /**
   71 |      * Resuelve consultas simples de disponibilidad/precios con Avirato sin llamar a Vertex.
   72 |      *
   73 |      * @param array<string, mixed> $input OpenAI chat completions shape
   74 |      * @return array{http_code:int, decoded:array, raw:string}|null
   75 |      */
   76 |     public static function tryAviratoLiteOpenAiResponse(array $input, array $aviratoConfig = []): ?array
   77 |     {
   78 |         if (!isset($input['messages']) || !is_array($input['messages'])) {
   79 |             return null;
   80 |         }
   81 |         $userText = self::latestUserMessageText($input['messages']);
   82 |         if ($userText === '' || !self::looksLikeAvailabilityIntent($userText)) {
   83 |             return null;
   84 |         }
   85 |         $params = self::extractAvailabilityParams($userText);
   86 |         $establishmentId = self::aviratoEstablishmentId($aviratoConfig);
   87 |         $roomFilter = self::aviratoRoomFilter($aviratoConfig);
   88 |         if ($establishmentId === '') {
   89 |             return self::aviratoLiteError(AviratoHandler::missingConfig(
   90 |                 $params['checkin'],
   91 |                 $params['checkout'],
   92 |                 $params['adults'],
   93 |                 $params['children'],
   94 |                 $roomFilter
   95 |             ));
   96 |         }
   97 |         $availability = AviratoHandler::getAvailability(
   98 |             $establishmentId,
   99 |             $params['checkin'],
  100 |             $params['checkout'],
  101 |             $params['adults'],
  102 |             $params['children'],
  103 |             $roomFilter
  104 |         );
  105 |         if (!AviratoHandler::canAnswerAvailability($availability)) {
  106 |             return null;
  107 |         }
  108 |         $text = AviratoHandler::formatAvailabilityAnswer($availability);
  109 |         $decoded = [
  110 |             'id'            => 'avirato-lite-' . bin2hex(random_bytes(6)),
  111 |             'object'        => 'chat.completion',
  112 |             'model'         => 'xabia-ia-lite-avirato',
  113 |             'choices'       => [
  114 |                 [
  115 |                     'index'         => 0,
  116 |                     'message'       => [
  117 |                         'role'    => 'assistant',
  118 |                         'content' => $text,
  119 |                     ],
  120 |                     'finish_reason' => 'stop',
  121 |                 ],
  122 |             ],
  123 |             'usage'         => [
  124 |                 'prompt_tokens'     => 0,
  125 |                 'completion_tokens' => 0,
  126 |                 'total_tokens'      => 0,
  127 |             ],
  128 |             'xabia_ia_lite' => true,
  129 |             'avirato'       => [
  130 |                 'source' => $availability['source'] ?? 'avirato',
  131 |                 'status' => $availability['status'] ?? 'success',
  132 |             ],
  133 |         ];
  134 | 
  135 |         return ['http_code' => 200, 'decoded' => $decoded, 'raw' => json_encode($decoded, JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE) ?: '{}'];

  156 |     private static function aviratoEstablishmentId(array $config): string

  161 |                 if ($value !== '') {
  162 |                     return substr($value, 0, 80);

  170 |     private static function aviratoRoomFilter(array $config): string

  175 |                 if ($value !== '') {
  176 |                     return substr($value, 0, 120);
  177 |                 }
  178 |             }
  179 |         }

  184 |     /**
  185 |      * @param array<string, mixed> $input OpenAI chat completions shape
  186 |      * @return array{http_code:int, decoded:?array, raw:string}
  187 |      */
  188 |     private static function forwardChatWithFallback(string $projectId, array $locations, string $token, array $models, array $input): array
  189 |     {
  190 |         $last = self::err(503, 'No se pudo resolver modelo/región Vertex');
  191 |         foreach ($locations as $loc) {
  192 |             foreach ($models as $model) {
  193 |                 $r = self::forwardChat($projectId, (string) $loc, $token, (string) $model, $input);
  194 |                 if ($r['http_code'] >= 200 && $r['http_code'] < 300) {
  195 |                     return $r;
  196 |                 }
  197 |                 $last = $r;
  198 |                 if (!self::shouldRetryVertexFailure($r)) {
  199 |                     return $r;
  200 |                 }
  201 |             }
  202 |         }
  203 | 
  204 |         return $last;
  205 |     }
  206 | 
  207 |     /**
  208 |      * @param array<string, mixed> $input OpenAI embeddings shape
  209 |      * @return array{http_code:int, decoded:?array, raw:string}
  210 |      */
  211 |     private static function forwardEmbeddingWithFallback(string $projectId, array $locations, string $token, array $input): array
  212 |     {
  213 |         $last = self::err(503, 'No se pudo resolver endpoint de embeddings en Vertex');
  214 |         foreach ($locations as $loc) {
  215 |             $r = self::forwardEmbedding($projectId, (string) $loc, $token, $input);
  216 |             if ($r['http_code'] >= 200 && $r['http_code'] < 300) {
  217 |                 return $r;
  218 |             }
  219 |             $last = $r;
  220 |             if (!self::shouldRetryVertexFailure($r)) {
  221 |                 return $r;
  222 |             }
  223 |         }
  224 | 
  225 |         return $last;
  226 |     }
  227 | 
  228 |     /**
  229 |      * @param array<string, mixed> $input OpenAI chat completions shape
  230 |      * @return array{http_code:int, decoded:?array, raw:string}
  231 |      */
  232 |     private static function forwardChat(string $projectId, string $location, string $token, string $geminiModel, array $input): array
  233 |     {
  234 |         $input = self::injectAviratoAvailabilityIfNeeded($input);
  235 |         $body = self::openAiMessagesToGeminiGenerateContent($input);
  236 |         if (($body['contents'] ?? []) === []) {
  237 |             return self::err(400, 'Sin mensajes user/assistant válidos para Gemini');
  238 |         }
  239 |         $url = sprintf(
  240 |             'https://%s-aiplatform.googleapis.com/v1/projects/%s/locations/%s/publishers/google/models/%s:generateContent',
  241 |             $location,
  242 |             rawurlencode($projectId),
  243 |             rawurlencode($location),
  244 |             rawurlencode($geminiModel)
  245 |         );
  246 |         $json = json_encode($body, JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE);
  247 |         if ($json === false) {
  248 |             return self::err(400, 'No se pudo serializar la petición Gemini');
  249 |         }
  250 |         $raw = self::curlJson($url, $token, $json);
  251 |         $gem = json_decode($raw['body'], true);
  252 |         if (!is_array($gem)) {
  253 |             return ['http_code' => $raw['code'] > 0 ? $raw['code'] : 502, 'decoded' => null, 'raw' => $raw['body']];
  254 |         }
  255 |         if ($raw['code'] < 200 || $raw['code'] >= 300) {
  256 |             return [
  257 |                 'http_code' => $raw['code'],
  258 |                 'decoded'   => self::geminiErrorToOpenAiShape($gem),
  259 |                 'raw'       => $raw['body'],
  260 |             ];
  261 |         }
  262 |         $openAi = self::geminiGenerateContentToOpenAiChat($gem, (string) ($input['model'] ?? $geminiModel));
  263 | 
  264 |         return ['http_code' => 200, 'decoded' => $openAi, 'raw' => json_encode($openAi, JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE)];
  265 |     }

  267 |     /**
  268 |      * @param array<string, mixed> $input OpenAI embeddings shape
  269 |      * @return array{http_code:int, decoded:?array, raw:string}
  270 |      */
  271 |     private static function forwardEmbedding(string $projectId, string $location, string $token, array $input): array
  272 |     {
  273 |         $instances = self::openAiInputToEmbeddingInstances($input['input'] ?? '');
  274 |         if ($instances === []) {
  275 |             return self::err(400, 'input vacío para embeddings');
  276 |         }
  277 |         $vertexBody = ['instances' => $instances];
  278 |         $m = self::EMBEDDING_MODEL;
  279 |         $url = sprintf(
  280 |             'https://%s-aiplatform.googleapis.com/v1/projects/%s/locations/%s/publishers/google/models/%s:predict',
  281 |             $location,
  282 |             rawurlencode($projectId),
  283 |             rawurlencode($location),
  284 |             rawurlencode($m)
  285 |         );
  286 |         $json = json_encode($vertexBody, JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE);
  287 |         if ($json === false) {
  288 |             return self::err(400, 'JSON predict inválido');
  289 |         }
  290 |         $raw = self::curlJson($url, $token, $json);
  291 |         $pred = json_decode($raw['body'], true);
  292 |         if (!is_array($pred)) {
  293 |             return ['http_code' => $raw['code'] > 0 ? $raw['code'] : 502, 'decoded' => null, 'raw' => $raw['body']];
  294 |         }
  295 |         if ($raw['code'] < 200 || $raw['code'] >= 300) {
  296 |             return [
  297 |                 'http_code' => $raw['code'],
  298 |                 'decoded'   => self::vertexPredictErrorToOpenAi($pred),
  299 |                 'raw'       => $raw['body'],
  300 |             ];
  301 |         }
  302 |         $usageTokens = self::extractEmbeddingBillableTokens($pred, $instances);
  303 |         $openAi = self::vertexPredictToOpenAiEmbeddings($pred, $input, $usageTokens);
  304 | 
  305 |         return ['http_code' => 200, 'decoded' => $openAi, 'raw' => json_encode($openAi, JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE)];
  306 |     }

  308 |     /**
  309 |      * @return array{code:int, body:string}
  310 |      */
  311 |     private static function curlJson(string $url, string $token, string $json): array
  312 |     {
  313 |         $ch = curl_init($url);
  314 |         curl_setopt_array($ch, [
  315 |             CURLOPT_POST           => true,
  316 |             CURLOPT_HTTPHEADER     => [
  317 |                 'Content-Type: application/json',
  318 |                 'Authorization: Bearer ' . $token,
  319 |             ],
  320 |             CURLOPT_POSTFIELDS     => $json,
  321 |             CURLOPT_RETURNTRANSFER => true,
  322 |             CURLOPT_TIMEOUT        => 180,
  323 |         ]);
  324 |         $body = curl_exec($ch);
  325 |         $code = (int) curl_getinfo($ch, CURLINFO_HTTP_CODE);
  326 |         curl_close($ch);
  327 |         if ($body === false) {
  328 |             return ['code' => 502, 'body' => '{"error":{"message":"curl failure","type":"xabia_vertex"}}'];
  329 |         }
  330 | 
  331 |         return ['code' => $code > 0 ? $code : 502, 'body' => $body];
  332 |     }

  334 |     /**
  335 |      * @param array<string, mixed> $openAi
  336 |      * @return array<string, mixed>
  337 |      */
  338 |     private static function openAiMessagesToGeminiGenerateContent(array $openAi): array
  339 |     {
  340 |         $messages = $openAi['messages'] ?? [];
  341 |         if (!is_array($messages)) {
  342 |             $messages = [];
  343 |         }
  344 |         $systemParts = [];
  345 |         $langSnippet = self::userLangGeminiSystemSnippet($openAi['user_lang'] ?? null);
  346 |         if ($langSnippet !== '') {
  347 |             $systemParts[] = ['text' => $langSnippet];
  348 |         }
  349 |         $contents = [];
  350 |         /** @var array<string, string> */
  351 |         $toolCallIdToName = [];
  352 |         foreach ($messages as $m) {
  353 |             if (!is_array($m)) {
  354 |                 continue;
  355 |             }
  356 |             $role = (string) ($m['role'] ?? 'user');
  357 |             if ($role === 'system') {
  358 |                 $text = self::openAiContentToPlainText($m['content'] ?? '');
  359 |                 if ($text !== '') {
  360 |                     $systemParts[] = ['text' => $text];
  361 |                 }
  362 | 
  363 |                 continue;
  364 |             }
  365 |             if ($role === 'assistant') {
  366 |                 $parts = [];
  367 |                 $text = self::openAiContentToPlainText($m['content'] ?? '');
  368 |                 if ($text !== '') {
  369 |                     $parts[] = ['text' => $text];
  370 |                 }
  371 |                 $tcs = $m['tool_calls'] ?? null;
  372 |                 if (is_array($tcs)) {
  373 |                     foreach ($tcs as $tc) {
  374 |                         if (!is_array($tc)) {
  375 |                             continue;
  376 |                         }
  377 |                         $fn = $tc['function'] ?? null;
  378 |                         if (!is_array($fn)) {
  379 |                             continue;
  380 |                         }
  381 |                         $fname = (string) ($fn['name'] ?? '');
  382 |                         $argsRaw = $fn['arguments'] ?? '{}';
  383 |                         $args = is_string($argsRaw) ? json_decode($argsRaw, true) : $argsRaw;
  384 |                         if (!is_array($args)) {
  385 |                             $args = [];
  386 |                         }
  387 |                         $tid = (string) ($tc['id'] ?? '');
  388 |                         if ($tid !== '' && $fname !== '') {
  389 |                             $toolCallIdToName[$tid] = $fname;
  390 |                         }
  391 |                         if ($fname !== '') {
  392 |                             $parts[] = [
  393 |                                 'functionCall' => [
  394 |                                     'name' => $fname,
  395 |                                     'args' => $args,
  396 |                                 ],
  397 |                             ];
  398 |                         }
  399 |                     }
  400 |                 }
  401 |                 if ($parts !== []) {
  402 |                     $contents[] = ['role' => 'model', 'parts' => $parts];
  403 |                 }
  404 | 
  405 |                 continue;
  406 |             }
  407 |             if ($role === 'tool') {
  408 |                 $tid = (string) ($m['tool_call_id'] ?? '');
  409 |                 $fname = $toolCallIdToName[$tid] ?? (string) ($m['name'] ?? '');
  410 |                 if ($fname === '') {
  411 |                     continue;
  412 |                 }
  413 |                 $toolText = self::openAiContentToPlainText($m['content'] ?? '');
  414 |                 $contents[] = [
  415 |                     'role'  => 'user',
  416 |                     'parts' => [
  417 |                         [
  418 |                             'functionResponse' => [
  419 |                                 'name'     => $fname,
  420 |                                 'response' => ['result' => $toolText],
  421 |                             ],
  422 |                         ],
  423 |                     ],
  424 |                 ];
  425 | 
  426 |                 continue;
  427 |             }
  428 |             $text = self::openAiContentToPlainText($m['content'] ?? '');
  429 |             if ($text === '') {
  430 |                 continue;
  431 |             }
  432 |             $gRole = 'user';
  433 |             $contents[] = [
  434 |                 'role'  => $gRole,
  435 |                 'parts' => [['text' => $text]],
  436 |             ];
  437 |         }
  438 |         $out = ['contents' => $contents];
  439 |         if ($systemParts !== []) {
  440 |             $out['systemInstruction'] = ['parts' => $systemParts];
  441 |         }
  442 |         $temp = isset($openAi['temperature']) ? (float) $openAi['temperature'] : 0.2;
  443 |         $maxOut = isset($openAi['max_tokens']) ? (int) $openAi['max_tokens'] : (isset($openAi['max_completion_tokens']) ? (int) $openAi['max_completion_tokens'] : 1024);
  444 |         if ($maxOut < 1) {
  445 |             $maxOut = 1024;
  446 |         }
  447 |         if ($maxOut > 8192) {
  448 |             $maxOut = 8192;
  449 |         }
  450 |         $out['generationConfig'] = [
  451 |             'temperature'     => max(0.0, min(2.0, $temp)),
  452 |             'maxOutputTokens' => $maxOut,
  453 |             'candidateCount'  => 1,
  454 |         ];
  455 | 
  456 |         $decls = self::openAiToolsToVertexFunctionDeclarations($openAi['tools'] ?? null);
  457 |         if ($decls !== []) {
  458 |             $out['tools'] = [['functionDeclarations' => $decls]];
  459 |             $out['toolConfig'] = [
  460 |                 'functionCallingConfig' => [
  461 |                     'mode' => 'AUTO',
  462 |                 ],
  463 |             ];
  464 |         }
  465 | 
  466 |         return $out;
  467 |     }

  474 |     private static function openAiToolsToVertexFunctionDeclarations(mixed $tools): array

  496 |             $params = $fn['parameters'] ?? null;

  498 |                 $params = ['type' => 'object', 'properties' => new \stdClass()];

  702 |     /**
  703 |      * @return list<array{content:string}>
  704 |      */
  705 |     private static function openAiInputToEmbeddingInstances($input): array
  706 |     {
  707 |         if (is_string($input)) {
  708 |             $t = trim($input);
  709 | 
  710 |             return $t === '' ? [] : [['content' => $t]];
  711 |         }
  712 |         if (!is_array($input)) {
  713 |             return [];
  714 |         }
  715 |         $out = [];
  716 |         foreach ($input as $line) {
  717 |             if (is_string($line)) {
  718 |                 $line = trim($line);
  719 |                 if ($line !== '') {
  720 |                     $out[] = ['content' => $line];
  721 |                 }
  722 |             }
  723 |         }
  724 | 
  725 |         return $out;
  726 |     }
  727 | 
  728 |     /**
  729 |      * Campos de Vertex que cuentan TOKENS (saldo Xabia = unidades tipo LLM).
  730 |      *
  731 |      * @param array<string, mixed> $bag
  732 |      */
  733 |     private static function readEmbeddingTokenFieldsFromBag(array $bag): int
  734 |     {
  735 |         foreach (['totalTokenCount', 'promptTokenCount', 'token_count'] as $k) {
  736 |             if (isset($bag[$k]) && is_numeric($bag[$k])) {
  737 |                 $n = (int) $bag[$k];
  738 | 
  739 |                 return $n > 0 ? $n : 0;
  740 |             }
  741 |         }
  742 | 
  743 |         return 0;
  744 |     }
  745 | 
  746 |     /**
  747 |      * Campos de Vertex en CARACTERES facturables: no son tokens; aproximación conservadora ÷4.
  748 |      *
  749 |      * @param array<string, mixed> $bag
  750 |      */
  751 |     private static function readEmbeddingCharsAsApproxTokensFromBag(array $bag): int
  752 |     {
  753 |         foreach (['billableCharacterCount', 'totalBillableCharacters', 'billable_character_count'] as $k) {
  754 |             if (isset($bag[$k]) && is_numeric($bag[$k])) {
  755 |                 $chars = max(0, (int) $bag[$k]);
  756 | 
  757 |                 return max(1, (int) ceil($chars / 4));
  758 |             }
  759 |         }
  760 | 
  761 |         return 0;
  762 |     }
  763 | 
  764 |     /**
  765 |      * @param list<array{content:string}> $instances
  766 |      */
  767 |     private static function extractEmbeddingBillableTokens(array $pred, array $instances): int
  768 |     {
  769 |         $meta = $pred['metadata'] ?? null;
  770 |         if (is_array($meta)) {
  771 |             $fromTok = self::readEmbeddingTokenFieldsFromBag($meta);
  772 |             if ($fromTok > 0) {
  773 |                 return $fromTok;
  774 |             }
  775 |             $fromChars = self::readEmbeddingCharsAsApproxTokensFromBag($meta);
  776 |             if ($fromChars > 0) {
  777 |                 return $fromChars;
  778 |             }
  779 |         }
  780 |         $sum = 0;
  781 |         foreach ($pred['predictions'] ?? [] as $p) {
  782 |             if (!is_array($p)) {
  783 |                 continue;
  784 |             }
  785 |             $fromTok = self::readEmbeddingTokenFieldsFromBag($p);
  786 |             if ($fromTok > 0) {
  787 |                 $sum += $fromTok;
  788 | 
  789 |                 continue;
  790 |             }
  791 |             $fromChars = self::readEmbeddingCharsAsApproxTokensFromBag($p);
  792 |             if ($fromChars > 0) {
  793 |                 $sum += $fromChars;
  794 | 
  795 |                 continue;
  796 |             }
  797 |             $emb = $p['embeddings'] ?? null;
  798 |             if (is_array($emb) && isset($emb['statistics']['token_count']) && is_numeric($emb['statistics']['token_count'])) {
  799 |                 $sum += max(1, (int) $emb['statistics']['token_count']);
  800 |             }
  801 |         }
  802 |         if ($sum > 0) {
  803 |             return $sum;
  804 |         }
  805 |         $len = 0;
  806 |         foreach ($instances as $ins) {
  807 |             $len += strlen($ins['content'] ?? '');
  808 |         }
  809 | 
  810 |         return max(1, (int) ceil($len / 4));
  811 |     }
  812 | 
  813 |     /**
  814 |      * @param array<string, mixed> $pred
  815 |      * @param array<string, mixed> $inputOriginal
  816 |      * @return array<string, mixed>
  817 |      */
  818 |     private static function vertexPredictToOpenAiEmbeddings(array $pred, array $inputOriginal, int $usageTokens): array
  819 |     {
  820 |         $data = [];
  821 |         $predictions = $pred['predictions'] ?? [];
  822 |         if (is_array($predictions)) {
  823 |             foreach ($predictions as $idx => $p) {
  824 |                 if (!is_array($p)) {
  825 |                     continue;
  826 |                 }
  827 |                 $vals = null;
  828 |                 if (isset($p['embeddings']['values']) && is_array($p['embeddings']['values'])) {
  829 |                     $vals = $p['embeddings']['values'];
  830 |                 } elseif (isset($p['embeddings']) && is_array($p['embeddings']) && isset($p['embeddings'][0])) {
  831 |                     $vals = $p['embeddings'];
  832 |                 }
  833 |                 if ($vals !== null) {
  834 |                     $data[] = ['object' => 'embedding', 'embedding' => array_map('floatval', $vals), 'index' => $idx];
  835 |                 }
  836 |             }
  837 |         }
  838 | 
  839 |         return [
  840 |             'object' => 'list',
  841 |             'model'  => (string) ($inputOriginal['model'] ?? self::EMBEDDING_MODEL),
  842 |             'data'   => $data,
  843 |             'usage'  => [
  844 |                 'prompt_tokens'     => $usageTokens,
  845 |                 'completion_tokens' => 0,
  846 |                 'total_tokens'      => $usageTokens,
  847 |             ],
  848 |         ];
  849 |     }

  851 |     /**
  852 |      * @param array<string, mixed> $pred
  853 |      * @return array<string, mixed>
  854 |      */
  855 |     private static function vertexPredictErrorToOpenAi(array $pred): array
  856 |     {
  857 |         $msg = 'Embedding Vertex error';
  858 |         if (isset($pred['error']['message'])) {
  859 |             $msg = (string) $pred['error']['message'];
  860 |         }
  861 | 
  862 |         return ['error' => ['message' => $msg, 'type' => 'vertex_embedding']];
  863 |     }

  865 |     /**
  866 |      * @return list<string>
  867 |      */
  868 |     private static function locationCandidates(string $primary): array
  869 |     {
  870 |         $primary = trim($primary);
  871 |         $candidates = [$primary];
  872 |         // Segunda región por defecto (compatibilidad). En .env: XABIA_VERTEX_LOCATION_FALLBACK=none para no probar USA.
  873 |         $fallback = Env::str('XABIA_VERTEX_LOCATION_FALLBACK', 'us-central1');
  874 |         if (strcasecmp($fallback, 'none') === 0) {
  875 |             $fallback = '';
  876 |         }
  877 |         if ($fallback !== '' && $fallback !== $primary) {
  878 |             $candidates[] = $fallback;
  879 |         }
  880 | 
  881 |         return array_values(array_unique(array_filter($candidates, static fn ($v) => is_string($v) && $v !== '')));
  882 |     }
  883 | 
  884 |     /**
  885 |      * Regiones para embeddings: por defecto solo la primaria (sin reintento automático en USA).
  886 |      * Opcional: XABIA_VERTEX_EMBEDDING_LOCATION_FALLBACK (p. ej. otra región UE) o none.
  887 |      *
  888 |      * @return list<string>
  889 |      */
  890 |     private static function embeddingLocationCandidates(string $primary): array
  891 |     {
  892 |         $primary = trim($primary);
  893 |         if ($primary === '') {
  894 |             $primary = 'europe-west1';
  895 |         }
  896 |         $candidates = [$primary];
  897 |         $fallback = trim(Env::str('XABIA_VERTEX_EMBEDDING_LOCATION_FALLBACK'));
  898 |         if ($fallback === '' || strcasecmp($fallback, 'none') === 0) {
  899 |             return array_values(array_unique(array_filter($candidates, static fn ($v) => is_string($v) && $v !== '')));
  900 |         }
  901 |         if ($fallback !== $primary) {
  902 |             $candidates[] = $fallback;
  903 |         }
  904 | 
  905 |         return array_values(array_unique(array_filter($candidates, static fn ($v) => is_string($v) && $v !== '')));
  906 |     }
  907 | 
  908 |     /**
  909 |      * @return list<string>
  910 |      */
  911 |     private static function geminiModelCandidates(string $primary, bool $usePro): array
  912 |     {

  916 |             $models[] = $primary;
  917 |             // Alias legado 1.5 → catálogo Vertex actual (Aktiba / 2026).
  918 |             if (preg_match('/^gemini-1\.5-flash(-001|-002)?$/i', $primary) === 1) {
  919 |                 $models[] = 'gemini-2.5-flash';

  963 |     /**
  964 |      * @param array{http_code:int, decoded:?array, raw:string} $res
  965 |      */
  966 |     private static function shouldRetryVertexFailure(array $res): bool
  967 |     {
  968 |         if (($res['http_code'] ?? 0) < 400) {
  969 |             return false;
  970 |         }
  971 |         $msg = '';
  972 |         if (is_array($res['decoded']) && isset($res['decoded']['error']['message']) && is_string($res['decoded']['error']['message'])) {
  973 |             $msg = strtolower($res['decoded']['error']['message']);
  974 |         }
  975 | 
  976 |         return str_contains($msg, 'not found')
  977 |             || str_contains($msg, 'does not have access')
  978 |             || str_contains($msg, 'permission')
  979 |             || str_contains($msg, 'publisher model');
  980 |     }

  982 |     /**
  983 |      * Si el usuario pregunta por disponibilidad/fechas, consultamos Avirato antes de enviar a Gemini.
  984 |      *
  985 |      * @param array<string, mixed> $input
  986 |      * @return array<string, mixed>
  987 |      */
  988 |     private static function injectAviratoAvailabilityIfNeeded(array $input): array
  989 |     {
  990 |         $userText = self::latestUserMessageText($input['messages'] ?? null);
  991 |         if ($userText === '' || !self::looksLikeAvailabilityIntent($userText)) {
  992 |             return $input;
  993 |         }
  994 |         $params = self::extractAvailabilityParams($userText);
  995 |         $aviratoConfig = isset($input['avirato']) && is_array($input['avirato']) ? $input['avirato'] : [];
  996 |         $establishmentId = self::aviratoEstablishmentId($aviratoConfig);
  997 |         if ($establishmentId === '') {
  998 |             return $input;
  999 |         }
 1000 |         $roomFilterKeyword = self::aviratoRoomFilter($aviratoConfig);
 1001 |         $availability = AviratoHandler::getAvailability(
 1002 |             $establishmentId,
 1003 |             $params['checkin'],
 1004 |             $params['checkout'],
 1005 |             $params['adults'],
 1006 |             $params['children'],
 1007 |             $roomFilterKeyword
 1008 |         );
 1009 |         $note = 'DATOS DE DISPONIBILIDAD (Avirato): ' . json_encode($availability, JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE)
 1010 |             . ' Usa estos datos antes de responder. No inventes disponibilidad ni precios.';
 1011 | 
 1012 |         $messages = $input['messages'] ?? [];
 1013 |         if (!is_array($messages)) {
 1014 |             $messages = [];
 1015 |         }
 1016 |         array_unshift($messages, ['role' => 'system', 'content' => $note]);
 1017 |         $input['messages'] = $messages;
 1018 | 
 1019 |         return $input;
 1020 |     }
 1021 | 
 1022 |     /**
 1023 |      * @param mixed $messages
 1024 |      */
 1025 |     private static function latestUserMessageText(mixed $messages): string
 1026 |     {
 1027 |         if (!is_array($messages)) {
 1028 |             return '';
 1029 |         }
 1030 |         for ($i = count($messages) - 1; $i >= 0; $i--) {
 1031 |             $m = $messages[$i] ?? null;
 1032 |             if (!is_array($m) || (string) ($m['role'] ?? '') !== 'user') {
 1033 |                 continue;
 1034 |             }
 1035 |             return trim(self::openAiContentToPlainText($m['content'] ?? ''));
 1036 |         }
 1037 | 
 1038 |         return '';
 1039 |     }
 1040 | 
 1041 |     private static function looksLikeAvailabilityIntent(string $text): bool
 1042 |     {
 1043 |         $t = function_exists('mb_strtolower') ? mb_strtolower($text, 'UTF-8') : strtolower($text);
 1044 |         foreach (['disponibilidad', 'disponible', 'fecha', 'fechas', 'reserva', 'reservar', 'habitacion', 'dormir', 'booking'] as $kw) {
 1045 |             if (str_contains($t, $kw)) {
 1046 |                 return true;
 1047 |             }
 1048 |         }
 1049 |         if (preg_match('/\b\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}\b/', $text) === 1) {
 1050 |             return true;
 1051 |         }
 1052 |         if (preg_match('/\b\d{1,2}\/\d{1,2}\/\d{4}\b/', $text) === 1) {
 1053 |             return true;
 1054 |         }
 1055 | 
 1056 |         return false;
 1057 |     }
 1058 | 
 1059 |     /**
 1060 |      * @return array{checkin:string, checkout:string, adults:int, children:int}
 1061 |      */
 1062 |     private static function extractAvailabilityParams(string $text): array
 1063 |     {
 1064 |         $dates = [];
 1065 |         if (preg_match_all('/\b(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})\b/', $text, $m, PREG_SET_ORDER)) {
 1066 |             foreach ($m as $hit) {
 1067 |                 $dates[] = sprintf('%04d-%02d-%02d', (int) $hit[1], (int) $hit[2], (int) $hit[3]);
 1068 |             }
 1069 |         }
 1070 |         if (count($dates) < 2 && preg_match_all('/\b(\d{1,2})\/(\d{1,2})\/(\d{4})\b/', $text, $m2, PREG_SET_ORDER)) {
 1071 |             foreach ($m2 as $hit) {
 1072 |                 $dates[] = sprintf('%04d-%02d-%02d', (int) $hit[3], (int) $hit[2], (int) $hit[1]);
 1073 |             }
 1074 |         }
 1075 |         $today = new \DateTimeImmutable('now');
 1076 |         $checkin = $dates[0] ?? $today->modify('+1 day')->format('Y-m-d');
 1077 |         $checkout = $dates[1] ?? (new \DateTimeImmutable($checkin))->modify('+1 day')->format('Y-m-d');
 1078 | 
 1079 |         $adults = 2;
 1080 |         if (preg_match('/(\d+)\s*(adult|adulto|adultos|persona|personas)/iu', $text, $am) === 1) {
 1081 |             $adults = max(1, (int) $am[1]);
 1082 |         }
 1083 |         $children = 0;
 1084 |         if (preg_match('/(\d+)\s*(niñ|nino|child|children)/iu', $text, $cm) === 1) {
 1085 |             $children = max(0, (int) $cm[1]);
 1086 |         }
 1087 | 
 1088 |         return [
 1089 |             'checkin'  => $checkin,
 1090 |             'checkout' => $checkout,
 1091 |             'adults'   => $adults,
 1092 |             'children' => $children,
 1093 |         ];
 1094 |     }
 1095 | 
 1096 |     /**
 1097 |      * @return array{http_code:int, decoded:array, raw:string}
 1098 |      */
 1099 |     private static function err(int $code, string $message): array
 1100 |     {
 1101 |         $d = ['error' => ['message' => $message, 'type' => 'xabia_vertex_config']];
 1102 | 
 1103 |         return ['http_code' => $code, 'decoded' => $d, 'raw' => json_encode($d)];
 1104 |     }

    5 | namespace XabiaCentral;
    6 | 
    7 | /**
    8 |  * Reenvío a la API oficial OpenAI con la master key del hub.
    9 |  *
   10 |  * @return array{http_code:int, decoded:?array, raw:string}
   11 |  */
   12 | final class OpenAiForwarder
   13 | {
   14 |     private const OPENAI_BASE = 'https://api.openai.com';
   15 | 
   16 |     /**
   17 |      * @param array<string, mixed> $payload
   18 |      * @return array{http_code:int, decoded:?array, raw:string}
   19 |      */
   20 |     public static function forward(string $path, array $payload): array
   21 |     {
   22 |         $apiKey = Env::str('OPENAI_API_KEY');
   23 |         if ($apiKey === '') {
   24 |             return [
   25 |                 'http_code' => 500,
   26 |                 'decoded'   => ['error' => ['message' => 'OPENAI_API_KEY no configurada en el hub', 'type' => 'xabia_hub_config']],
   27 |                 'raw'       => '',
   28 |             ];
   29 |         }
   30 |         $url = self::OPENAI_BASE . $path;
   31 |         $json = json_encode($payload, JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE);
   32 |         if ($json === false) {
   33 |             return [
   34 |                 'http_code' => 400,
   35 |                 'decoded'   => ['error' => ['message' => 'JSON inválido', 'type' => 'invalid_request']],
   36 |                 'raw'       => '',
   37 |             ];
   38 |         }
   39 |         $ch = curl_init($url);
   40 |         curl_setopt_array($ch, [
   41 |             CURLOPT_POST           => true,
   42 |             CURLOPT_HTTPHEADER     => [
   43 |                 'Content-Type: application/json',
   44 |                 'Authorization: Bearer ' . $apiKey,
   45 |             ],
   46 |             CURLOPT_POSTFIELDS     => $json,
   47 |             CURLOPT_RETURNTRANSFER => true,
   48 |             CURLOPT_TIMEOUT        => 120,
   49 |         ]);
   50 |         $raw = curl_exec($ch);
   51 |         $code = (int) curl_getinfo($ch, CURLINFO_HTTP_CODE);
   52 |         if ($raw === false) {
   53 |             $err = curl_error($ch);
   54 |             curl_close($ch);
   55 | 
   56 |             return [
   57 |                 'http_code' => 502,
   58 |                 'decoded'   => ['error' => ['message' => $err ?: 'curl error', 'type' => 'xabia_hub_upstream']],
   59 |                 'raw'       => '',
   60 |             ];
   61 |         }
   62 |         curl_close($ch);
   63 |         $decoded = json_decode($raw, true);
   64 | 
   65 |         return [
   66 |             'http_code' => $code > 0 ? $code : 502,
   67 |             'decoded'   => is_array($decoded) ? $decoded : null,
   68 |             'raw'       => $raw,
   69 |         ];
   70 |     }
   71 | 
   72 |     /**
   73 |      * Decide ruta OpenAI según cuerpo (espejo del contrato del plugin).
   74 |      *
   75 |      * @param array<string, mixed> $payload
   76 |      * @return array{path:string,payload:array<string,mixed>}|null
   77 |      */
   78 |     public static function resolveOpenAiRoute(array $payload): ?array
   79 |     {
   80 |         if (isset($payload['messages']) && is_array($payload['messages'])) {
   81 |             return ['path' => '/v1/chat/completions', 'payload' => $payload];
   82 |         }
   83 |         if (isset($payload['input']) && isset($payload['model'])) {
   84 |             return ['path' => '/v1/embeddings', 'payload' => $payload];
   85 |         }
   86 | 
   87 |         return null;
   88 |     }
   89 | }

  220 |         if (!is_array($decoded)) {
  221 |             return [];
  222 |         }
  223 | 
  224 |         return array_values(array_filter($decoded, static fn ($room) => is_array($room)));
  225 |     }
  226 | 
  227 |     /**
  228 |      * @param list<array<string, mixed>> $rooms
  229 |      * @return list<array<string, mixed>>
  230 |      */
  231 |     private static function filterRooms(array $rooms, string $filterKeyword): array
  232 |     {
  233 |         $normalizedFilter = self::normalizeText($filterKeyword);
  234 | 
  235 |         $filtered = [];
  236 |         foreach ($rooms as $room) {
  237 |             $subtipo = (string) ($room['subtipoEspacio'] ?? '');
  238 |             $name = (string) ($room['nombreEspacio'] ?? ($room['name'] ?? ($room['nombre'] ?? '')));
  239 |             $haystack = self::normalizeText($subtipo . ' ' . $name);
  240 |             if ($haystack === '') {
  241 |                 continue;
  242 |             }
  243 |             if (self::containsExcludedTerm($haystack)) {
  244 |                 continue;
  245 |             }
  246 |             if ($normalizedFilter !== '' && !str_contains(self::normalizeText($subtipo), $normalizedFilter)) {
  247 |                 continue;
  248 |             }
  249 |             $filtered[] = $room;
  250 |         }
  251 | 
  252 |         return $filtered;
  253 |     }
  254 | 
  255 |     private static function containsExcludedTerm(string $haystack): bool
  256 |     {
  257 |         foreach (self::ALWAYS_EXCLUDED_TERMS as $term) {
  258 |             if (str_contains($haystack, self::normalizeText($term))) {
  259 |                 return true;
  260 |             }
  261 |         }
  262 | 
  263 |         return false;
  264 |     }
  265 | 
  266 |     private static function normalizeText(string $value): string
  267 |     {
  268 |         $value = trim($value);
  269 |         if ($value === '') {
  270 |             return '';
  271 |         }
  272 |         if (function_exists('mb_strtolower')) {
  273 |             return mb_strtolower($value, 'UTF-8');
  274 |         }
  275 | 
  276 |         return strtolower($value);
  277 |     }
  278 | 
  279 |     private static function toBookingDate(string $date): string
  280 |     {
  281 |         $dt = \DateTimeImmutable::createFromFormat('Y-m-d', $date);
  282 |         if ($dt === false) {
  283 |             return $date;
  284 |         }
  285 | 
  286 |         return $dt->format('d-m-Y');
  287 |     }
  288 | 
  289 |     /**
  290 |      * @return array<string, mixed>
  291 |      */
  292 |     private static function fallbackOrError(
  293 |         string $checkin,
  294 |         string $checkout,
  295 |         int $adults,
  296 |         int $children,
  297 |         string $filterKeyword,
  298 |         array $query,
  299 |         string $reason,
  300 |         int $code = 0
  301 |     ): array {
  302 |         if (self::shouldUseMockFallback()) {
  303 |             return self::mockAvailability($checkin, $checkout, $adults, $children, $filterKeyword, $reason);
  304 |         }
  305 | 
  306 |         return [
  307 |             'ok'      => false,
  308 |             'status'  => 'error',
  309 |             'source'  => 'avirato_scraping_error',
  310 |             'message' => $reason,
  311 |             'code'    => $code,
  312 |             'query'   => $query,
  313 |         ];
  314 |     }
  315 | 
  316 |     private static function shouldUseMockFallback(): bool
  317 |     {
  318 |         $env = self::normalizeText(Env::str('APP_ENV', Env::str('XABIA_ENV', Env::str('ENVIRONMENT', ''))));
  319 | 
  320 |         return in_array($env, ['dev', 'development', 'local'], true);
  321 |     }
  322 | }
  323 |
